**1\. Purpose**

E1 menetapkan **Performance Constitution** sebagai fondasi konstitusional bagi  
seluruh Performance Architecture PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

E1 mendefinisikan:

- performance philosophy;
- performance principles;
- performance architecture boundary;
- hubungan performance dengan Governance & Compliance;
- hubungan performance dengan organizational authority;
- hubungan performance dengan operating model;
- evidence dan traceability discipline;
- measurement → evaluation → insight → feedback pattern.

E1 menjadi **parent constitution** bagi E2–E10.

E1 tidak mendefinisikan:

- KPI domain secara spesifik;
- metric catalogue;
- target;
- threshold;
- review cadence domain;
- maturity score;
- operational procedure;
- governance authority baru.

Elemen-elemen tersebut harus ditetapkan pada section yang relevan berdasarkan  
authoritative source dan applicable authority.

⸻

**2\. Performance Philosophy**

PD-03 memandang performance bukan sekadar kumpulan KPI.

Performance merupakan kemampuan untuk mengetahui secara evidence-based:

1. apa yang hendak dicapai;
2. kondisi Governance & Compliance yang diharapkan;
3. kondisi aktual yang dapat dibuktikan;
4. seberapa besar gap antara expected dan actual condition;
5. apakah governance mechanism bekerja sebagaimana dimaksud;
6. apakah control, policy, certification, dan compliance-related capability  
   menghasilkan intended outcome;
7. apakah terdapat weakness atau degradation;
8. apakah improvement menghasilkan perubahan yang efektif;
9. apakah performance condition dapat dipertahankan.

Canonical performance cycle:

Objective / Criterion

&nbsp; ↓

Governance & Compliance Activity

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Evaluation

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Insight

&nbsp; ↓

Review

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Feedback

Performance bukan tujuan tersendiri.

Performance adalah **measurement and feedback architecture** yang membantu PD-03  
memahami kualitas dan efektivitas Governance & Compliance operation.

⸻

**3\. PD-03 Performance Context**

Performance Architecture PD-03 harus selalu dibaca dalam konteks:

PD-03

Governance & Compliance

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├── Policy

&nbsp; ├── Control

&nbsp; ├── Certification

&nbsp; ├── Governance Standards

&nbsp; └── Governance Baseline

PD-03 memiliki primary organizational domain **Governance & Compliance**.

Performance Architecture mengukur dan mengevaluasi performance domain tersebut.

Performance Architecture tidak memperluas domain PD-03 hanya karena suatu  
aktivitas memiliki governance implication.

⸻

Dengan demikian Performance Architecture menerima input dari:

Performance Architecture tidak menjadi sumber authority bagi Part A–D.

Governance Authority

&nbsp; ↓

**5\. Fundamental Performance Principle**

**Performance conclusions must be grounded in observable and traceable evidence, and performance findings must remain within the authority and ownership boundaries established by the architecture.**

Dengan demikian:

Objective

&nbsp; ↓

Criterion

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Evaluation

&nbsp; ↓

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Review

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Response

Tidak boleh ada lompatan langsung:

KPI Result

&nbsp; ↓

Authority Change

atau:

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Ownership Transfer

⸻

**6\. Performance Principles**

**P-01 — Performance Follows Approved Intent**

Performance harus dinilai terhadap objective, criterion, obligation, atau  
expected condition yang telah ditetapkan melalui authority yang berlaku.

Performance Management tidak menetapkan strategic atau governance intent  
secara unilateral.

⸻

**P-02 — Performance Must Be Evidence-Based**

Setiap material performance conclusion harus memiliki evidence yang memadai.

Evidence harus dapat ditelusuri ke sumber yang digunakan untuk menghasilkan  
measurement atau assessment.

⸻

**P-03 — Performance Must Be Traceable**

Minimum canonical chain:

Performance ID

&nbsp; ↓

Objective / Criterion

&nbsp; ↓

Metric / Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Result

&nbsp; ↓

Evaluation

&nbsp; ↓

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Review / Action

Historical evidence tidak boleh diubah hanya untuk memperbaiki performance  
appearance.

⸻

**P-04 — Performance Is Multidimensional**

Performance PD-03 tidak boleh direduksi menjadi satu KPI atau satu score.

Performance harus dapat dilihat melalui dimensi yang relevan dengan Governance &  
Compliance context.

Detail performance dimensions ditetapkan oleh E2.

⸻

**P-05 — Measurement ≠ Evaluation**

E1 mempertahankan separation:

Measurement

"What happened?"

Evaluation

"What does it mean?"

Measurement menghasilkan evidence/result.

Evaluation menghasilkan interpretation/assessment.

⸻

**P-06 — Performance ≠ Governance Authority**

Performance Architecture dapat:

- measure;
- evaluate;
- assess;
- report;
- identify finding;
- generate improvement signal.

Performance Architecture tidak otomatis:

- create authority;
- override governance decision;
- change governance rule;
- transfer decision right.

⸻

**P-07 — Performance ≠ Operational Execution**

Performance Architecture mengevaluasi operation.

Performance Architecture tidak menjalankan operation yang dinilai.

PD-03 Governance & Compliance

&nbsp; ↓

Operating Model

&nbsp; ↓

Execution

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Architecture

Performance Management tidak mengambil alih operational execution.

⸻

**P-08 — Performance Does Not Transfer Accountability**

Performance failure tidak secara otomatis memindahkan accountability.

Root cause harus dapat dibedakan antara:

- objective failure;
- execution failure;
- measurement failure;
- governance failure;
- capability gap;
- external constraint.

Premature attribution tidak diperbolehkan.

⸻

**P-09 — Performance Finding ≠ Decision**

Performance finding merupakan assessment.

Decision tetap mengikuti authority yang berlaku.

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Review

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Action

⸻

**P-10 — Improvement Requires Verification**

Improvement tidak dianggap efektif hanya karena action telah selesai.

Canonical rule:

Action Completed

&nbsp; ≠

Improvement Effective

Effectiveness harus diverifikasi melalui re-measurement.

⸻

**P-11 — Performance Must Preserve Domain Ownership**

Performance Architecture tidak mengambil ownership atas domain yang diukur.

Untuk PD-03:

Governance & Compliance

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Domain

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Assessment

Performance assessment tidak mengubah ownership domain.

⸻

**P-12 — Performance Must Preserve Cross-Platform Autonomy**

PD-03 dapat mengukur governance/compliance relationship dengan Platform lain,  
tetapi tidak otomatis mengambil ownership atas execution Platform tersebut.

PD-03

Governance / Compliance

&nbsp; ↓

Cross-Platform Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Platform

&nbsp; ↓

Platform-Owned Execution

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Assessment

Dependency tidak berarti subordination.

⸻

**7\. Performance Architecture Layers**

Performance Architecture PD-03 memiliki lima conceptual layers.

**Layer 1 — Performance Intent**

Menentukan apa yang perlu dipahami berdasarkan approved objective,  
criterion, obligation, atau expected condition.

Output:

- Performance Objective;
- Expected Condition;
- Performance Criterion.

⸻

**Layer 2 — Performance Measurement**

Menghasilkan evidence dan measurement result.

Output:

- Measurement Result;
- Metric Result;
- KPI Result;
- Performance Evidence.

⸻

**Layer 3 — Performance Evaluation**

Membandingkan actual condition dengan expected condition.

Output:

- Performance Assessment;
- Gap Analysis;
- Trend;
- Evaluation Result.

⸻

**Layer 4 — Performance Intelligence**

Mengubah hasil evaluation menjadi insight.

Output:

- Performance Insight;
- Performance Finding;
- Risk/Weakness Signal;
- Improvement Signal.

⸻

**Layer 5 — Performance Feedback**

Mengembalikan insight kepada review dan improvement mechanism.

Output:

- Review Input;
- Improvement Signal;
- Follow-up Requirement;
- Re-measurement Requirement.

Feedback tidak berarti automatic intervention.

⸻

**8\. PD-03 Performance Scope**

**In Scope**

Performance Architecture PD-03 mencakup pengukuran dan evaluasi terhadap  
performance yang berhubungan dengan:

- Governance & Compliance;
- Governance Policy;
- Governance Control;
- Certification;
- Governance Standards;
- Governance Baseline;
- governance operating effectiveness;
- governance decision support;
- compliance-related performance;
- governance evidence;
- improvement effectiveness;
- performance maturity;
- performance success.

**Out of Scope**

E1 tidak memberikan ownership kepada Performance Architecture atas:

- enterprise strategy;
- technical architecture;
- technical design;
- operational execution Platform lain;
- security execution;
- quality execution;
- infrastructure execution;
- AI engineering execution;
- developer execution;
- domain execution yang dimiliki Platform lain;
- governance authority di luar applicable mandate;
- organizational restructuring.

Performance Architecture berinteraksi dengan area tersebut hanya sejauh  
diperlukan untuk legitimate performance assessment.

⸻

**9\. Governance Boundary**

Performance Architecture harus mempertahankan boundary:

Governance Architecture

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├── Authority

&nbsp; ├── Decision

&nbsp; ├── Accountability

&nbsp; └── Governance Rules

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ▼

&nbsp; Performance Architecture

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├── Measure

&nbsp; ├── Evaluate

&nbsp; ├── Review

&nbsp; ├── Report

&nbsp; └── Feedback

Performance Architecture tidak berada di atas Governance Architecture.

⸻

**10\. Operational Boundary**

Performance Architecture berhubungan dengan operating model melalui evidence.

Governance Architecture

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Operations

&nbsp; ↓

Operating Result

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Architecture

Performance Architecture tidak menggantikan D1–D10.

D10 menentukan operating success.

Part E mengukur dan mengevaluasi performance architecture dan performance  
condition.

⸻

**11\. Organizational Boundary**

PD-03 memiliki primary organizational domain:

**Governance & Compliance**

Performance Architecture tidak menciptakan organizational sub-division baru.

Performance Architecture tidak mengubah:

- reporting hierarchy;
- organizational ownership;
- role;
- capability ownership;
- decision rights.

Setiap perubahan tersebut harus mengikuti applicable organizational or  
governance authority.

⸻

**12\. Policy Boundary**

PD-03 memiliki responsibility terhadap **Governance Policy**.

Performance Architecture dapat mengukur:

- policy effectiveness;
- policy adherence;
- policy lifecycle performance;
- policy-related governance outcomes;

apabila applicable measurement telah ditetapkan.

Performance Architecture tidak otomatis menjadi owner seluruh policy di AIOS.

Governance Policy

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03

Technical / Domain Policy

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Domain Owner

⸻

**13\. Control Boundary**

PD-03 memiliki responsibility terhadap Governance & Compliance control.

Performance Architecture dapat mengevaluasi:

- control effectiveness;
- control integrity;
- control evidence;
- control weakness;
- control improvement.

Namun:

Governance Control

&nbsp; ≠

All Technical Controls

dan:

Control Responsibility

&nbsp; ≠

Execution Ownership

Operational control implementation tetap berada pada applicable domain owner  
apabila ownership tersebut telah diberikan.

⸻

**14\. Certification Boundary**

Performance Architecture dapat mengukur certification performance, termasuk:

- certification coverage;
- evidence readiness;
- certification findings;
- certification completion;
- certification effectiveness;
- certification follow-up.

Namun:

Certification

&nbsp; ≠

Implementation

Certification performance tidak memindahkan implementation ownership kepada  
PD-03.

⸻

**15\. Compliance Boundary**

Performance Architecture dapat mengukur dan mengevaluasi compliance-related  
performance apabila obligation, criterion, dan evidence telah ditetapkan.

Compliance performance harus mempertahankan distinction:

Compliance Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Execution / Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Governance & Compliance Assessment

PD-03 tidak mengambil alih execution hanya karena compliance finding  
mengidentifikasi gap.

⸻

**16\. Governance Standards Boundary**

Governance Standards merupakan bagian dari PD-03 domain.

Performance Architecture dapat menilai:

- standard coverage;
- standard adoption;
- standard compliance;
- standard effectiveness;
- standard-related findings;
- improvement effectiveness.

Namun:

Governance Standards

&nbsp; ≠

All Technical Standards

Technical implementation standards tetap mengikuti applicable domain ownership.

⸻

**17\. Governance Baseline Relationship**

Governance Baseline menjadi canonical reference bagi PD-03.

Performance Architecture menggunakan Governance Baseline sebagai reference  
apabila suatu performance criterion memang diturunkan darinya.

Relationship:

Governance Baseline

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Criterion

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

Performance Architecture tidak mengubah Governance Baseline melalui  
performance finding.

Perubahan terhadap baseline harus mengikuti governance/change authority yang  
berlaku.

⸻

**18\. Performance Evidence Constitution**

Setiap material conclusion harus memiliki evidence yang memadai.

Canonical evidence chain:

Governance Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Expected Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence Source

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Evaluation

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Review

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Response

Evidence harus memiliki:

- source;
- context;
- provenance;
- applicable time;
- definition;
- integrity;
- sufficient quality.

Jika evidence tidak cukup:

**Do not manufacture a performance conclusion.**

⸻

**19\. Measurement Integrity**

Measurement harus mempertahankan:

1. definisi metric;
2. formula apabila applicable;
3. source;
4. owner;
5. historical integrity;
6. measurement method;
7. measurement lifecycle;
8. controlled change.

Minimum anti-gaming principle:

Metric Definition

&nbsp; ↓

Locked / Controlled

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Historical Result

&nbsp; ↓

Traceable Evaluation

KPI tidak boleh menjadi satu-satunya representation of Governance & Compliance  
performance.

⸻

**20\. Performance Feedback Loop**

Canonical PD-03 feedback cycle:

Define

&nbsp; ↓

Measure

&nbsp; ↓

Evaluate

&nbsp; ↓

Interpret

&nbsp; ↓

Review

&nbsp; ↓

Improve

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measure

&nbsp; ↓

Re-assess

Loop ini memungkinkan Governance & Compliance belajar dari evidence.

Feedback tidak secara otomatis mengubah governance, organizational ownership,  
atau operating model.

⸻

**21\. Performance and Accountability**

Performance Architecture harus menjaga accountability chain:

Objective

&nbsp; ↓

Accountability

&nbsp; ↓

Governance / Operation

&nbsp; ↓

Result

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Assessment

Jika terdapat failure, assessment harus terlebih dahulu menentukan apakah  
failure berasal dari:

- objective;
- governance;
- execution;
- measurement;
- capability;
- external constraint.

Performance finding tidak boleh digunakan untuk menetapkan blame tanpa  
evidence.

⸻

**22\. Performance and Decision Authority**

Performance Architecture dapat menyediakan:

- evidence;
- assessment;
- finding;
- trend;
- insight;
- recommendation;
- improvement signal.

Performance Architecture tidak secara unilateral:

- mengubah governance;
- mengubah authority;
- mengubah ownership;
- mengubah policy;
- mengubah standards;
- mengubah operating model;
- mengubah strategic objective;
- mengubah organizational structure.

Canonical relationship:

Performance Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Review

&nbsp; ↓

Decision Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Decision

⸻

**23\. Performance and Improvement**

Performance improvement mengikuti:

Performance Result

&nbsp; ↓

Review

&nbsp; ↓

Gap / Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Signal

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Action

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Effectiveness Assessment

E1 menetapkan constitutional principle.

Detail improvement mechanism berada pada E8.

⸻

**24\. Performance and Maturity**

Performance result dan performance maturity harus dibedakan.

Performance

"What results are being achieved?"

Maturity

"How capable is the performance system

of measuring, evaluating, governing,

improving, and adapting those results?"

High KPI achievement tidak otomatis berarti high performance maturity.

E9 menjadi authoritative maturity layer.

⸻

**25\. Performance and Success**

Performance Architecture success tidak boleh ditentukan hanya berdasarkan:

- KPI achievement;
- reporting completeness;
- number of completed actions;
- maturity label;
- dashboard quality.

Success harus dinilai secara integrated melalui E10.

E10 menjadi terminal acceptance layer bagi Part E.

⸻

**26\. Separation of Performance, Governance, and Operation**

Canonical constitutional separation:

PERFORMANCE

Measure

Evaluate

Interpret

Report

Improve

Feedback

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ▼

GOVERNANCE

Authorize

Decide

Govern

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ▼

OPERATION

Execute

Implement

Operate

Ketiga layer saling berinteraksi tetapi tidak boleh saling mengambil alih.

⸻

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Architecture menggunakan:

- objectives;

⸻

**29\. Inheritance Rules**

E1 mewariskan kepada E2–E10:

**IR-01 — Performance Philosophy**

Performance adalah evidence-driven measurement and feedback architecture.

**IR-02 — Performance Boundary**

Performance tidak mengambil alih governance atau operation.

**IR-03 — Authority Separation**

Performance assessment tidak menciptakan authority baru.

**IR-04 — Ownership Preservation**

Performance tidak memindahkan domain ownership.

**IR-05 — Evidence Discipline**

Material conclusion harus traceable terhadap evidence.

**IR-06 — Feedback Discipline**

Improvement harus dapat diverifikasi melalui re-measurement.

**IR-07 — Historical Integrity**

Historical evidence dan result tidak boleh diubah tanpa controlled process.

**IR-08 — Domain Adaptation**

Domain-specific metric, KPI, target, threshold, dan evidence source harus  
ditetapkan berdasarkan PD-03 context.

⸻

**30\. Non-Inheritance Rules**

Hal-hal berikut **tidak diwariskan otomatis** dari reference Performance  
Architecture:

- generic KPI catalogue;
- metric numbers;
- target;
- threshold;
- weighting;
- universal SLA;
- measurement frequency;
- domain evidence source;
- maturity score;
- domain-specific success threshold.

Jika authoritative PD-03 source tidak ditemukan:

**Classify as SOURCE GAP and carry forward for Claude Code reconciliation.**

Jangan mengubah SOURCE GAP menjadi requirement melalui inference.

⸻

⸻

**32\. Performance Constitution Success Criteria**

E1 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1. Performance Architecture PD-03 memiliki tujuan dan boundary yang eksplisit;
2. Performance dapat dibedakan dari Governance Authority;
3. Performance dapat dibedakan dari Operational Execution;
4. Performance dapat dibedakan dari organizational ownership;
5. Performance conclusion membutuhkan evidence;
6. evidence memiliki traceability;
7. measurement dapat dibedakan dari evaluation;
8. finding dapat dibedakan dari decision;
9. improvement dapat dibedakan dari action completion;
10. performance failure tidak otomatis memindahkan accountability;
11. cross-platform interaction tidak memindahkan ownership;
12. Governance Policy tetap berada dalam Governance & Compliance boundary;
13. Governance Control tidak otomatis berarti execution ownership;
14. Certification tidak berarti implementation ownership;
15. Governance Standards tidak disamakan dengan seluruh technical standards;
16. historical evidence tetap memiliki integrity;
17. domain-specific KPI dan metric tidak diinvensikan;
18. Performance Architecture dapat diwariskan ke E2–E10 tanpa mengubah  
    constitutional boundary;
19. material architectural changes terhadap E1 mengikuti Architecture Change  
    Control.

⸻

**33\. Source-Fidelity Classification**

**SOURCE-SUPPORTED**

Baseline mendukung:

- E1 sebagai Performance Constitution;
- E1 sebagai parent constitution;
- Performance Philosophy;
- Performance Principles;
- Performance Boundary;
- separation Performance–Governance–Operation;
- measurement → evaluation → insight → feedback;
- evidence and traceability discipline;
- inheritance pattern untuk PD-02–PD-10.

**PD-03-SPECIFIC SOURCE-SUPPORTED**

PD-03 memiliki domain:

**Governance & Compliance**

dengan responsibility yang mencakup Policy, Control, Certification, Governance  
Standards, dan Governance Baseline relationship. Boundary rules juga  
menetapkan bahwa:

- authority ≠ ownership;
- dependency ≠ subordination;
- certification ≠ implementation;
- control ≠ execution;
- boundary change harus explicit.

**BOUNDED CANONICAL SYNTHESIS**

Elemen berikut merupakan adaptation yang tetap berada dalam constitutional  
boundary:

- Governance & Compliance performance context;
- Policy performance boundary;
- Control performance boundary;
- Certification performance boundary;
- Compliance performance boundary;
- Governance Standards performance boundary;
- Governance Baseline → performance criterion relationship.

**SOURCE GAP**

Belum ada authoritative PD-03-specific source yang cukup untuk membekukan:

- exact E1 PD-03 KPI;
- exact metric catalogue;
- exact performance targets;
- exact thresholds;
- exact measurement frequency;
- exact maturity score;
- exact performance success thresholds;
- exact evidence repository schema;
- exact performance review cadence.

**Tidak ada nilai numerik atau requirement tersebut yang diinvensikan dalam  
E1.**

⸻

**34\. Claude Code Reconciliation Directive**

Sebelum implementation-level detail dibekukan, Claude Code **MUST** melakukan  
source reconciliation terhadap:

1. exact PD-03 E1 definition;
2. exact Performance Philosophy;
3. exact Performance Principles;
4. exact Performance Boundary;
5. exact PD-03 performance objectives;
6. exact Governance & Compliance performance dimensions;
7. exact evidence sources;
8. exact performance ownership;
9. exact review authority;
10. exact measurement requirements;
11. exact KPI/metric requirements;
12. exact target/threshold requirements;
13. exact relationship dengan D10 Operating Success;
14. exact relationship dengan C10 Governance Success;
15. exact Part E success criteria;
16. exact Architecture Change Control requirement.

Setiap finding harus diklasifikasikan:

SOURCE-SUPPORTED

REFERENCE-PATTERN

SOURCE-GAP

Claude Code **MUST NOT**:

- mengarang KPI;
- mengarang target;
- mengarang threshold;
- mengarang SLA;
- mengarang authority;
- mengarang organizational ownership;
- mengubah governance boundary;
- mengubah historical evidence;
- menjadikan performance finding sebagai governance decision;
- menjadikan performance measurement sebagai execution ownership.

Jika source tidak ditemukan:

**Preserve the bounded synthesis and carry the SOURCE GAP forward.**

**37\. Canonical Freeze Statement**

**E1 — Performance Constitution establishes the constitutional foundation of Performance Architecture for PD-03 Governance & Compliance. Performance Architecture exists to measure, evaluate, interpret, report, and provide feedback on Governance & Compliance performance using evidence and traceability, while preserving the authority, accountability, ownership, and operating boundaries established by the wider AIOS architecture.**

**PD-03 Performance Architecture applies specifically to the Governance & Compliance domain and does not convert performance responsibility into governance authority, organizational ownership, or operational execution ownership.**

**Policy, Control, Certification, Governance Standards, and applicable Governance Baseline relationships remain within their established PD-03 boundary. Control responsibility does not automatically constitute execution ownership, certification does not constitute implementation ownership, and cross-platform dependency does not constitute subordination.**

**E1 is the parent constitution for E2–E10. All subsequent Performance Architecture sections must conform to its performance philosophy, evidence discipline, authority separation, ownership preservation, historical integrity, and Performance–Governance–Operation boundary.**

**Domain-specific KPI, metric, target, threshold, cadence, evidence source, maturity score, and success threshold remain subject to source reconciliation and are not invented by E1.**

**E1 is therefore FROZEN as the Performance Constitution for Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance.**

\### Final Verdict

\*\*E1 — Performance Constitution: \`FROZEN — SOURCE-BOUNDED\`\*\*

E1 sekarang menjadi \*\*constitutional parent\*\* untuk E2–E10 PD-03. Fondasi utamanya terkunci:

\*\*Performance mengukur → mengevaluasi → menghasilkan insight → memberi feedback; Governance tetap mengotorisasi dan memutuskan; Operation tetap mengeksekusi.\*\*

E2

\# E2 — Performance Model

1\. Purpose

E2 menetapkan Performance Model untuk PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

E2 menjawab pertanyaan:

"Dimensi apa yang digunakan untuk memahami performance PD-03, bagaimana dimensi tersebut berhubungan, dan bagaimana kondisi performance PD-03 dievaluasi secara integrated?"

E2 menerjemahkan prinsip E1 menjadi model multidimensional.

E2 tidak menjadi:

• KPI catalogue;

• measurement specification;

• governance rule;

• operational procedure;

• authority model;

• decision-right model;

• organizational structure.

Concern tersebut berada pada section atau architecture layer yang sesuai.

⸻

2\. Constitutional Position

Relationship:

E1 — Performance Constitution

&nbsp; ↓

"What is performance and what are its boundaries?"

&nbsp; ↓

E2 — Performance Model

&nbsp; ↓

&nbsp; ↓

E3 — Performance Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

"How are those conditions measured?"

E1 menetapkan constitutional boundary.

E2 menetapkan interpretive model.

E3 kemudian menerjemahkan model tersebut menjadi measurement architecture.

Dengan demikian:

E1

Constitution

&nbsp; ↓

E2

Model

&nbsp; ↓

E3

Measurement

E2 tidak boleh melompat langsung dari dimension ke numeric target.

⸻

3\. Core Performance Model Principle

PD-03 performance is multidimensional and must be interpreted through complementary performance dimensions rather than a single KPI, metric, score, or operational result.

Performance model harus memungkinkan PD-03 mengetahui:

• apa yang sedang dicapai;

• bagaimana Governance & Compliance beroperasi;

• apakah governance mechanism berfungsi;

• apakah capability yang diperlukan tersedia;

• apakah improvement menghasilkan perubahan yang efektif.

Model tidak boleh membuat setiap dimension menjadi performance system yang  
terpisah.

Canonical relationship:

Performance Dimensions

&nbsp; ↓

Dimension Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Cross-Dimension Interpretation

&nbsp; ↓

Integrated Performance View

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Insight

⸻

4\. Canonical Performance Dimensions

Berdasarkan reference model yang tersedia, E2 menggunakan lima performance  
dimensions:

1\. Strategic Performance

2\. Operational Performance

3\. Governance Performance

4\. Capability Performance

5\. Improvement Performance

Kelima dimensi adalah lenses, bukan lima sistem yang berdiri sendiri.

&nbsp; PD-03 PERFORMANCE

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ┌─────────────────┼─────────────────┐

&nbsp; ↓ ↓ ↓

&nbsp; Strategic Operational Governance

&nbsp; │ │ │

&nbsp; └─────────────────┼─────────────────┘

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ┌───────┴───────┐

&nbsp; ↓ ↓

&nbsp; Capability Improvement

&nbsp; │ │

&nbsp; └───────┬───────┘

&nbsp; ↓

&nbsp; Integrated Assessment

Source secara eksplisit memberikan lima dimensi tersebut sebagai reference  
model.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

5\. Source-Fidelity Note — Dimension Count

Terdapat satu inconsistency dalam historical review material:

• salah satu review menyebut "six requested performance dimensions";

• canonical list yang sama hanya mencantumkan lima dimensions.

Karena tidak terdapat source authoritative yang menetapkan dimensi keenam,  
E2 menggunakan lima dimensions yang benar-benar didefinisikan oleh source.

Jangan menginferensikan dimensi keenam.

Jika Claude Code menemukan authoritative PD-03 source yang menetapkan dimensi  
tambahan, perubahan harus diproses melalui source reconciliation dan  
Architecture Change Control.

⸻

6\. Strategic Performance

6.1 Definition

Strategic Performance mengukur sejauh mana PD-03 berkontribusi terhadap  
strategic intent yang memang berada dalam scope PD-03.

Untuk PD-03, strategic performance tidak berarti PD-03 menjadi owner seluruh  
AIOS strategy.

Strategic Performance menjawab:

"Sejauh mana Governance & Compliance mendukung strategic condition yang  
memang menjadi tanggung jawab PD-03?"

⸻

6.2 PD-03 Strategic Boundary

Strategic Performance PD-03 dapat berhubungan dengan:

• Governance & Compliance strategic objectives;

• governance integrity;

• compliance posture;

• governance capability;

• policy/control/certification outcomes.

Namun tidak secara otomatis mencakup:

• enterprise strategy ownership;

• Architecture strategy ownership;

• Runtime strategy ownership;

• AI Engineering strategy ownership;

• Infrastructure strategy ownership;

• Security strategy ownership;

• Quality strategy ownership.

⸻

6.3 Strategic Performance Model

Applicable Strategic Intent

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Strategic Responsibility

&nbsp; ↓

Governance & Compliance Outcome

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Strategic Performance Assessment

Strategic Performance tidak menetapkan strategic intent.

⸻

7\. Operational Performance

7.1 Definition

Operational Performance menilai seberapa baik PD-03 operating model menghasilkan  
intended Governance & Compliance operational outcomes.

Operational Performance menjawab:

"Apakah Governance & Compliance operation berjalan sebagaimana  
dimaksud?"

⸻

7.2 PD-03 Operational Context

Operational Performance dapat mencakup:

• governance operating effectiveness;

• decision operation;

• governance review operation;

• coordination;

• operational control;

• governance reporting;

• improvement execution effectiveness.

D1–D10 menjadi primary operating architecture context.

⸻

7.3 Operational Boundary

Operational Performance tidak menjadikan Performance Architecture sebagai  
operator.

PD-03 Operating Model

&nbsp; ↓

Operation

&nbsp; ↓

Result

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Assessment

Performance Architecture menilai operation.

Performance Architecture tidak menggantikan operation.

⸻

8\. Governance Performance

8.1 Definition

Governance Performance menilai apakah Governance & Compliance governance  
mechanisms berfungsi sesuai authority, accountability, decision, review, dan  
control framework yang berlaku.

Governance Performance menjawab:

"Apakah governance mechanism PD-03 berfungsi dengan integrity dan  
authority yang benar?"

⸻

8.2 Governance Performance Scope

Governance Performance dapat mencakup:

• governance decision effectiveness;

• governance review effectiveness;

• authority clarity;

• accountability clarity;

• governance control effectiveness;

• policy governance;

• certification governance;

• compliance governance;

• governance evidence integrity.

⸻

8.3 Governance Boundary

Governance Performance tidak menciptakan governance authority baru.

Governance Architecture

&nbsp; ↓

Defines Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Operation

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Architecture

&nbsp; ↓

Assesses Governance Performance

Performance finding tidak menggantikan governance decision.

9\. Capability Performance

9.1 Definition

Capability Performance menilai apakah capability yang diperlukan untuk  
menjalankan Governance & Compliance tersedia dan berfungsi sesuai kebutuhan.

Capability Performance menjawab:

"Apakah PD-03 memiliki capability yang cukup untuk menjalankan mandatnya?"

⸻

9.2 Capability Scope

Capability Performance dapat melihat:

• capability readiness;

• capability effectiveness;

• capability availability;

• capability resilience;

• capability gaps;

• capability improvement.

⸻

9.3 Capability Ownership Boundary

Performance Architecture tidak mengambil ownership capability.

Capability Owner

Capability

Review

10\. Improvement Performance

10.1 Definition

Improvement Performance menilai apakah improvement menghasilkan perubahan  
yang efektif dan sustainable.

Improvement Performance menjawab:

"Apakah improvement yang dilakukan benar-benar memperbaiki kondisi  
performance?"

⸻

10.2 Improvement vs Completion

Canonical rule:

Action Completed

&nbsp; ≠

Improvement Effective

Improvement Performance membutuhkan evidence setelah action.

Canonical flow:

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Action

&nbsp; ↓

Implementation

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Effectiveness Assessment

⸻

10.3 Improvement Boundary

Improvement Performance tidak memberikan authority kepada Performance  
Management untuk menjalankan improvement secara unilateral.

Performance Architecture:

• identifies;

• evaluates;

• signals;

• verifies.

Authorized owner:

• decides;

• implements;

• executes.

12\. Cross-Dimension Interpretation

Performance assessment harus dapat menjawab:

"Apa hubungan antara hasil pada satu dimension dengan hasil pada  
dimension lainnya?"

Contoh:

Low Governance Performance

&nbsp; +

Strong Capability

&nbsp; ↓

Possible Governance Process Issue

atau:

Low Operational Performance

&nbsp; +

Strong Governance

&nbsp; +

Weak Capability

&nbsp; ↓

Possible Capability Constraint

Interpretation harus berbasis evidence.

Jangan menetapkan root cause hanya berdasarkan correlation.

13\. Performance Health

E2 membedakan:

Performance Score

Nilai hasil measurement terhadap metric atau criterion tertentu.

Performance Health

Interpretasi multidimensional terhadap kondisi PD-03.

Canonical model:

Metrics

&nbsp; ↓

Dimension Results

&nbsp; ↓

Cross-Dimension Analysis

&nbsp; ↓

Context

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Health

Dengan demikian:

Score is a measurement output; health is an evaluated performance  
condition.

Performance health tidak boleh diturunkan hanya dari satu angka.

14\. Performance Trend

Performance harus dapat dipahami secara temporal.

Canonical model:

Previous Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Current Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Trend

&nbsp; ↓

Expected Direction

&nbsp; ↓

Evaluation

Trend classification dapat mencakup:

Improving

Stable

Declining

Volatile

Insufficient Evidence

Classification tersebut bersifat interpretive.

Exact thresholds tidak dibekukan oleh E2.

⸻

15\. Current State vs Trend

Current performance dan trend harus dibedakan.

Contoh:

High Current Performance

&nbsp; +

Declining Trend

berbeda dari:

Low Current Performance

&nbsp; +

Improving Trend

Keduanya menghasilkan governance/performance interpretation yang berbeda.

Trend tidak menggantikan current-state assessment.

⸻

16\. Performance Diagnostic Model

Ketika performance berada di bawah expected condition, E2 harus memungkinkan  
diagnosis tanpa premature attribution.

Minimum diagnostic categories:

Strategic Gap

Operational Gap

Governance Gap

Capability Gap

Improvement Gap

Measurement Gap

External Constraint

Source secara eksplisit menekankan bahwa performance failure tidak boleh  
langsung dianggap sebagai execution failure.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

17\. Strategic Gap

Strategic Gap terjadi apabila:

• objective tidak tercapai;

• expected strategic condition tidak tercapai;

• objective definition bermasalah;

• strategic assumption berubah.

Performance Architecture tidak mengubah objective.

Jika objective perlu diubah:

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Strategic Review

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Decision

⸻

18\. Operational Gap

Operational Gap terjadi apabila operating mechanism tidak menghasilkan  
expected condition.

Possible causes:

• process weakness;

• coordination failure;

• control weakness;

• execution constraint;

• operating design issue.

Performance Architecture tidak mengambil alih operational correction.

⸻

19\. Governance Gap

Governance Gap terjadi apabila performance evidence menunjukkan masalah pada:

• authority;

• decision;

• accountability;

• governance mechanism;

• policy;

• review;

• governance control.

Governance Gap harus diarahkan kepada Governance Architecture/Authority yang  
sesuai.

⸻

20\. Capability Gap

Capability Gap terjadi apabila capability tidak mencukupi untuk memenuhi  
expected condition.

Capability gap dapat meliputi:

• missing capability;

• insufficient capability;

• degraded capability;

• unavailable capability;

• capability mismatch.

Performance Architecture tidak menentukan organizational redesign secara  
sepihak.

⸻

21\. Improvement Gap

Improvement Gap terjadi apabila:

• finding berulang;

• corrective action tidak efektif;

• improvement impact rendah;

• regression terjadi;

• improvement tidak sustainable.

Improvement Gap mengarah ke E8.

⸻

22\. Measurement Gap

Measurement Gap terjadi apabila:

• metric tidak tersedia;

• evidence tidak cukup;

• measurement method tidak reliable;

• source tidak valid;

• data quality tidak memadai.

Canonical status:

Not Measured

Measurement Gap

Insufficient Evidence

Measurement Pending

Ketiadaan measurement tidak boleh diubah menjadi performance failure.

⸻

23\. External Constraint

External Constraint terjadi apabila performance condition dipengaruhi oleh  
faktor yang berada di luar control/authority PD-03.

Contoh generic:

• external dependency;

• regulatory change;

• unavailable upstream condition;

• external constraint.

External constraint tidak boleh digunakan sebagai excuse tanpa evidence.

⸻

24\. Integrated Performance Assessment

Overall performance assessment harus mengintegrasikan seluruh dimensions.

Canonical model:

Strategic Assessment

&nbsp; +

Operational Assessment

&nbsp; +

Governance Assessment

&nbsp; +

Capability Assessment

&nbsp; +

Improvement Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Integrated Performance Assessment

Tidak diperbolehkan:

Dimension A = 90

Dimension B = 80

Dimension C = 70

Dimension D = 90

Dimension E = 80

&nbsp; ↓

Simple Average = Overall Performance

kecuali weighting dan aggregation method telah ditetapkan oleh authoritative  
measurement/performance framework.

E2 sendiri tidak menetapkan weighting.

⸻

25\. No Single-Score Principle

Tidak ada single score yang secara default merepresentasikan seluruh PD-03  
performance.

Jika suatu integrated score diperlukan:

• formula harus explicit;

• weighting harus authoritative;

• data source harus traceable;

• material exception tidak boleh tersembunyi;

• confidence harus tersedia;

• aggregation rule harus controlled.

⸻

26\. Dimension Independence

Setiap dimension harus dapat dianalisis secara independen.

Tujuannya:

• menemukan weakness;

• mencegah double counting;

• menemukan trade-off;

• mempertahankan diagnostic clarity.

Namun independence tidak berarti isolation.

Independent Assessment

&nbsp; +

Cross-Dimension Interpretation

&nbsp; ↓

Integrated View

⸻

27\. Double-Counting Control

Satu performance condition dapat berhubungan dengan lebih dari satu dimension.

Jika demikian, harus ditentukan:

• primary dimension;

• secondary dimension;

• relationship;

• interpretation purpose.

Contoh:

Governance Control Failure

Primary:

Governance Performance

Possible Secondary:

Operational Performance

Improvement Performance

Jangan menghitung satu finding sebagai tiga independent failures tanpa  
rationale.

56\. Canonical Freeze Statement

E2 — Performance Model establishes the multidimensional model through which PD-03 Governance & Compliance performance is understood, interpreted, and assessed. The canonical model uses Strategic, Operational, Governance, Capability, and Improvement Performance as complementary lenses rather than independent performance systems.

PD-03-specific interpretation of those dimensions remains bounded by the Governance & Compliance domain and its Policy, Control, Certification, and Compliance responsibilities. Governance Standards and Governance Baseline relationships may provide applicable performance context without transferring ownership outside PD-03's established boundary.

Performance Health is an evaluated multidimensional condition and is not equivalent to a single performance score. Performance Trend is distinct from current-state performance. Performance Diagnostic categories must distinguish strategic, operational, governance, capability, improvement, measurement, and external conditions before attributing cause.

Performance Model does not create strategy, governance authority, operational authority, organizational ownership, or cross-platform execution ownership. Control responsibility does not become execution ownership; certification does not become implementation ownership; dependency does not become subordination.

E2 provides the interpretive foundation for E3 Performance Measurement, E4 KPI & Success Metrics, E5 Performance Review, E6 Performance Governance, E7 Reporting, E8 Improvement, E9 Maturity, and E10 Performance Success Constitution.

Domain-specific metrics, KPI, targets, thresholds, weighting, health formulas, trend thresholds, and score models remain SOURCE GAP until verified through authoritative PD-03 source reconciliation.

E2 is therefore FROZEN as the Performance Model for Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

⸻

**1\. Purpose**

E2 menetapkan Performance Model untuk PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

E2 menjawab pertanyaan:

"Dimensi apa yang digunakan untuk memahami performance PD-03, bagaimana dimensi tersebut berhubungan, dan bagaimana kondisi performance PD-03 dievaluasi secara integrated?"

E2 menerjemahkan prinsip E1 menjadi model multidimensional.

E2 tidak menjadi:

- KPI catalogue;
- measurement specification;
- governance rule;
- operational procedure;
- authority model;
- decision-right model;
- organizational structure.

Concern tersebut berada pada section atau architecture layer yang sesuai.

⸻

**2\. Constitutional Position**

E1

E2

Model

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

⸻

**3\. Core Performance Model Principle**

PD-03 performance is multidimensional and must be interpreted through complementary performance dimensions rather than a single KPI, metric, score, or operational result.

Performance model harus memungkinkan PD-03 mengetahui:

- apa yang sedang dicapai;
- bagaimana Governance & Compliance beroperasi;
- apakah governance mechanism berfungsi;
- apakah capability yang diperlukan tersedia;
- apakah improvement menghasilkan perubahan yang efektif.

Model tidak boleh membuat setiap dimension menjadi performance system yang  
terpisah.

Canonical relationship:

Performance Dimensions

&nbsp; ↓

Dimension Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Cross-Dimension Interpretation

&nbsp; ↓

Integrated Performance View

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Insight

⸻

**4\. Canonical Performance Dimensions**

Berdasarkan reference model yang tersedia, E2 menggunakan lima performance  
dimensions:

1\. Strategic Performance

2\. Operational Performance

3\. Governance Performance

4\. Capability Performance

5\. Improvement Performance

Kelima dimensi adalah lenses, bukan lima sistem yang berdiri sendiri.

model.

**5\. Source-Fidelity Note — Dimension Count**

Terdapat satu inconsistency dalam historical review material:

- salah satu review menyebut "six requested performance dimensions";
- canonical list yang sama hanya mencantumkan lima dimensions.

Karena tidak terdapat source authoritative yang menetapkan dimensi keenam,  
E2 menggunakan lima dimensions yang benar-benar didefinisikan oleh source.

Jangan menginferensikan dimensi keenam.

Jika Claude Code menemukan authoritative PD-03 source yang menetapkan dimensi  
tambahan, perubahan harus diproses melalui source reconciliation dan  
Architecture Change Control.

**6\. Strategic Performance**

**6.1 Definition**

Strategic Performance mengukur sejauh mana PD-03 berkontribusi terhadap  
strategic intent yang memang berada dalam scope PD-03.

Untuk PD-03, strategic performance tidak berarti PD-03 menjadi owner seluruh  
AIOS strategy.

Strategic Performance menjawab:

"Sejauh mana Governance & Compliance mendukung strategic condition yang  
memang menjadi tanggung jawab PD-03?"

⸻

**6.2 PD-03 Strategic Boundary**

Strategic Performance PD-03 dapat berhubungan dengan:

- Governance & Compliance strategic objectives;
- governance integrity;
- compliance posture;
- governance capability;
- policy/control/certification outcomes.

Namun tidak secara otomatis mencakup:

- enterprise strategy ownership;
- Architecture strategy ownership;
- Runtime strategy ownership;
- AI Engineering strategy ownership;
- Infrastructure strategy ownership;
- Security strategy ownership;
- Quality strategy ownership.

⸻

**6.3 Strategic Performance Model**

Applicable Strategic Intent

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Strategic Responsibility

&nbsp; ↓

Governance & Compliance Outcome

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Strategic Performance Assessment

Strategic Performance tidak menetapkan strategic intent.

⸻

**7\. Operational Performance**

**7.1 Definition**

Operational Performance menilai seberapa baik PD-03 operating model menghasilkan  
intended Governance & Compliance operational outcomes.

Operational Performance menjawab:

"Apakah Governance & Compliance operation berjalan sebagaimana  
dimaksud?"

⸻

**7.2 PD-03 Operational Context**

Operational Performance dapat mencakup:

- governance operating effectiveness;
- decision operation;
- governance review operation;
- coordination;
- operational control;
- governance reporting;
- improvement execution effectiveness.

D1–D10 menjadi primary operating architecture context.

⸻

**7.3 Operational Boundary**

Operational Performance tidak menjadikan Performance Architecture sebagai  
operator.

PD-03 Operating Model

&nbsp; ↓

Operation

Performance Architecture tidak menggantikan operation.

- governance control effectiveness;

- compliance governance;
- governance evidence integrity.

&nbsp; ≠

**12\. Cross-Dimension Interpretation**

Performance assessment harus dapat menjawab:

"Apa hubungan antara hasil pada satu dimension dengan hasil pada  
dimension lainnya?"

Contoh:

Low Governance Performance

&nbsp; +

Strong Capability

&nbsp; ↓

Possible Governance Process Issue

atau:

Low Operational Performance

&nbsp; +

Strong Governance

&nbsp; +

Weak Capability

&nbsp; ↓

Possible Capability Constraint

Interpretation harus berbasis evidence.

Jangan menetapkan root cause hanya berdasarkan correlation.

**13\. Performance Health**

Performance Health

Interpretasi multidimensional terhadap kondisi PD-03.

Metrics

&nbsp; ↓

Dimension Results

&nbsp; ↓

Cross-Dimension Analysis

&nbsp; ↓

Context

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Health

Performance health tidak boleh diturunkan hanya dari satu angka.

**14\. Performance Trend**

Performance harus dapat dipahami secara temporal.

Canonical model:

Previous Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Current Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Trend

&nbsp; ↓

Expected Direction

&nbsp; ↓

Evaluation

Trend classification dapat mencakup:

Improving

Stable

Declining

Volatile

Insufficient Evidence

Classification tersebut bersifat interpretive.

Exact thresholds tidak dibekukan oleh E2.

⸻

**15\. Current State vs Trend**

Current performance dan trend harus dibedakan.

Contoh:

High Current Performance

&nbsp; +

Declining Trend

berbeda dari:

Low Current Performance

&nbsp; +

Improving Trend

Keduanya menghasilkan governance/performance interpretation yang berbeda.

Trend tidak menggantikan current-state assessment.

⸻

**16\. Performance Diagnostic Model**

Ketika performance berada di bawah expected condition, E2 harus memungkinkan  
diagnosis tanpa premature attribution.

Minimum diagnostic categories:

Strategic Gap

Operational Gap

Governance Gap

Capability Gap

Improvement Gap

Measurement Gap

External Constraint

Source secara eksplisit menekankan bahwa performance failure tidak boleh  
langsung dianggap sebagai execution failure.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

**17\. Strategic Gap**

Strategic Gap terjadi apabila:

- objective tidak tercapai;
- expected strategic condition tidak tercapai;
- objective definition bermasalah;
- strategic assumption berubah.

Performance Architecture tidak mengubah objective.

Jika objective perlu diubah:

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Strategic Review

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Decision

⸻

**18\. Operational Gap**

Operational Gap terjadi apabila operating mechanism tidak menghasilkan  
expected condition.

Possible causes:

- process weakness;
- coordination failure;
- control weakness;
- execution constraint;
- operating design issue.

Performance Architecture tidak mengambil alih operational correction.

⸻

**19\. Governance Gap**

Governance Gap terjadi apabila performance evidence menunjukkan masalah pada:

- authority;
- decision;
- accountability;
- governance mechanism;
- policy;
- review;
- governance control.

Governance Gap harus diarahkan kepada Governance Architecture/Authority yang  
sesuai.

⸻

**20\. Capability Gap**

Capability Gap terjadi apabila capability tidak mencukupi untuk memenuhi  
expected condition.

Capability gap dapat meliputi:

- missing capability;
- insufficient capability;
- degraded capability;
- unavailable capability;
- capability mismatch.

Performance Architecture tidak menentukan organizational redesign secara  
sepihak.

⸻

**21\. Improvement Gap**

Improvement Gap terjadi apabila:

- finding berulang;
- corrective action tidak efektif;
- improvement impact rendah;
- regression terjadi;
- improvement tidak sustainable.

Improvement Gap mengarah ke E8.

⸻

**22\. Measurement Gap**

Measurement Gap terjadi apabila:

- metric tidak tersedia;
- evidence tidak cukup;
- measurement method tidak reliable;
- source tidak valid;
- data quality tidak memadai.

Canonical status:

Not Measured

Measurement Gap

Insufficient Evidence

Measurement Pending

Ketiadaan measurement tidak boleh diubah menjadi performance failure.

⸻

**23\. External Constraint**

External Constraint terjadi apabila performance condition dipengaruhi oleh  
faktor yang berada di luar control/authority PD-03.

Contoh generic:

- external dependency;
- regulatory change;
- unavailable upstream condition;
- external constraint.

External constraint tidak boleh digunakan sebagai excuse tanpa evidence.

⸻

**24\. Integrated Performance Assessment**

Overall performance assessment harus mengintegrasikan seluruh dimensions.

Canonical model:

Strategic Assessment

&nbsp; +

Operational Assessment

&nbsp; +

Governance Assessment

&nbsp; +

Capability Assessment

&nbsp; +

Improvement Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Integrated Performance Assessment

Tidak diperbolehkan:

Dimension A = 90

Dimension B = 80

Dimension C = 70

Dimension D = 90

Dimension E = 80

&nbsp; ↓

Simple Average = Overall Performance

kecuali weighting dan aggregation method telah ditetapkan oleh authoritative  
measurement/performance framework.

E2 sendiri tidak menetapkan weighting.

⸻

**25\. No Single-Score Principle**

Tidak ada single score yang secara default merepresentasikan seluruh PD-03  
performance.

Jika suatu integrated score diperlukan:

- formula harus explicit;
- weighting harus authoritative;
- data source harus traceable;
- material exception tidak boleh tersembunyi;
- confidence harus tersedia;
- aggregation rule harus controlled.

⸻

**26\. Dimension Independence**

Setiap dimension harus dapat dianalisis secara independen.

Tujuannya:

- menemukan weakness;
- mencegah double counting;
- menemukan trade-off;
- mempertahankan diagnostic clarity.

Namun independence tidak berarti isolation.

Independent Assessment

&nbsp; +

Cross-Dimension Interpretation

&nbsp; ↓

Integrated View

⸻

**27\. Double-Counting Control**

Satu performance condition dapat berhubungan dengan lebih dari satu dimension.

Jika demikian, harus ditentukan:

- primary dimension;
- secondary dimension;
- relationship;
- interpretation purpose.

Contoh:

Governance Control Failure

Primary:

Governance Performance

Possible Secondary:

Operational Performance

Improvement Performance

Jangan menghitung satu finding sebagai tiga independent failures tanpa  
rationale.

⸻

**50\. Non-Goals**

E2 tidak bertujuan untuk:

- menetapkan strategic direction;
- menetapkan governance authority;
- menetapkan operational process;
- menetapkan organizational structure;
- menentukan individual performance;
- menentukan compensation;
- menetapkan universal KPI;
- menetapkan universal target;
- menetapkan universal threshold;
- menghapus domain ownership;
- mengambil alih Platform lain.

⸻

**51\. Performance Model Success Criteria**

E2 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1. PD-03 performance dapat dipahami secara multidimensional;
2. lima canonical dimensions dapat dibedakan;
3. setiap dimension memiliki interpretation purpose;
4. Strategic Performance tidak mengambil strategic authority;
5. Operational Performance tidak mengambil operational ownership;
6. Governance Performance tidak mengambil governance authority;
7. Capability Performance tidak mengambil capability ownership;
8. Improvement Performance menilai effectiveness, bukan completion semata;
9. dimension results dapat dianalisis independently;
10. cross-dimension relationship dapat dianalisis;
11. performance health dapat dibedakan dari performance score;
12. performance trend dapat dibedakan dari current state;
13. diagnostic categories dapat membedakan kemungkinan root conditions;
14. measurement gap dapat dinyatakan tanpa false score;
15. insufficient evidence dapat menghentikan conclusion;
16. cross-platform dependency tidak mengubah ownership;
17. Policy performance tetap berada pada applicable governance scope;
18. Control performance tidak menjadi technical execution ownership;
19. Certification performance tidak menjadi implementation ownership;
20. Compliance performance tetap mempertahankan applicable ownership;
21. integrated assessment tidak otomatis menggunakan simple average;
22. E2 tetap conform terhadap E1;
23. E2 menyediakan stable interpretive foundation untuk E3–E10;
24. material E2 changes mengikuti Architecture Change Control.

⸻

- primary domain = Governance & Compliance;
- Policy;
- Control;
- Certification;
- Compliance;
- Governance Standards relationship;
- Governance Baseline relationship;
- authority ≠ ownership;
- dependency ≠ subordination;
- certification ≠ implementation;
- control ≠ execution;
- cross-platform ownership preservation.

**BOUNDED CANONICAL SYNTHESIS**

Adaptasi PD-03 dalam E2 meliputi:

- interpretation of Operational Performance for Governance & Compliance;

Adaptasi tersebut tidak mengubah five-dimension reference model atau  
constitutional boundary E1.

**SOURCE GAP**

Belum ditemukan authoritative PD-03-specific source yang menetapkan secara  
eksplisit:

Tidak ada item tersebut yang diinvensikan sebagai requirement dalam E2.

**56\. Canonical Freeze Statement**

E2 — Performance Model establishes the multidimensional model through which PD-03 Governance & Compliance performance is understood, interpreted, and assessed. The canonical model uses Strategic, Operational, Governance, Capability, and Improvement Performance as complementary lenses rather than independent performance systems.

PD-03-specific interpretation of those dimensions remains bounded by the Governance & Compliance domain and its Policy, Control, Certification, and Compliance responsibilities. Governance Standards and Governance Baseline relationships may provide applicable performance context without transferring ownership outside PD-03's established boundary.

Performance Health is an evaluated multidimensional condition and is not equivalent to a single performance score. Performance Trend is distinct from current-state performance. Performance Diagnostic categories must distinguish strategic, operational, governance, capability, improvement, measurement, and external conditions before attributing cause.

Performance Model does not create strategy, governance authority, operational authority, organizational ownership, or cross-platform execution ownership. Control responsibility does not become execution ownership; certification does not become implementation ownership; dependency does not become subordination.

E2 provides the interpretive foundation for E3 Performance Measurement, E4 KPI & Success Metrics, E5 Performance Review, E6 Performance Governance, E7 Reporting, E8 Improvement, E9 Maturity, and E10 Performance Success Constitution.

Domain-specific metrics, KPI, targets, thresholds, weighting, health formulas, trend thresholds, and score models remain SOURCE GAP until verified through authoritative PD-03 source reconciliation.

E2 is therefore FROZEN as the Performance Model for Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

⸻

**Final Verdict**

E2 — Performance Model: FROZEN — SOURCE-BOUNDED

1\. Purpose

E3 menetapkan bagaimana performance PD-03 diukur secara evidence-based,  
traceable, repeatable, dan controlled.

E3 menerjemahkan:

E1 — Performance Constitution

&nbsp; ↓

E2 — Performance Model

&nbsp; ↓

E3 — Performance Measurement

E3 menjawab:

"Bagaimana kondisi performance PD-03 diubah menjadi measurement result yang dapat dipercaya dan ditelusuri?"

E3 tidak menjawab:

• apakah performance tersebut baik atau buruk;

• apakah objective berhasil;

• apakah governance decision harus diubah;

• apakah operational owner harus melakukan tindakan tertentu.

Pertanyaan tersebut berada pada layer evaluation, review, governance, dan  
improvement yang sesuai.

⸻

2\. Constitutional Position

Canonical boundary:

E2 — Performance Model

&nbsp; ↓

What should be understood?

&nbsp; ↓

E3 — Performance Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

How is it measured?

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Result

&nbsp; ↓

E5 / Performance Evaluation

&nbsp; ↓

What does the result mean?

Measurement result adalah input evidence untuk evaluation.

Measurement result bukan performance conclusion.

⸻

3\. Measurement Philosophy

Measurement harus mengubah kondisi nyata menjadi evidence yang:

• observable;

• definable;

• reproducible;

• comparable;

• traceable;

• quality-assessed;

• version-controlled.

Canonical chain:

Real Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Definition

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Method

&nbsp; ↓

Validated Data

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Result

&nbsp; ↓

Traceability

Measurement bukan sekadar pengumpulan angka.

Measurement harus mempertahankan hubungan antara:

What

Why

How

Source

When

Owner

Result

Evidence

⸻

Governance & Compliance

dengan primary responsibilities:

Control

Compliance

tersebut tanpa memperluas ownership PD-03 ke domain Platform lain.
