Governance Standard

&nbsp; ↓

Expected Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Observed Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Variance

Namun:

Governance Standards

&nbsp; ≠

All Technical Standards

Technical implementation standards tetap berada pada applicable domain.

⸻

11\. Governance Baseline Relationship

Governance Baseline dapat menjadi reference condition untuk measurement.

Governance Baseline

&nbsp; ↓

Expected Governance Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Result

Measurement tidak mengubah Governance Baseline.

Jika measurement menunjukkan gap terhadap baseline:

Measurement Result

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Evaluation

&nbsp; ↓

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Governance Review

⸻

12\. Performance Dimensions

Setiap measurement harus dapat dipetakan ke Performance Model E2.

Canonical dimensions:

Strategic

Operational

Governance

Capability

Improvement

Measurement harus memiliki primary dimension apabila satu measurement  
berhubungan dengan lebih dari satu dimension.

Contoh:

Governance Decision Compliance

&nbsp; ↓

Primary: Governance

Governance Process Timeliness

&nbsp; ↓

Primary: Operational

Governance Capability Readiness

&nbsp; ↓

Primary: Capability

Improvement Effectiveness

&nbsp; ↓

Primary: Improvement

⸻

13\. Cross-Dimension Measurement

Satu measurement dapat memberikan evidence kepada lebih dari satu dimension.

Namun penggunaan lintas-dimensi harus eksplisit.

Canonical rule:

One Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Primary Dimension

&nbsp; +

Secondary Context

Tujuannya mencegah:

• double counting;

• duplicated findings;

• distorted integrated assessment.

⸻

14\. Measurement Hierarchy

Canonical hierarchy:

Performance Objective

&nbsp; ↓

Success Criterion

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Metric

&nbsp; ↓

Indicator

&nbsp; ↓

KPI

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Result

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Evaluation

Tidak semua performance condition membutuhkan KPI.

Tidak semua metric harus menjadi KPI.

Tidak semua indicator harus menjadi executive metric.

⸻

15\. Metric

Metric adalah definisi terukur yang digunakan untuk mengamati kondisi tertentu.

Minimum metric definition:

Metric ID

Metric Name

Purpose

Definition

Dimension

Unit

Source

Method

Frequency

Owner

Evidence Reference

Version

Metric harus memiliki definisi yang dapat dipahami dan direproduksi.

⸻

16\. Indicator

Indicator menggunakan satu atau lebih measurement result untuk menunjukkan  
kondisi atau arah tertentu.

Indicator dapat digunakan sebagai:

• leading indicator;

• lagging indicator;

• supporting indicator;

• diagnostic indicator.

Indicator tidak otomatis menjadi KPI.

⸻

17\. KPI

KPI adalah metric/indicator yang secara formal dipilih sebagai representasi  
penting terhadap keberhasilan atau kondisi performance.

Canonical relationship:

Metric

&nbsp; ↓

Candidate Indicator

&nbsp; ↓

KPI Selection

&nbsp; ↓

Formal KPI

E3 tidak menentukan KPI catalogue PD-03.

KPI selection menjadi concern E4.

⸻

18\. Leading Indicator

Leading indicator memberikan sinyal terhadap kondisi yang dapat  
mendahului outcome.

Contoh generic:

Governance Review Completion

&nbsp; ↓

Potential Signal

&nbsp; ↓

Future Governance Outcome

Leading indicator tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai proof of final success.

⸻

19\. Lagging Indicator

Lagging indicator mengukur kondisi atau outcome yang telah terjadi.

Contoh:

Compliance Result

&nbsp; ↓

Observed Outcome

&nbsp; ↓

Lagging Indicator

Leading dan lagging indicators harus dapat dibedakan secara eksplisit.

⸻

20\. Measurement Requirement

Measurement Requirement muncul ketika organisasi perlu mengetahui kondisi  
tertentu.

Trigger dapat berasal dari:

• strategic objective;

• success criterion;

• performance dimension;

• governance requirement;

• operational requirement;

• capability requirement;

• improvement requirement;

• compliance requirement;

• certification requirement;

• control requirement.

Output:

Measurement Requirement

⸻

21\. Measurement Specification

Setiap formal measurement harus memiliki specification.

Minimum:

What is measured?

Why is it measured?

How is it measured?

Who owns it?

What is the source?

What is the unit?

What is the frequency?

What is the validity rule?

What evidence supports it?

Output:

Measurement Specification

⸻

22\. Measurement Lifecycle

Canonical lifecycle:

1\. Identify

&nbsp; ↓

2\. Define

&nbsp; ↓

3\. Approve

&nbsp; ↓

4\. Instrument

&nbsp; ↓

5\. Collect

&nbsp; ↓

6\. Validate

&nbsp; ↓

7\. Calculate

&nbsp; ↓

8\. Record

&nbsp; ↓

9\. Report

&nbsp; ↓

10\. Review

&nbsp; ↓

11\. Maintain

&nbsp; ↓

12\. Retire / Replace

Lifecycle ini berlaku sebagai reference pattern.

Domain-specific implementation dapat menyesuaikan detailnya tanpa mengubah  
measurement principles.

⸻

23\. Phase 1 — Identify

Identifikasi measurement dilakukan ketika terdapat legitimate need untuk  
mengetahui suatu condition.

Output:

Measurement Requirement

Measurement tidak boleh dibuat hanya karena data tersedia.

⸻

24\. Phase 2 — Define

Measurement definition harus ditetapkan sebelum formal collection.

Minimum:

• metric;

• purpose;

• dimension;

• unit;

• source;

• method;

• frequency;

• ownership;

• validity;

• evidence.

Output:

Measurement Specification

⸻

25\. Phase 3 — Approve

Formal measurement mechanism harus memperoleh approval sesuai authority  
yang berlaku.

Approval memastikan:

• legitimate purpose;

• ownership jelas;

• definition tidak ambigu;

• evidence source valid;

• measurement tidak menciptakan authority baru;

• measurement tidak bertentangan dengan architecture.

Performance Management tidak otomatis memiliki authority untuk menetapkan  
semua measurement lintas organisasi.

⸻

26\. Phase 4 — Instrument

Measurement mechanism disiapkan.

Possible instrumentation:

• system-generated data;

• workflow record;

• governance record;

• compliance record;

• certification record;

• audit record;

• structured reporting;

• validated manual input;

• approved evidence source.

Instrument harus konsisten dengan measurement specification.

⸻

27\. Phase 5 — Collect

Data dikumpulkan berdasarkan:

• defined source;

• collection scope;

• collection method;

• measurement period;

• applicable frequency.

Collection harus mempertahankan provenance.

⸻

28\. Phase 6 — Validate

Data harus divalidasi sebelum digunakan sebagai formal measurement.

Validation dapat mencakup:

Completeness

Accuracy

Consistency

Validity

Timeliness

Traceability

Source Integrity

Duplicate Detection

Anomaly Detection

Jika validation gagal:

Validation Failure

&nbsp; ↓

Flag / Hold / Reject

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Status

Jangan memperlakukan invalid data sebagai reliable evidence.

⸻

29\. Phase 7 — Calculate

Validated data dapat diproses menjadi measurement result menggunakan formula  
atau rule yang telah ditetapkan.

Calculation harus:

• reproducible;

• version-aware;

• traceable;

• consistent.

Formula tidak boleh berubah secara silent.

⸻

30\. Phase 8 — Record

Measurement result harus direkam bersama metadata yang diperlukan.

Minimum:

Metric ID

Measurement Period

Result

Unit

Source

Method Version

Validation Status

Timestamp

Evidence Reference

Owner

Historical measurement harus dapat ditelusuri.

⸻

31\. Phase 9 — Report

Measurement result dapat diteruskan kepada reporting architecture.

Namun:

Measurement

&nbsp; ≠

Reporting

E3 menghasilkan measurement result.

E7 menentukan bagaimana information tersebut dikomunikasikan.

⸻

32\. Phase 10 — Review

Measurement result dapat digunakan dalam Performance Review.

Namun:

Measurement Result

&nbsp; ≠

Performance Evaluation

Review melakukan interpretation terhadap result.

E3 tidak mengambil alih review authority.

⸻

33\. Phase 11 — Maintain

Measurement harus dipelihara sepanjang lifecycle.

Maintenance dapat mencakup:

• source update;

• method update;

• definition clarification;

• quality correction;

• version update;

• evidence-source replacement.

Material changes harus controlled.

⸻

34\. Phase 12 — Retire / Replace

Measurement dapat:

• retired;

• replaced;

• superseded;

• merged;

• deprecated.

Retirement harus menjaga historical integrity.

Historical measurement tidak boleh dihapus hanya karena metric sudah retired.

⸻

35\. Measurement Quality

Measurement quality harus dievaluasi sebelum result digunakan untuk material  
performance assessment.

Minimum dimensions:

Accuracy

Completeness

Consistency

Timeliness

Validity

Traceability

Quality issue harus tercatat.

⸻

36\. Measurement Confidence

Measurement result dapat memiliki confidence classification:

High Confidence

Medium Confidence

Low Confidence

Insufficient Evidence

Confidence harus mempertimbangkan:

• data quality;

• evidence completeness;

• source reliability;

• calculation stability;

• known limitations.

Confidence menjelaskan quality of measurement basis.

Confidence bukan performance score.

⸻

37\. Measurement Status

Measurement dapat memiliki status:

Measured

Validated

Pending Validation

Not Measured

Measurement Gap

Measurement Pending

Insufficient Evidence

Invalid

Superseded

Retired

Status harus dipisahkan dari performance conclusion.

⸻

38\. Measurement Gap

Measurement gap terjadi ketika kondisi yang seharusnya diketahui belum dapat  
diukur secara reliable.

Canonical rule:

No Measurement

&nbsp; ≠

Performance Failure

Jika measurement belum tersedia:

Measurement Gap

harus digunakan daripada fabricated score.

⸻

39\. Insufficient Evidence

Jika evidence tidak cukup:

Insufficient Evidence

harus menjadi valid measurement status.

Tidak diperbolehkan:

No Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Assume Failure

atau:

No Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Assume Success

⸻

40\. Measurement and Performance Dimensions

Setiap formal measurement harus memiliki relationship ke E2.

Canonical mapping:

Mapping domain-specific harus ditentukan berdasarkan actual measurement  
purpose.

⸻

41\. Measurement vs Evaluation

Canonical separation:

Measurement

"What happened / what is observed?"

&nbsp; ↓

Evaluation

"What does it mean?"

Measurement menghasilkan:

Result

Evidence

Confidence

Quality

Evaluation menghasilkan:

Assessment

Gap

Trend

Finding

Insight

E3 tidak boleh menyatakan evaluation conclusion hanya karena measurement  
result tersedia.

⸻

42\. Measurement vs Target

Measurement:

Actual Condition

Target:

Expected Condition

Relationship:

Actual

&nbsp; +

Expected

&nbsp; ↓

Evaluation

E3 tidak menciptakan target secara sepihak.

Target yang domain-specific harus berasal dari applicable objective, governance,  
or performance framework.

⸻

43\. Measurement vs KPI

Canonical separation:

Metric

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Result

KPI

&nbsp; ↓

Selected Metric / Indicator

&nbsp; ↓

Formal Performance Signal

Tidak semua measurement menjadi KPI.

Tidak semua KPI harus menjadi satu-satunya performance representation.

⸻

44\. Measurement Anti-Gaming Controls

Measurement Architecture harus melindungi integrity metric.

Minimum controls:

0\. metric definition dikunci;

0\. formula terdokumentasi;

0\. source dapat ditelusuri;

0\. historical result tidak diubah tanpa controlled revision;

0\. metric owner dan data owner dipisahkan apabila diperlukan;

0\. material anomaly dapat ditinjau;

0\. metric change memiliki rationale;

0\. KPI tidak boleh menjadi satu-satunya representation of performance;

0\. measurement limitation harus visible;

0\. correction harus memiliki audit trail.

⸻

45\. Historical Integrity

Measurement history harus mempertahankan:

Original Result

Correction

Reason

Timestamp

Approver

Method Version

Evidence Reference

Historical result tidak boleh diubah secara silent.

Jika correction diperlukan:

Original

&nbsp; ↓

Controlled Correction

&nbsp; ↓

Corrected Version

⸻

46\. Measurement Versioning

Material measurement definition harus memiliki version.

Version change dapat terjadi karena:

• definition change;

• formula change;

• source change;

• method change;

• scope change;

• unit change;

• validation rule change.

Material version change harus mempertahankan lineage antara old dan new  
measurement definition.

⸻

47\. Measurement Ownership

Performance Management memiliki ownership atas:

performance measurement capability

Domain owner tetap memiliki ownership atas:

• domain;

• operational evidence;

• source systems;

• domain execution;

• domain result.

Canonical boundary:

Performance Management

&nbsp; ↓

Owns Measurement Capability

Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Owns Domain Evidence

Measurement ownership tidak menjadi domain ownership.

⸻

48\. Measurement Accountability

Measurement accountability harus dapat ditelusuri.

Minimum roles:

Measurement Owner

Data / Evidence Owner

Measurement Operator

Validator

Approver

Tidak semua measurement membutuhkan seluruh role secara terpisah.

Role separation harus diterapkan apabila materiality atau independence  
membutuhkannya.

⸻

49\. Measurement Governance

Measurement governance memastikan measurement tetap berada dalam approved  
authority architecture.

Canonical model:

Governance

&nbsp; ↓

Approves applicable framework / material definitions

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Management

&nbsp; ↓

Maintains Measurement Architecture

&nbsp; ↓

Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Produces / Provides Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Result

Performance Management tidak menciptakan governance authority melalui  
measurement.

⸻

50\. Measurement Change Control

Perubahan material terhadap:

• measurement architecture;

• metric definition;

• calculation method;

• measurement scope;

• evidence source;

• formal KPI measurement;

• measurement governance;

harus melalui applicable change-control mechanism.

Tidak diperbolehkan melakukan silent mutation terhadap canonical measurement  
definition.

⸻

51\. Measurement Quality Failure

Jika measurement quality tidak memadai:

Measurement Quality Failure

&nbsp; ↓

Confidence Reduction

&nbsp; ↓

Possible Measurement Gap

&nbsp; ↓

Review / Remediation

Measurement failure tidak boleh langsung diklasifikasikan sebagai:

Execution Failure

Root-cause classification tetap berada pada Performance Review.

⸻

52\. PD-03 Governance Measurement Examples

Contoh reference pattern:

Governance Decision Compliance

Decision Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Governance Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Observed Decision Record

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Dimension

Policy Adherence

Governance Policy

&nbsp; ↓

Expected Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Assessment

Certification Readiness

Certification Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Certification Performance

Compliance Condition

Compliance Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Compliance Performance

Contoh di atas adalah reference patterns, bukan frozen PD-03 KPI catalogue.

⸻

53\. Policy Measurement Principle

Policy measurement harus mengukur:

• applicability;

• adoption;

• adherence;

• effectiveness;

• exception condition;

• governance impact;

apabila applicable.

Namun policy measurement tidak boleh berubah menjadi technical execution  
measurement tanpa domain ownership yang sah.

⸻

54\. Control Measurement Principle

Control measurement dapat melihat:

• control coverage;

• control effectiveness;

• control operation;

• control exception;

• control evidence;

• control remediation.

Namun:

Control Measurement

&nbsp; ≠

Control Execution Ownership

⸻

55\. Certification Measurement Principle

Certification measurement dapat melihat:

• certification readiness;

• evidence completeness;

• certification status;

• material certification findings;

• certification remediation progress.

Certification measurement tidak memberikan PD-03 ownership atas implementation  
domain yang dinilai.

⸻

56\. Compliance Measurement Principle

Compliance measurement dapat melihat:

• compliance status;

• requirement coverage;

• exception;

• evidence completeness;

• material finding;

• remediation status.

Compliance measurement harus mempertahankan applicable owner.

⸻

57\. Cross-Platform Measurement

PD-03 dapat membutuhkan evidence dari Platform lain.

Canonical relationship:

PD-03 Measurement Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Cross-Platform Evidence Request

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Platform Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Measurement

Evidence dependency tidak mengubah ownership.

Dependency

&nbsp; ≠

Ownership

⸻

58\. Measurement Dependency

Jika measurement PD-03 bergantung pada source milik Platform lain:

• source owner tetap memiliki source;

• PD-03 memiliki measurement use;

• evidence provenance harus dipertahankan;

• dependency harus dapat ditelusuri;

• material failure harus dapat dieskalasikan.

⸻

59\. Platform Autonomy

Measurement tidak boleh digunakan untuk membangun hidden hierarchy.

PD-03 Measurement Authority

&nbsp; ≠

PD-03 Authority Over Other Platforms

Cross-platform measurement hanya memberikan evidence untuk legitimate  
performance purpose.

⸻

60\. Measurement and Performance Health

E3 menghasilkan measurement result.

E2/E5 kemudian dapat menggunakannya untuk health assessment.

Canonical chain:

Measurement Results

&nbsp; ↓

Dimension Results

&nbsp; ↓

Cross-Dimension Analysis

&nbsp; ↓

Context

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Health

E3 tidak menghasilkan final Performance Health conclusion.

⸻

61\. Measurement and Performance Trend

Measurement menyediakan time-series evidence.

Measurement T1

Measurement T2

Measurement T3

&nbsp; ↓

Trend Analysis

Trend classification dilakukan oleh evaluation/review layer.

E3 memastikan measurement history cukup reliable untuk trend analysis.

⸻

62\. Measurement and Improvement

Measurement menyediakan baseline dan re-measurement.

Canonical improvement loop:

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Action

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measurement

&nbsp; ↓

New Measurement Result

&nbsp; ↓

Effectiveness Evaluation

Completion action bukan measurement of effectiveness.

⸻

63\. Measurement and E4

E4 menggunakan E3 sebagai measurement foundation.

E3

Metric / Measurement Discipline

&nbsp; ↓

E4

KPI & Success Metrics

E4 menentukan KPI selection dan success metric relationship.

E3 menentukan measurement integrity.

⸻

64\. Measurement and E5

E5 menggunakan measurement result sebagai input review.

E3

Measurement Result

&nbsp; ↓

E5

Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment / Finding

E3 tidak melakukan review decision.

⸻

65\. Measurement and E6

E6 mengatur governance atas performance management.

E3

Measurement Mechanism

&nbsp; ↓

E6

Measurement Governance

E3 tidak menciptakan authority baru.

⸻

66\. Measurement and E7

E7 menggunakan validated measurement results sebagai reporting input.

E3

Validated Result

&nbsp; ↓

E7

Reporting

Reporting tidak mengubah source measurement.

⸻

67\. Measurement and E8

E8 menggunakan measurement untuk:

• baseline;

• gap identification;

• re-measurement;

• improvement effectiveness.

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measurement

⸻

68\. Measurement and E9

E9 menilai maturity Performance Architecture.

E3 menyediakan evidence untuk measurement maturity.

Namun:

Measurement Maturity

&nbsp; ≠

Performance Result

A mature measurement system dapat tetap menghasilkan poor performance.

⸻

69\. Measurement and E10

E10 menilai success Performance Architecture secara integrated.

E3 menyediakan salah satu prerequisite:

Measurement Integrity

&nbsp; ↓

Trustworthy Performance Conclusion

&nbsp; ↓

E10 Success Assessment

Poor measurement quality dapat membatasi confidence pada E10.

⸻

70\. Measurement Confidence and Success

E10 tidak boleh mengklaim trustworthy success jika measurement foundation  
tidak reliable.

Canonical relationship:

Measurement Quality

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence Confidence

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Confidence

&nbsp; ↓

Success Confidence

Confidence propagation harus transparan.

⸻

71\. Reference Implementation Pattern

E3 menjadi Reference Performance Measurement Architecture untuk Platform  
lain.

Yang diwariskan:

• measurement hierarchy;

• measurement principles;

• metric definition discipline;

• KPI/metric separation;

• leading/lagging distinction;

• measurement lifecycle;

• evidence traceability;

• quality model;

• confidence model;

• anti-gaming controls;

• versioning;

• change control.

Yang TIDAK diwariskan otomatis:

• metric catalogue;

• KPI;

• target;

• threshold;

• frequency;

• formula;

• evidence source;

• owner;

• domain-specific measurement method.

Semua elemen tersebut harus ditentukan sesuai domain Platform masing-masing.

⸻

72\. Non-Goals

E3 tidak bertujuan untuk:

• menetapkan strategic objective;

• menetapkan strategic direction;

• menetapkan governance authority;

• menentukan operational execution;

• menentukan organizational ownership;

• mengambil alih domain evidence ownership;

• menetapkan universal KPI;

• menetapkan universal target;

• menetapkan universal threshold;

• menetapkan performance conclusion;

• menggantikan Performance Review;

• menggantikan Governance;

• menggantikan Operation.

⸻

73\. Measurement Integrity Principles

E3 membekukan prinsip berikut:

Measurement must be:

1\. Purposeful

2\. Defined

3\. Traceable

4\. Validated

5\. Reproducible

6\. Versioned

7\. Quality-assessed

8\. Evidence-backed

9\. Governed

10\. Change-controlled

⸻

74\. Success Criteria

E3 dianggap berhasil apabila:

0\. setiap material measurement memiliki objective, dimension, criterion,  
requirement, atau legitimate measurement purpose;

0\. metric memiliki definition yang dapat dipahami dan direproduksi;

0\. measurement method dapat dijelaskan;

0\. source/evidence dapat ditelusuri;

0\. measurement result memiliki measurement period;

0\. measurement quality dapat dinilai;

0\. confidence dapat dinyatakan;

0\. leading dan lagging indicators dapat dibedakan;

0\. KPI dapat dibedakan dari metric;

0\. measurement lifecycle lengkap dari identification sampai retirement;

0\. historical measurement memiliki integrity;

0\. material measurement changes memiliki controlled revision;

0\. measurement gap dapat dinyatakan tanpa fabricated score;

0\. insufficient evidence dapat menghentikan conclusion;

0\. measurement tidak mengambil alih performance evaluation;

0\. measurement tidak menciptakan governance authority;

0\. measurement tidak memindahkan domain ownership;

0\. cross-platform measurement dependency mempertahankan ownership;

0\. Policy measurement mempertahankan governance boundary;

0\. Control measurement tidak menjadi execution ownership;

0\. Certification measurement tidak menjadi implementation ownership;

0\. Compliance measurement mempertahankan applicable ownership;

0\. measurement dapat digunakan untuk trend analysis;

0\. measurement dapat digunakan untuk improvement re-measurement;

0\. measurement quality dapat digunakan sebagai confidence input;

0\. E3 menyediakan stable foundation bagi E4–E10;

0\. framework dapat diwariskan ke Platform lain tanpa memaksakan metric  
domain PD-03.

⸻

75\. Source-Fidelity Qualification

SOURCE-SUPPORTED

Baseline mendukung:

• E3 sebagai Performance Measurement Model;

• E3 sebagai mekanisme pengukuran performance;

• measurement hierarchy;

• metric/indicator/KPI separation;

• leading/lagging distinction;

• measurement lifecycle;

• measurement specification;

• measurement quality;

• measurement confidence;

• evidence traceability;

• measurement governance;

• anti-gaming controls;

• measurement change control;

• distinction measurement vs evaluation;

• measurement gap handling.

PD-03-SPECIFIC SOURCE-SUPPORTED

PD-03-specific source mendukung:

• Governance & Compliance sebagai domain;

• Governance Policy responsibility;

• Governance Control responsibility;

• Certification responsibility;

• Compliance responsibility;

• Governance Standards relationship;

• Governance Baseline relationship;

• cross-platform ownership preservation;

• dependency ≠ ownership;

• certification ≠ implementation;

• control ≠ execution.

BOUNDED CANONICAL SYNTHESIS

Adaptasi PD-03 yang dibekukan dalam E3 meliputi:

• Governance Policy measurement;

• Governance Control measurement;

• Certification measurement;

• Compliance measurement;

• Governance Standards measurement;

• Governance Baseline measurement;

• cross-platform evidence dependency;

• PD-03 measurement interpretation.

Adaptasi tersebut tidak memperluas organizational ownership PD-03.

SOURCE GAP

Belum ditemukan source authoritative yang menetapkan secara eksplisit:

• final PD-03 metric catalogue;

• final PD-03 KPI catalogue;

• exact KPI formula;

• exact measurement frequency;

• exact target;

• exact threshold;

• exact weighting;

• exact PD-03 evidence repository;

• exact domain-specific measurement owner per metric;

• exact PD-03 measurement dashboard schema.

Elemen tersebut sengaja tidak diinvensikan dalam E3.

⸻

76\. Claude Code Reconciliation Directive

Claude Code MUST melakukan source reconciliation sebelum implementation-level  
PD-03 Performance Measurement dianggap complete.

Search harus mencakup:

0\. exact PD-03 E3 definition;

0\. exact PD-03 metric hierarchy;

0\. exact PD-03 measurement requirements;

0\. exact PD-03 measurement specification;

0\. exact Policy measurements;

0\. exact Control measurements;

0\. exact Certification measurements;

0\. exact Compliance measurements;

0\. exact Governance Standards measurements;

0\. exact Governance Baseline measurements;

0\. exact metric catalogue;

0\. exact KPI catalogue;

0\. exact evidence sources;

0\. exact measurement owners;

0\. exact data owners;

0\. exact measurement frequency;

0\. exact calculation formula;

0\. exact threshold;

0\. exact target;

0\. exact confidence model;

0\. exact measurement quality model;

0\. exact measurement lifecycle implementation;

0\. exact measurement change-control mechanism.

Setiap finding harus diklasifikasikan:

SOURCE-SUPPORTED

REFERENCE-PATTERN

SOURCE-GAP

Claude Code MUST NOT:

• mengarang KPI;

• mengarang metric;

• mengarang target;

• mengarang threshold;

• mengarang weighting;

• mengarang formula;

• mengarang evidence source;

• mengarang measurement owner;

• mengubah measurement result menjadi performance conclusion;

• mengambil authority dari Governance;

• mengambil execution ownership;

• mengambil domain ownership Platform lain;

• menyamakan Control dengan Execution;

• menyamakan Certification dengan Implementation.

Jika source authoritative tidak ditemukan:

Pertahankan E3 sebagai Bounded Canonical Synthesis dan carry forward  
SOURCE GAP untuk reconciliation berikutnya.

79\. Canonical Freeze Statement

E3 — Performance Measurement establishes the controlled measurement architecture through which PD-03 Governance & Compliance converts observable governance, compliance, control, certification, capability, operational, strategic, and improvement conditions into traceable measurement results.

E3 preserves the constitutional distinction between measurement and evaluation. Measurement produces evidence and result; evaluation determines meaning. Measurement does not create strategic authority, governance authority, operational authority, or domain ownership.

PD-03-specific measurement remains bounded by Governance & Compliance responsibility over Governance Policy, Governance Control, Certification, Compliance, Governance Standards, and Governance Baseline relationships. Control measurement does not become execution ownership; certification measurement does not become implementation ownership; cross-platform evidence dependency does not become cross-platform ownership.

Measurement quality, confidence, provenance, historical integrity, versioning, lifecycle control, anti-gaming protection, and controlled change are mandatory characteristics of formal performance measurement.

Absence of measurement is not automatically performance failure. Insufficient evidence must remain an explicit state rather than being converted into a fabricated performance score.

Domain-specific KPI, metric catalogue, target, threshold, weighting, frequency, formula, evidence source, and measurement ownership remain SOURCE GAP until verified from authoritative PD-03 sources.

E3 is therefore FROZEN as the Performance Measurement Architecture for Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

⸻

Final Verdict

E3 — Performance Measurement: FROZEN — SOURCE-BOUNDED

\# Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance

\# E3 — Performance Measurement

\> \*\*Section ID:\*\* E3

\> \*\*Status:\*\* FROZEN

\> \*\*Freeze Decision:\*\* APPROVED — SOURCE-BOUNDED

**1\. Purpose**

E3 menetapkan bagaimana performance PD-03 diukur secara evidence-based,  
traceable, repeatable, dan controlled.

E3 menerjemahkan:

E1 — Performance Constitution

&nbsp; ↓

E2 — Performance Model

&nbsp; ↓

E3 — Performance Measurement

E3 menjawab:

"Bagaimana kondisi performance PD-03 diubah menjadi measurement result yang dapat dipercaya dan ditelusuri?"

E3 tidak menjawab:

- apakah performance tersebut baik atau buruk;
- apakah objective berhasil;
- apakah governance decision harus diubah;
- apakah operational owner harus melakukan tindakan tertentu.

Pertanyaan tersebut berada pada layer evaluation, review, governance, dan  
improvement yang sesuai.

⸻

**2\. Constitutional Position**

Canonical boundary:

E2 — Performance Model

&nbsp; ↓

What should be understood?

&nbsp; ↓

E3 — Performance Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

How is it measured?

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Result

&nbsp; ↓

E5 / Performance Evaluation

&nbsp; ↓

What does the result mean?

Measurement result adalah input evidence untuk evaluation.

Measurement result bukan performance conclusion.

⸻

**3\. Measurement Philosophy**

Measurement harus mengubah kondisi nyata menjadi evidence yang:

- observable;
- definable;
- reproducible;
- comparable;
- traceable;
- quality-assessed;
- version-controlled.

Canonical chain:

Real Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Definition

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Method

&nbsp; ↓

Validated Data

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Result

&nbsp; ↓

Traceability

Measurement bukan sekadar pengumpulan angka.

Measurement harus mempertahankan hubungan antara:

What

Why

How

Source

When

Owner

Result

Evidence

⸻

**4\. PD-03 Measurement Context**

Performance measurement PD-03 harus mencerminkan domain:

Governance & Compliance

dengan primary responsibilities:

Policy

Control

Certification

Compliance

Measurement harus digunakan untuk memahami kondisi performance dalam scope  
tersebut tanpa memperluas ownership PD-03 ke domain Platform lain.

⸻

**5\. PD-03 Measurement Boundary**

Measurement PD-03 dapat mencakup kondisi yang berkaitan dengan:

- Governance Policy;
- Governance Standards;
- Governance Control;
- Certification;
- Compliance;
- governance effectiveness;
- governance decision effectiveness;
- governance operating effectiveness;
- governance capability;
- governance improvement.

Measurement tidak otomatis menciptakan ownership atas execution domain.

⸻

**6\. Governance Policy Measurement Boundary**

PD-03 dapat mengukur performance yang berkaitan dengan Governance Policy.

Canonical boundary:

Governance Policy

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03

&nbsp; ↓

Policy Performance Measurement

Namun:

Technical / Domain Policy

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Domain Owner

PD-03 tidak menjadi primary owner hanya karena suatu policy memiliki  
governance implication.

⸻

**7\. Control Measurement Boundary**

PD-03 dapat mengukur Governance Control performance.

Namun:

Governance Control

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Governance Scope

Control Implementation

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Domain Owner

Control measurement tidak memberikan PD-03 authority untuk menjalankan  
technical control implementation milik Platform lain.

⸻

**8\. Certification Measurement Boundary**

Certification-related performance dapat diukur dalam scope Governance &  
Compliance.

Canonical model:

Certification Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Certification Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Result

Tetapi:

Certification

&nbsp; ≠

Implementation Ownership

Platform yang menjadi subject certification tetap mempertahankan execution  
ownership.

⸻

**9\. Compliance Measurement Boundary**

Compliance measurement harus mempertahankan applicable ownership.

Compliance Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Compliance Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Performance Interpretation

PD-03 dapat mengukur compliance condition dalam scope governance tanpa  
mengambil alih execution responsibility.

⸻

**10\. Governance Standards Measurement**

Governance Standards dapat menjadi measurement reference apabila applicable.

Contoh:

Governance Standard

&nbsp; ↓

Expected Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Observed Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Variance

Namun:

Governance Standards

&nbsp; ≠

All Technical Standards

Technical implementation standards tetap berada pada applicable domain.

⸻

**11\. Governance Baseline Relationship**

Governance Baseline dapat menjadi reference condition untuk measurement.

Governance Baseline

&nbsp; ↓

Expected Governance Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Result

Measurement tidak mengubah Governance Baseline.

Jika measurement menunjukkan gap terhadap baseline:

Measurement Result

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Evaluation

&nbsp; ↓

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Governance Review

⸻

**12\. Performance Dimensions**

Setiap measurement harus dapat dipetakan ke Performance Model E2.

Canonical dimensions:

Strategic

Operational

Governance

Capability

Improvement

Measurement harus memiliki primary dimension apabila satu measurement  
berhubungan dengan lebih dari satu dimension.

Contoh:

Governance Decision Compliance

&nbsp; ↓

Primary: Governance

Governance Process Timeliness

&nbsp; ↓

Primary: Operational

Governance Capability Readiness

&nbsp; ↓

Primary: Capability

Improvement Effectiveness

&nbsp; ↓

Primary: Improvement

⸻

**13\. Cross-Dimension Measurement**

Satu measurement dapat memberikan evidence kepada lebih dari satu dimension.

Namun penggunaan lintas-dimensi harus eksplisit.

Canonical rule:

One Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Primary Dimension

&nbsp; +

Secondary Context

Tujuannya mencegah:

- double counting;
- duplicated findings;
- distorted integrated assessment.

⸻

**14\. Measurement Hierarchy**

Canonical hierarchy:

Performance Objective

&nbsp; ↓

Success Criterion

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Metric

&nbsp; ↓

Indicator

&nbsp; ↓

KPI

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Result

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Evaluation

Tidak semua performance condition membutuhkan KPI.

Tidak semua metric harus menjadi KPI.

Tidak semua indicator harus menjadi executive metric.

⸻

**15\. Metric**

Metric adalah definisi terukur yang digunakan untuk mengamati kondisi tertentu.

Minimum metric definition:

Metric ID

Metric Name

Purpose

Definition

Dimension

Unit

Source

Method

Frequency

Owner

Evidence Reference

Version

Metric harus memiliki definisi yang dapat dipahami dan direproduksi.

⸻

**16\. Indicator**

Indicator menggunakan satu atau lebih measurement result untuk menunjukkan  
kondisi atau arah tertentu.

Indicator dapat digunakan sebagai:

- leading indicator;
- lagging indicator;
- supporting indicator;
- diagnostic indicator.

Indicator tidak otomatis menjadi KPI.

⸻

**17\. KPI**

KPI adalah metric/indicator yang secara formal dipilih sebagai representasi  
penting terhadap keberhasilan atau kondisi performance.

Canonical relationship:

Metric

&nbsp; ↓

Candidate Indicator

&nbsp; ↓

KPI Selection

&nbsp; ↓

Formal KPI

E3 tidak menentukan KPI catalogue PD-03.

KPI selection menjadi concern E4.

⸻

**18\. Leading Indicator**

Leading indicator memberikan sinyal terhadap kondisi yang dapat  
mendahului outcome.

Contoh generic:

Governance Review Completion

&nbsp; ↓

Potential Signal

&nbsp; ↓

Future Governance Outcome

Leading indicator tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai proof of final success.

⸻

**19\. Lagging Indicator**

Lagging indicator mengukur kondisi atau outcome yang telah terjadi.

Contoh:

Compliance Result

&nbsp; ↓

Observed Outcome

&nbsp; ↓

Lagging Indicator

Leading dan lagging indicators harus dapat dibedakan secara eksplisit.

⸻

**20\. Measurement Requirement**

Measurement Requirement muncul ketika organisasi perlu mengetahui kondisi  
tertentu.

Trigger dapat berasal dari:

- strategic objective;
- success criterion;
- performance dimension;
- governance requirement;
- operational requirement;
- capability requirement;
- improvement requirement;
- compliance requirement;
- certification requirement;
- control requirement.

Output:

Measurement Requirement

⸻

**21\. Measurement Specification**

Setiap formal measurement harus memiliki specification.

Minimum:

What is measured?

Why is it measured?

How is it measured?

Who owns it?

What is the source?

What is the unit?

What is the frequency?

What is the validity rule?

What evidence supports it?

Output:

Measurement Specification

⸻

**22\. Measurement Lifecycle**

Canonical lifecycle:

1\. Identify

&nbsp; ↓

2\. Define

&nbsp; ↓

3\. Approve

&nbsp; ↓

4\. Instrument

&nbsp; ↓

5\. Collect

&nbsp; ↓

6\. Validate

&nbsp; ↓

7\. Calculate

&nbsp; ↓

8\. Record

&nbsp; ↓

9\. Report

&nbsp; ↓

10\. Review

&nbsp; ↓

11\. Maintain

&nbsp; ↓

12\. Retire / Replace

Lifecycle ini berlaku sebagai reference pattern.

Domain-specific implementation dapat menyesuaikan detailnya tanpa mengubah  
measurement principles.

⸻

**23\. Phase 1 — Identify**

Identifikasi measurement dilakukan ketika terdapat legitimate need untuk  
mengetahui suatu condition.

Output:

Measurement Requirement

Measurement tidak boleh dibuat hanya karena data tersedia.

⸻

**24\. Phase 2 — Define**

Measurement definition harus ditetapkan sebelum formal collection.

Minimum:

- metric;
- purpose;
- dimension;
- unit;
- source;
- method;
- frequency;
- ownership;
- validity;
- evidence.

Output:

Measurement Specification

⸻

**25\. Phase 3 — Approve**

Formal measurement mechanism harus memperoleh approval sesuai authority  
yang berlaku.

Approval memastikan:

- legitimate purpose;
- ownership jelas;
- definition tidak ambigu;
- evidence source valid;
- measurement tidak menciptakan authority baru;
- measurement tidak bertentangan dengan architecture.

Performance Management tidak otomatis memiliki authority untuk menetapkan  
semua measurement lintas organisasi.

⸻

**26\. Phase 4 — Instrument**

Measurement mechanism disiapkan.

Possible instrumentation:

- system-generated data;
- workflow record;
- governance record;
- compliance record;
- certification record;
- audit record;
- structured reporting;
- validated manual input;
- approved evidence source.

Instrument harus konsisten dengan measurement specification.

⸻

**27\. Phase 5 — Collect**

Data dikumpulkan berdasarkan:

- defined source;
- collection scope;
- collection method;
- measurement period;
- applicable frequency.

Collection harus mempertahankan provenance.

⸻

**28\. Phase 6 — Validate**

Data harus divalidasi sebelum digunakan sebagai formal measurement.

Validation dapat mencakup:

Completeness

Accuracy

Consistency

Validity

Timeliness

Traceability

Source Integrity

Duplicate Detection

Anomaly Detection

Jika validation gagal:

Validation Failure

&nbsp; ↓

Flag / Hold / Reject

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Status

Jangan memperlakukan invalid data sebagai reliable evidence.

⸻

**29\. Phase 7 — Calculate**

Validated data dapat diproses menjadi measurement result menggunakan formula  
atau rule yang telah ditetapkan.

Calculation harus:

- reproducible;
- version-aware;
- traceable;
- consistent.

Formula tidak boleh berubah secara silent.

⸻

**30\. Phase 8 — Record**

Measurement result harus direkam bersama metadata yang diperlukan.

Minimum:

Metric ID

Measurement Period

Result

Unit

Source

Method Version

Validation Status

Timestamp

Evidence Reference

Owner

Historical measurement harus dapat ditelusuri.

⸻

**31\. Phase 9 — Report**

Measurement result dapat diteruskan kepada reporting architecture.

Namun:

Measurement

&nbsp; ≠

Reporting

E3 menghasilkan measurement result.

E7 menentukan bagaimana information tersebut dikomunikasikan.

⸻

**32\. Phase 10 — Review**

Measurement result dapat digunakan dalam Performance Review.

Namun:

Measurement Result

&nbsp; ≠

Performance Evaluation

Review melakukan interpretation terhadap result.

E3 tidak mengambil alih review authority.

⸻

**33\. Phase 11 — Maintain**

Measurement harus dipelihara sepanjang lifecycle.

Maintenance dapat mencakup:

- source update;
- method update;
- definition clarification;
- quality correction;
- version update;
- evidence-source replacement.

Material changes harus controlled.

⸻

**34\. Phase 12 — Retire / Replace**

Measurement dapat:

- retired;
- replaced;
- superseded;
- merged;
- deprecated.

Retirement harus menjaga historical integrity.

Historical measurement tidak boleh dihapus hanya karena metric sudah retired.

⸻

**35\. Measurement Quality**

Measurement quality harus dievaluasi sebelum result digunakan untuk material  
performance assessment.

Minimum dimensions:

Accuracy

Completeness

Consistency

Timeliness

Validity

Traceability

Quality issue harus tercatat.

⸻

**36\. Measurement Confidence**

Measurement result dapat memiliki confidence classification:

High Confidence

Medium Confidence

Low Confidence

Insufficient Evidence

Confidence harus mempertimbangkan:

- data quality;
- evidence completeness;
- source reliability;
- calculation stability;
- known limitations.

Confidence menjelaskan quality of measurement basis.

Confidence bukan performance score.

⸻

**37\. Measurement Status**

Measurement dapat memiliki status:

Measured

Validated

Pending Validation

Not Measured

Measurement Gap

Measurement Pending

Insufficient Evidence

Invalid

Superseded

Retired

Status harus dipisahkan dari performance conclusion.

⸻

**38\. Measurement Gap**

Measurement gap terjadi ketika kondisi yang seharusnya diketahui belum dapat  
diukur secara reliable.

Canonical rule:

No Measurement

&nbsp; ≠

Performance Failure

Jika measurement belum tersedia:

Measurement Gap

harus digunakan daripada fabricated score.

⸻

**39\. Insufficient Evidence**

Jika evidence tidak cukup:

Insufficient Evidence

harus menjadi valid measurement status.

Tidak diperbolehkan:

No Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Assume Failure

atau:

No Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Assume Success

⸻

**40\. Measurement and Performance Dimensions**

Setiap formal measurement harus memiliki relationship ke E2.

Canonical mapping:

| **Measurement Purpose**            | **Primary Dimension**    |
| ---------------------------------- | ------------------------ |
| Strategic Objective Achievement    | Strategic                |
| Governance Operating Effectiveness | Operational / Governance |
| Governance Decision Compliance     | Governance               |
| Capability Readiness               | Capability               |
| Improvement Effectiveness          | Improvement              |

Mapping domain-specific harus ditentukan berdasarkan actual measurement  
purpose.

⸻

**41\. Measurement vs Evaluation**

Canonical separation:

Measurement

"What happened / what is observed?"

&nbsp; ↓

Evaluation

"What does it mean?"

Measurement menghasilkan:

Result

Evidence

Confidence

Quality

Evaluation menghasilkan:

Assessment

Gap

Trend

Finding

Insight

E3 tidak boleh menyatakan evaluation conclusion hanya karena measurement  
result tersedia.

⸻

**42\. Measurement vs Target**

Measurement:

Actual Condition

Target:

Expected Condition

Relationship:

Actual

&nbsp; +

Expected

&nbsp; ↓

Evaluation

E3 tidak menciptakan target secara sepihak.

Target yang domain-specific harus berasal dari applicable objective, governance,  
or performance framework.

⸻

**43\. Measurement vs KPI**

Canonical separation:

Metric

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Result

KPI

&nbsp; ↓

Selected Metric / Indicator

&nbsp; ↓

Formal Performance Signal

Tidak semua measurement menjadi KPI.

Tidak semua KPI harus menjadi satu-satunya performance representation.

⸻

**44\. Measurement Anti-Gaming Controls**

Measurement Architecture harus melindungi integrity metric.

Minimum controls:

1. metric definition dikunci;
2. formula terdokumentasi;
3. source dapat ditelusuri;
4. historical result tidak diubah tanpa controlled revision;
5. metric owner dan data owner dipisahkan apabila diperlukan;
6. material anomaly dapat ditinjau;
7. metric change memiliki rationale;
8. KPI tidak boleh menjadi satu-satunya representation of performance;
9. measurement limitation harus visible;
10. correction harus memiliki audit trail.

⸻

**45\. Historical Integrity**

Measurement history harus mempertahankan:

Original Result

Correction

Reason

Timestamp

Approver

Method Version

Evidence Reference

Historical result tidak boleh diubah secara silent.

Jika correction diperlukan:

Original

&nbsp; ↓

Controlled Correction

&nbsp; ↓

Corrected Version

⸻

**46\. Measurement Versioning**

Material measurement definition harus memiliki version.

Version change dapat terjadi karena:

- definition change;
- formula change;
- source change;
- method change;
- scope change;
- unit change;
- validation rule change.

Material version change harus mempertahankan lineage antara old dan new  
measurement definition.

⸻

**47\. Measurement Ownership**

Performance Management memiliki ownership atas:

performance measurement capability

Domain owner tetap memiliki ownership atas:

- domain;
- operational evidence;
- source systems;
- domain execution;
- domain result.

Canonical boundary:

Performance Management

&nbsp; ↓

Owns Measurement Capability

Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Owns Domain Evidence

Measurement ownership tidak menjadi domain ownership.

⸻

**48\. Measurement Accountability**

Measurement accountability harus dapat ditelusuri.

Minimum roles:

Measurement Owner

Data / Evidence Owner

Measurement Operator

Validator

Approver

Tidak semua measurement membutuhkan seluruh role secara terpisah.

Role separation harus diterapkan apabila materiality atau independence  
membutuhkannya.

⸻

**49\. Measurement Governance**

Measurement governance memastikan measurement tetap berada dalam approved  
authority architecture.

Canonical model:

Governance

&nbsp; ↓

Approves applicable framework / material definitions

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Management

&nbsp; ↓

Maintains Measurement Architecture

&nbsp; ↓

Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Produces / Provides Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Result

Performance Management tidak menciptakan governance authority melalui  
measurement.

⸻

**50\. Measurement Change Control**

Perubahan material terhadap:

- measurement architecture;
- metric definition;
- calculation method;
- measurement scope;
- evidence source;
- formal KPI measurement;
- measurement governance;

harus melalui applicable change-control mechanism.

Tidak diperbolehkan melakukan silent mutation terhadap canonical measurement  
definition.

⸻

**51\. Measurement Quality Failure**

Jika measurement quality tidak memadai:

Measurement Quality Failure

&nbsp; ↓

Confidence Reduction

&nbsp; ↓

Possible Measurement Gap

&nbsp; ↓

Review / Remediation

Measurement failure tidak boleh langsung diklasifikasikan sebagai:

Execution Failure

Root-cause classification tetap berada pada Performance Review.

⸻

**52\. PD-03 Governance Measurement Examples**

Contoh reference pattern:

Governance Decision Compliance

Decision Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

Governance Dimension

&nbsp; ↓

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Contoh di atas adalah reference patterns, bukan frozen PD-03 KPI catalogue.

⸻

**53\. Policy Measurement Principle**

Policy measurement harus mengukur:

- applicability;
- adoption;
- adherence;
- effectiveness;
- exception condition;
- governance impact;

apabila applicable.

Namun policy measurement tidak boleh berubah menjadi technical execution  
measurement tanpa domain ownership yang sah.

⸻

**54\. Control Measurement Principle**

Control measurement dapat melihat:

- control coverage;
- control effectiveness;
- control operation;
- control exception;
- control evidence;
- control remediation.

Namun:

Control Measurement

&nbsp; ≠

Control Execution Ownership

⸻

**55\. Certification Measurement Principle**

Certification measurement dapat melihat:

- certification readiness;
- evidence completeness;
- certification status;
- material certification findings;
- certification remediation progress.

Certification measurement tidak memberikan PD-03 ownership atas implementation  
domain yang dinilai.

⸻

**56\. Compliance Measurement Principle**

Compliance measurement dapat melihat:

- compliance status;
- requirement coverage;
- exception;
- evidence completeness;
- material finding;
- remediation status.

Compliance measurement harus mempertahankan applicable owner.

⸻

**57\. Cross-Platform Measurement**

PD-03 dapat membutuhkan evidence dari Platform lain.

Canonical relationship:

PD-03 Measurement Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Cross-Platform Evidence Request

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Platform Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Measurement

Evidence dependency tidak mengubah ownership.

Dependency

&nbsp; ≠

Ownership

⸻

**58\. Measurement Dependency**

Jika measurement PD-03 bergantung pada source milik Platform lain:

- source owner tetap memiliki source;
- PD-03 memiliki measurement use;
- evidence provenance harus dipertahankan;
- dependency harus dapat ditelusuri;
- material failure harus dapat dieskalasikan.

⸻

**59\. Platform Autonomy**

Measurement tidak boleh digunakan untuk membangun hidden hierarchy.

PD-03 Measurement Authority

&nbsp; ≠

PD-03 Authority Over Other Platforms

Cross-platform measurement hanya memberikan evidence untuk legitimate  
performance purpose.

⸻

**60\. Measurement and Performance Health**

&nbsp; ↓

⸻

**71\. Reference Implementation Pattern**

E3 menjadi Reference Performance Measurement Architecture untuk Platform  
lain.

Yang diwariskan:

- measurement hierarchy;
- measurement principles;
- metric definition discipline;
- KPI/metric separation;
- leading/lagging distinction;
- measurement lifecycle;
- evidence traceability;
- quality model;
- confidence model;
- anti-gaming controls;
- versioning;
- change control.

Yang TIDAK diwariskan otomatis:

- metric catalogue;
- KPI;
- target;
- threshold;
- frequency;
- formula;
- evidence source;
- owner;
- domain-specific measurement method.

Semua elemen tersebut harus ditentukan sesuai domain Platform masing-masing.

⸻

**72\. Non-Goals**

E3 tidak bertujuan untuk:

- menetapkan strategic objective;
- menetapkan strategic direction;
- menetapkan governance authority;
- menentukan operational execution;
- menentukan organizational ownership;
- mengambil alih domain evidence ownership;
- menetapkan universal KPI;
- menetapkan universal target;
- menetapkan universal threshold;
- menetapkan performance conclusion;
- menggantikan Performance Review;
- menggantikan Governance;
- menggantikan Operation.

⸻

**73\. Measurement Integrity Principles**

E3 membekukan prinsip berikut:

Measurement must be:

1\. Purposeful

2\. Defined

3\. Traceable

4\. Validated

5\. Reproducible

6\. Versioned

7\. Quality-assessed

8\. Evidence-backed

9\. Governed

10\. Change-controlled

⸻

**74\. Success Criteria**

E3 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1. setiap material measurement memiliki objective, dimension, criterion,  
   requirement, atau legitimate measurement purpose;
2. metric memiliki definition yang dapat dipahami dan direproduksi;
3. measurement method dapat dijelaskan;
4. source/evidence dapat ditelusuri;
5. measurement result memiliki measurement period;
6. measurement quality dapat dinilai;
7. confidence dapat dinyatakan;
8. leading dan lagging indicators dapat dibedakan;
9. KPI dapat dibedakan dari metric;
10. measurement lifecycle lengkap dari identification sampai retirement;
11. historical measurement memiliki integrity;
12. material measurement changes memiliki controlled revision;
13. measurement gap dapat dinyatakan tanpa fabricated score;
14. insufficient evidence dapat menghentikan conclusion;
15. measurement tidak mengambil alih performance evaluation;
16. measurement tidak menciptakan governance authority;
17. measurement tidak memindahkan domain ownership;
18. cross-platform measurement dependency mempertahankan ownership;
19. Policy measurement mempertahankan governance boundary;
20. Control measurement tidak menjadi execution ownership;
21. Certification measurement tidak menjadi implementation ownership;
22. Compliance measurement mempertahankan applicable ownership;
23. measurement dapat digunakan untuk trend analysis;
24. measurement dapat digunakan untuk improvement re-measurement;
25. measurement quality dapat digunakan sebagai confidence input;
26. E3 menyediakan stable foundation bagi E4–E10;
27. framework dapat diwariskan ke Platform lain tanpa memaksakan metric  
    domain PD-03.

⸻

**SOURCE-SUPPORTED**

Baseline mendukung:

- measurement specification;
- measurement quality;
- measurement confidence;
- evidence traceability;
- measurement governance;

- distinction measurement vs evaluation;
- measurement gap handling.

**PD-03-SPECIFIC SOURCE-SUPPORTED**

PD-03-specific source mendukung:

- Governance & Compliance sebagai domain;
- Governance Policy responsibility;
- Governance Control responsibility;
- Certification responsibility;
- Compliance responsibility;
- Governance Standards relationship;
- Governance Baseline relationship;
- cross-platform ownership preservation;
- dependency ≠ ownership;
- certification ≠ implementation;
- control ≠ execution.

**BOUNDED CANONICAL SYNTHESIS**

Adaptasi PD-03 yang dibekukan dalam E3 meliputi:

- Governance Policy measurement;
- Governance Control measurement;
- Certification measurement;
- Compliance measurement;
- Governance Standards measurement;
- Governance Baseline measurement;
- cross-platform evidence dependency;
- PD-03 measurement interpretation.

Adaptasi tersebut tidak memperluas organizational ownership PD-03.

**SOURCE GAP**

Belum ditemukan source authoritative yang menetapkan secara eksplisit:

- final PD-03 metric catalogue;
- final PD-03 KPI catalogue;
- exact KPI formula;
- exact measurement frequency;
- exact target;
- exact threshold;
- exact weighting;
- exact PD-03 evidence repository;

- exact PD-03 measurement dashboard schema.

Elemen tersebut sengaja tidak diinvensikan dalam E3.

**79\. Canonical Freeze Statement**

E3 — Performance Measurement establishes the controlled measurement architecture through which PD-03 Governance & Compliance converts observable governance, compliance, control, certification, capability, operational, strategic, and improvement conditions into traceable measurement results.

E3 preserves the constitutional distinction between measurement and evaluation. Measurement produces evidence and result; evaluation determines meaning. Measurement does not create strategic authority, governance authority, operational authority, or domain ownership.

PD-03-specific measurement remains bounded by Governance & Compliance responsibility over Governance Policy, Governance Control, Certification, Compliance, Governance Standards, and Governance Baseline relationships. Control measurement does not become execution ownership; certification measurement does not become implementation ownership; cross-platform evidence dependency does not become cross-platform ownership.

Measurement quality, confidence, provenance, historical integrity, versioning, lifecycle control, anti-gaming protection, and controlled change are mandatory characteristics of formal performance measurement.

Absence of measurement is not automatically performance failure. Insufficient evidence must remain an explicit state rather than being converted into a fabricated performance score.

Domain-specific KPI, metric catalogue, target, threshold, weighting, frequency, formula, evidence source, and measurement ownership remain SOURCE GAP until verified from authoritative PD-03 sources.

E3 is therefore FROZEN as the Performance Measurement Architecture for Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

⸻

**Final Verdict**

E3 — Performance Measurement: FROZEN — SOURCE-BOUNDED

1\. Purpose

E4 menetapkan framework untuk menerjemahkan objective dan success criterion  
PD-03 menjadi success metrics, KPI, supporting metrics, dan diagnostic  
metrics yang measurable, traceable, dan useful untuk Performance Review.

E4 menjawab:

"Indikator apa yang menunjukkan apakah objective Governance & Compliance  
tercapai?"

E4 tidak menciptakan objective baru.

E4 tidak mengubah objective yang telah disahkan.

E4 tidak menciptakan governance authority.

E4 tidak mengambil alih domain execution dari Platform lain.

⸻

2\. Constitutional Position

Canonical Part E relationship:

E1 — Performance Constitution

&nbsp; ↓

"What is performance?"

&nbsp; ↓

E2 — Performance Model

&nbsp; ↓

"What dimensions describe performance?"

&nbsp; ↓

E3 — Performance Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

"How is performance measured?"

&nbsp; ↓

E4 — KPI & Success Metrics

&nbsp; ↓

"What indicates success?"

&nbsp; ↓

E5 — Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

"How is performance evaluated?"

E4 merupakan translation layer antara measurement architecture E3 dan  
performance review E5.

⸻

3\. Objective-to-KPI Architecture

Canonical model:

Objective

&nbsp; ↓

Success Criterion

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Metric

&nbsp; ├── KPI

&nbsp; ├── Supporting Metric

&nbsp; └── Diagnostic Metric

&nbsp; ↓

Target / Expected Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Result

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Evaluation

Pemisahan ini wajib dipertahankan.

Objective

&nbsp; ≠

Metric

&nbsp; ≠

KPI

&nbsp; ≠

Target

&nbsp; ≠

Measurement Result

&nbsp; ≠

Performance Conclusion

⸻

4\. Success Metric

Success Metric adalah metric yang digunakan untuk menunjukkan apakah  
objective atau success criterion menghasilkan kondisi yang diharapkan.

Success Metric harus memiliki:

• objective linkage;

• success criterion linkage;

• definition;

• measurement method;

• evidence basis;

• ownership;

• applicable context.

Tidak setiap Success Metric harus menjadi KPI.

⸻

5\. KPI

KPI adalah metric yang dipilih secara formal sebagai indikator utama yang  
material terhadap monitoring dan review.

Relationship:

Success Metrics

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├── KPI

&nbsp; ├── Supporting Metrics

&nbsp; └── Diagnostic Metrics

Dengan demikian:

KPI merupakan subset terpilih dari metric, bukan keseluruhan metric  
architecture.

E4 reference framework secara eksplisit membedakan KPI dari supporting dan  
diagnostic metrics.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

6\. KPI Selection Principles

Metric dapat dipilih sebagai KPI apabila secara material:

0\. berhubungan dengan objective;

0\. relevan terhadap success criterion;

0\. berguna untuk review;

0\. dapat diukur secara reliable;

0\. memiliki evidence source;

0\. memiliki owner;

0\. dapat ditelusuri;

0\. tidak redundant;

0\. tidak mudah menghasilkan misleading representation;

0\. memberikan decision-useful information.

KPI selection harus mengutamakan materiality dan usefulness, bukan jumlah  
KPI.

⸻

7\. PD-03 Objective Families

E4 PD-03 menggunakan objective yang berasal dari predecessor architecture.

Objective tidak boleh diciptakan oleh E4.

Apabila source PD-03 menyediakan objective family, objective tersebut dapat  
dipetakan ke:

Strategic Objectives

Operational Objectives

Governance Objectives

Capability Objectives

Jika objective family tertentu belum ditemukan pada authoritative PD-03  
source:

Do not invent the objective.

Tetapkan sebagai:

SOURCE GAP

dan teruskan ke reconciliation.

⸻

Governance & Compliance

Result

E4 tidak boleh mengubah Governance Baseline.

E4 hanya menggunakan baseline sebagai legitimate measurement reference apabila  
measurement requirement memang berlaku.

⸻

16\. Cross-Platform KPI Boundary

PD-03 dapat membutuhkan evidence dari Platform lain.

Canonical relationship:

PD-03 KPI Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence Dependency

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Platform

&nbsp; ↓

Domain Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Measurement

Dependency tidak mengubah ownership.

Dependency

&nbsp; ≠

Subordination

&nbsp; ≠

Ownership Transfer

Platform lain tetap menjadi owner atas domain evidence mereka.

⸻

17\. KPI Ownership

Setiap formal KPI harus memiliki ownership yang eksplisit.

Minimum distinction:

KPI Owner

Evidence Owner

Measurement Owner

Review Owner

Role dapat digabung apabila tidak menimbulkan material conflict.

Namun:

Performance KPI ownership tidak otomatis berarti ownership atas domain yang  
diukur.

Contoh:

Governance KPI

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Management / Governance Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Platform Owner

⸻

18\. Target and Criterion Linkage

E4 mempertahankan separation:

Success Criterion

&nbsp; ↓

Target / Expected Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Actual Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Variance

&nbsp; ↓

Evaluation

Target bukan measurement result.

Measurement result bukan target.

Performance Management tidak boleh mengubah approved target secara unilateral.

Jika target source belum ditemukan:

TARGET SOURCE GAP

harus dicatat.

Jangan mengisi target dengan angka default.

⸻

19\. Leading and Lagging Indicators

E4 harus membedakan:

Leading Indicator

Memberikan early signal terhadap kondisi yang dapat memengaruhi outcome.

Lagging Indicator

Mengukur outcome atau condition yang telah terjadi.

Canonical model:

Leading Indicator

&nbsp; ↓

Potential Future Condition

dan:

Lagging Indicator

&nbsp; ↓

Observed Outcome

Leading indicator tidak boleh dianggap sebagai bukti final success.

Lagging indicator tidak selalu menjelaskan root cause.

⸻

20\. Supporting Metrics

Supporting Metric digunakan untuk memberikan context terhadap KPI.

Contoh relationship:

Primary KPI

&nbsp; +

Supporting Metric

&nbsp; +

Diagnostic Metric

&nbsp; ↓

Better Interpretation

Supporting metric tidak otomatis menjadi KPI.

⸻

21\. Diagnostic Metrics

Diagnostic Metric digunakan untuk membantu menjelaskan mengapa suatu KPI  
berubah.

Canonical:

KPI Variance

&nbsp; ↓

Diagnostic Metrics

&nbsp; ↓

Potential Cause

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Review

Diagnostic metric tidak boleh digunakan sebagai success conclusion tanpa  
evaluation.

⸻

22\. KPI Coverage

Setiap material objective yang memiliki legitimate measurable success  
condition harus memiliki:

Objective

&nbsp; ↓

Success Criterion

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Coverage

Jika KPI tidak appropriate:

Objective

&nbsp; ↓

Justified Measurement Exception

Measurement exception harus terdokumentasi.

Tidak boleh ada KPI hanya untuk memenuhi target jumlah KPI.

⸻

23\. KPI Catalogue

PD-03 harus memiliki controlled KPI catalogue apabila authoritative source  
menetapkannya.

Minimum schema yang diwariskan dari E4 reference framework:

Metric ID

Metric Name

Objective

Success Criterion

KPI Status

Dimension

Indicator Type

Definition

Formula

Target / Criterion

Owner

Evidence Source

Frequency

Status

Version

Namun field values PD-03 belum boleh dianggap frozen tanpa source.

⸻

24\. KPI Lifecycle

Canonical lifecycle:

Candidate Metric

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

KPI Selection

&nbsp; ↓

Approval

&nbsp; ↓

Active KPI

&nbsp; ↓

Review

&nbsp; ↓

Modify / Retain

&nbsp; ↓

Retire / Replace

Setiap material KPI change harus memiliki rationale dan version.

⸻

25\. KPI Approval

KPI approval harus mengikuti authority yang berlaku.

KPI selection tidak boleh:

• menciptakan governance authority;

• mengubah ownership;

• mengubah objective;

• mengubah architecture;

• mengubah operational execution;

• mengubah approved target secara unilateral.

Jika KPI memiliki material cross-platform implication, applicable governance  
and authority mechanism harus digunakan.

⸻

26\. KPI Change Control

Material changes terhadap:

• definition;

• formula;

• target;

• threshold;

• evidence source;

• scope;

• owner;

• frequency;

• classification;

harus controlled.

Historical KPI result tidak boleh dimutasi secara silent.

⸻

27\. KPI Versioning

Setiap material KPI definition harus memiliki version.

Contoh:

KPI-GOV-001

v1.0

Jika formula berubah:

KPI-GOV-001

v2.0

Historical results harus tetap dapat dikaitkan dengan version definition yang  
digunakan saat measurement dilakukan.

⸻

28\. KPI Result vs Performance Health

KPI result bukan Performance Health.

Canonical chain:

KPI Results

&nbsp; ↓

Dimension Results

&nbsp; ↓

Cross-Dimension Analysis

&nbsp; ↓

Context

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Health

Tidak boleh:

One KPI

&nbsp; ↓

Platform Health

Reference E4 secara eksplisit menetapkan bahwa KPI result bukan Performance  
Health.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

29\. KPI Result States

Result state dapat digunakan jika evaluation criteria telah ditetapkan.

Possible states:

Achieved

On Track

At Risk

Below Criterion

Critical

Not Measured

Measurement Pending

Insufficient Evidence

State bukan pengganti actual measurement.

State juga bukan governance decision.

⸻

30\. Evidence Confidence

KPI harus mempertahankan evidence confidence.

Possible confidence:

High

Medium

Low

Insufficient Evidence

Confidence mempertimbangkan:

• evidence completeness;

• source reliability;

• measurement quality;

• calculation integrity;

• traceability.

Confidence tidak boleh digunakan untuk memperindah performance result.

⸻

31\. Insufficient Evidence

Jika evidence tidak memadai:

Insufficient Evidence

harus menjadi legitimate state.

Tidak diperbolehkan:

No Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Assume Success

atau:

No Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Assume Failure

Kondisi tersebut diteruskan ke Performance Review.

⸻

32\. KPI and Governance Authority

KPI tidak menciptakan authority.

Contoh:

KPI:

Authority Path Compliance

Result:

Below Criterion

Finding:

Governance Compliance Gap

Performance Management dapat:

• identify;

• measure;

• report;

• review;

• recommend;

• escalate.

Performance Management tidak otomatis:

• revoke authority;

• invalidate decision;

• impose sanction;

• restructure Platform.

Authority tetap mengikuti Governance Architecture.

⸻

33\. KPI and Policy

Policy KPI dapat menunjukkan:

Policy

&nbsp; ↓

Adoption

&nbsp; ↓

Adherence

&nbsp; ↓

Exception

&nbsp; ↓

Effectiveness

Namun KPI tersebut harus tetap berada pada Governance Policy boundary.

⸻

34\. KPI and Control

Control KPI dapat menunjukkan:

Control Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Control Operation

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Effectiveness

35\. KPI and Certification

Certification KPI dapat menunjukkan:

&nbsp; ↓

Readiness / Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Certification Condition

&nbsp; ↓

KPI

Certification KPI tidak menjadi implementation KPI.

⸻

36\. KPI and Compliance

Compliance KPI dapat menunjukkan:

Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Compliance Condition

&nbsp; ↓

KPI

Compliance ownership tetap mengikuti domain ownership.

⸻

37\. KPI and Governance Standards

Governance Standards dapat digunakan sebagai criterion source.

Governance Standard

&nbsp; ↓

Criterion

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

KPI

Technical standard tidak boleh dimasukkan ke PD-03 KPI catalogue hanya karena  
memiliki governance implication.

⸻

38\. KPI and Governance Baseline

Governance Baseline dapat digunakan untuk:

• baseline comparison;

• governance maturity evidence;

• compliance condition;

• control condition;

• certification condition.

Namun penggunaan tersebut harus tetap traceable kepada applicable requirement.

⸻

39\. KPI Overlap Control

Satu kondisi dapat memengaruhi beberapa dimension.

Namun satu metric tidak boleh dihitung sebagai KPI independen di beberapa  
dimension tanpa rationale.

Minimum mapping:

Metric

&nbsp; ↓

Primary Dimension

&nbsp; ↓

Primary Objective

&nbsp; ↓

Usage Context

Jika diperlukan:

Secondary Dimension

boleh dicatat tanpa menggandakan KPI.

Tujuannya mencegah double counting.

⸻

40\. KPI Materiality

KPI harus diklasifikasikan berdasarkan materiality.

Possible classification:

Critical

Material

Supporting

Diagnostic

Classification harus memiliki rationale.

KPI proliferation harus dihindari.

⸻

41\. KPI Anti-Gaming

E4 mewarisi anti-gaming controls:

0\. KPI definition harus fixed;

0\. formula harus traceable;

0\. source harus identifiable;

0\. target tidak boleh diubah untuk memperbaiki score secara retroaktif;

0\. historical result tidak boleh dimutasi secara silent;

0\. context harus tersedia;

0\. limitation harus terlihat;

0\. KPI tidak boleh menjadi satu-satunya evidence of success;

0\. supporting/diagnostic metrics dapat digunakan untuk context;

0\. material anomalies harus dapat direview.

tanpa context.

54\. Success Criteria

E4 PD-03 dianggap berhasil apabila:

0\. objective dapat ditelusuri ke success criterion;

0\. success criterion dapat ditelusuri ke measurement requirement;

0\. measurement requirement dapat ditelusuri ke metric;

0\. metric dapat diklasifikasikan sebagai KPI, supporting, atau diagnostic;

0\. Policy measurement tetap berada pada Governance Policy boundary;

0\. Control measurement tetap berada pada Governance Control boundary;

0\. Certification measurement tidak mengambil implementation ownership;

0\. Compliance measurement mempertahankan domain ownership;

0\. Governance Standards tetap dibedakan dari technical standards;

0\. Governance Baseline dapat digunakan sebagai legitimate reference;

0\. setiap formal KPI memiliki owner;

0\. evidence source dapat ditelusuri;

0\. target/criterion terpisah dari actual measurement;

0\. leading/lagging distinction dapat diterapkan;

0\. KPI overlap dan double counting dapat dikendalikan;

0\. KPI lifecycle dapat dikelola;

0\. KPI definition memiliki version;

0\. material KPI changes memiliki change control;

0\. insufficient evidence dapat dinyatakan;

0\. KPI result tidak disamakan dengan Performance Health;

0\. KPI tidak menciptakan governance authority;

0\. KPI tidak memindahkan domain ownership;

0\. cross-platform dependency tidak menjadi organizational subordination;

0\. KPI dapat digunakan sebagai input E5;

0\. KPI dapat digunakan sebagai input E7;

0\. KPI dapat digunakan sebagai improvement signal E8;

0\. KPI dapat menjadi maturity evidence E9;

0\. KPI dapat menjadi evidence E10 tanpa menjadi sole success criterion;

0\. framework dapat diwariskan tanpa memaksakan PD-03 metric catalogue ke  
Platform lain.

58\. Canonical Freeze Statement

E4 — KPI & Success Metrics establishes the controlled translation layer through which approved PD-03 Governance & Compliance objectives and success criteria are translated into measurable success metrics, selected KPIs, supporting metrics, and diagnostic metrics.

E4 preserves the distinction between objective, criterion, metric, KPI, target, measurement result, and performance evaluation.

For PD-03, the KPI framework is bounded by the Governance & Compliance domain and its assigned Policy, Control, Certification, Governance Standards, and Governance Baseline responsibilities.

Governance interaction with Architecture, Security, Quality, and other Platform Divisions does not transfer domain ownership. Control measurement does not become execution ownership; Certification measurement does not become implementation ownership.

No PD-03-specific KPI, target, threshold, formula, frequency, evidence source, or final KPI catalogue is fabricated where authoritative source support is absent. Such elements remain controlled SOURCE GAP pending reconciliation.

E4 is therefore FROZEN as the KPI & Success Metrics Architecture for Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

⸻
