**1\. Purpose**

E4 menetapkan framework untuk menerjemahkan objective dan success criterion  
PD-03 menjadi success metrics, KPI, supporting metrics, dan diagnostic  
metrics yang measurable, traceable, dan useful untuk Performance Review.

E4 menjawab:

"Indikator apa yang menunjukkan apakah objective Governance & Compliance  
tercapai?"

E4 tidak menciptakan objective baru.

E4 tidak mengubah objective yang telah disahkan.

E4 tidak menciptakan governance authority.

E4 tidak mengambil alih domain execution dari Platform lain.

⸻

**2\. Constitutional Position**

Canonical Part E relationship:

E1 — Performance Constitution

&nbsp; ↓

"What is performance?"

&nbsp; ↓

E2 — Performance Model

&nbsp; ↓

"What dimensions describe performance?"

&nbsp; ↓

E3 — Performance Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

"How is performance measured?"

&nbsp; ↓

E4 — KPI & Success Metrics

&nbsp; ↓

"What indicates success?"

&nbsp; ↓

E5 — Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

"How is performance evaluated?"

E4 merupakan translation layer antara measurement architecture E3 dan  
performance review E5.

⸻

**3\. Objective-to-KPI Architecture**

Canonical model:

Objective

&nbsp; ↓

Success Criterion

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Metric

&nbsp; ├── KPI

&nbsp; ├── Supporting Metric

&nbsp; └── Diagnostic Metric

&nbsp; ↓

Target / Expected Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Result

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Evaluation

Pemisahan ini wajib dipertahankan.

Objective

&nbsp; ≠

Metric

&nbsp; ≠

KPI

&nbsp; ≠

Target

&nbsp; ≠

Measurement Result

&nbsp; ≠

Performance Conclusion

⸻

**4\. Success Metric**

Success Metric adalah metric yang digunakan untuk menunjukkan apakah  
objective atau success criterion menghasilkan kondisi yang diharapkan.

Success Metric harus memiliki:

- objective linkage;
- success criterion linkage;
- definition;
- measurement method;
- evidence basis;
- ownership;
- applicable context.

Tidak setiap Success Metric harus menjadi KPI.

⸻

**5\. KPI**

KPI adalah metric yang dipilih secara formal sebagai indikator utama yang  
material terhadap monitoring dan review.

Relationship:

Success Metrics

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├── KPI

&nbsp; ├── Supporting Metrics

&nbsp; └── Diagnostic Metrics

Dengan demikian:

KPI merupakan subset terpilih dari metric, bukan keseluruhan metric  
architecture.

E4 reference framework secara eksplisit membedakan KPI dari supporting dan  
diagnostic metrics.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

**6\. KPI Selection Principles**

Metric dapat dipilih sebagai KPI apabila secara material:

1. berhubungan dengan objective;
2. relevan terhadap success criterion;
3. berguna untuk review;
4. dapat diukur secara reliable;
5. memiliki evidence source;
6. memiliki owner;
7. dapat ditelusuri;
8. tidak redundant;
9. tidak mudah menghasilkan misleading representation;
10. memberikan decision-useful information.

KPI selection harus mengutamakan materiality dan usefulness, bukan jumlah  
KPI.

⸻

**7\. PD-03 Objective Families**

E4 PD-03 menggunakan objective yang berasal dari predecessor architecture.

Objective tidak boleh diciptakan oleh E4.

Apabila source PD-03 menyediakan objective family, objective tersebut dapat  
dipetakan ke:

Strategic Objectives

Operational Objectives

Governance Objectives

Capability Objectives

Jika objective family tertentu belum ditemukan pada authoritative PD-03  
source:

Do not invent the objective.

Tetapkan sebagai:

SOURCE GAP

dan teruskan ke reconciliation.

⸻

**8\. Governance & Compliance Performance Boundary**

E4 harus mengikuti organizational boundary PD-03:

PD-03

Governance & Compliance

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├── Policy

&nbsp; ├── Control

&nbsp; ├── Certification

&nbsp; └── Governance Standards

Governance Baseline menjadi canonical governance reference.

PD-03 tidak menjadi owner atas:

- Architecture execution;
- Security execution;
- Quality execution;
- technical design;
- operational execution Platform lain.

Baseline PD-03 secara eksplisit mempertahankan ownership domain masing-masing  
Platform.

⸻

**9\. Governance Objective → Success Metric**

Governance Objective harus diterjemahkan melalui:

Governance Objective

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Success Criterion

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Measurement Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Metric

&nbsp; ↓

Governance KPI

Governance KPI dapat digunakan untuk mengukur kondisi seperti:

- governance effectiveness;
- policy adherence;
- governance decision integrity;
- accountability;
- control effectiveness;
- certification condition;
- compliance condition;
- governance exception.

Namun metric konkret hanya boleh dibekukan jika memiliki authoritative  
source.

⸻

**10\. Governance Policy Metrics**

PD-03 memiliki responsibility atas Governance Policy.

Policy measurement dapat digunakan untuk mengukur kondisi seperti:

Governance Policy

&nbsp; ↓

Expected Governance Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Success Metric / KPI

Possible measurement dimensions:

- applicability;
- adoption;
- adherence;
- exception;
- effectiveness;
- review status.

Namun:

Governance Policy

&nbsp; ≠

All AIOS Policies

Technical/domain policy tetap berada pada applicable domain owner.

Baseline PD-03 secara eksplisit membatasi Policy sebagai Governance Policy.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

**11\. Governance Control Metrics**

PD-03 memiliki responsibility atas Governance Control.

Control metrics dapat mengukur:

- control coverage;
- control operation;
- control effectiveness;
- control exception;
- control evidence;
- remediation condition.

Canonical boundary:

Governance Control

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03

Control Implementation

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Domain Owner

Karena itu:

Control KPI tidak boleh berubah menjadi operational execution KPI milik  
PD-03 tanpa authoritative ownership basis.

Baseline secara eksplisit membedakan Governance Control dari Control  
Implementation.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

**12\. Certification Metrics**

PD-03 memiliki organizational responsibility terhadap Certification dalam  
scope Governance & Compliance.

Certification success measurement dapat memperhatikan:

- certification requirement;
- evidence completeness;
- certification status;
- certification finding;
- remediation condition;
- certification readiness.

Canonical relationship:

Certification Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Certification Metric

&nbsp; ↓

KPI / Success Metric

&nbsp; ↓

Evaluation

Namun:

Certification

&nbsp; ≠

Implementation Ownership

Platform yang menjadi subject certification tetap memiliki execution ownership  
atas domainnya.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

**13\. Compliance Metrics**

Compliance metric digunakan untuk menunjukkan kondisi compliance terhadap  
approved governance requirement.

Canonical model:

Governance Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Compliance Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Compliance Metric

&nbsp; ↓

KPI

PD-03 dapat memiliki governance/compliance framework ownership, tetapi tidak  
mengambil alih domain compliance execution Platform lain.

Baseline menyatakan bahwa governance assurance dan domain compliance ownership  
harus dibedakan.

Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang .txt

⸻

**14\. Governance Standards Metrics**

Governance Standards dapat menjadi basis success measurement.

Canonical model:

Governance Standard

&nbsp; ↓

Expected Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Observed Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Metric

&nbsp; ↓

KPI / Supporting Metric

Namun:

Governance Standards

&nbsp; ≠

All Technical Standards

Jika standard merupakan technical implementation standard, applicable domain  
owner tetap memiliki ownership.

⸻

**15\. Governance Baseline Metrics**

Governance Baseline dapat digunakan sebagai reference condition.

Governance Baseline

&nbsp; ↓

Expected Governance State

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Metric

&nbsp; ↓

Result

E4 tidak boleh mengubah Governance Baseline.

E4 hanya menggunakan baseline sebagai legitimate measurement reference apabila  
measurement requirement memang berlaku.

⸻

**16\. Cross-Platform KPI Boundary**

PD-03 dapat membutuhkan evidence dari Platform lain.

Canonical relationship:

PD-03 KPI Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence Dependency

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Platform

&nbsp; ↓

Domain Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Measurement

Dependency tidak mengubah ownership.

Dependency

&nbsp; ≠

Subordination

&nbsp; ≠

Ownership Transfer

Platform lain tetap menjadi owner atas domain evidence mereka.

⸻

**17\. KPI Ownership**

Setiap formal KPI harus memiliki ownership yang eksplisit.

Minimum distinction:

KPI Owner

Evidence Owner

Measurement Owner

Review Owner

Role dapat digabung apabila tidak menimbulkan material conflict.

Namun:

Performance KPI ownership tidak otomatis berarti ownership atas domain yang  
diukur.

Contoh:

Governance KPI

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Management / Governance Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Platform Owner

⸻

**18\. Target and Criterion Linkage**

E4 mempertahankan separation:

Success Criterion

&nbsp; ↓

Target / Expected Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Actual Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Variance

&nbsp; ↓

Evaluation

Target bukan measurement result.

Measurement result bukan target.

Performance Management tidak boleh mengubah approved target secara unilateral.

Jika target source belum ditemukan:

TARGET SOURCE GAP

harus dicatat.

Jangan mengisi target dengan angka default.

⸻

**19\. Leading and Lagging Indicators**

E4 harus membedakan:

Leading Indicator

Memberikan early signal terhadap kondisi yang dapat memengaruhi outcome.

Lagging Indicator

Mengukur outcome atau condition yang telah terjadi.

Canonical model:

Leading Indicator

&nbsp; ↓

Potential Future Condition

dan:

Lagging Indicator

&nbsp; ↓

Observed Outcome

Leading indicator tidak boleh dianggap sebagai bukti final success.

Lagging indicator tidak selalu menjelaskan root cause.

⸻

**20\. Supporting Metrics**

Supporting Metric digunakan untuk memberikan context terhadap KPI.

Contoh relationship:

Primary KPI

&nbsp; +

Supporting Metric

&nbsp; +

Diagnostic Metric

&nbsp; ↓

Better Interpretation

Supporting metric tidak otomatis menjadi KPI.

⸻

**21\. Diagnostic Metrics**

Diagnostic Metric digunakan untuk membantu menjelaskan mengapa suatu KPI  
berubah.

Canonical:

KPI Variance

&nbsp; ↓

Diagnostic Metrics

&nbsp; ↓

Potential Cause

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Review

Diagnostic metric tidak boleh digunakan sebagai success conclusion tanpa  
evaluation.

⸻

**22\. KPI Coverage**

Setiap material objective yang memiliki legitimate measurable success  
condition harus memiliki:

Objective

&nbsp; ↓

Success Criterion

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Coverage

Jika KPI tidak appropriate:

Objective

&nbsp; ↓

Justified Measurement Exception

Measurement exception harus terdokumentasi.

Tidak boleh ada KPI hanya untuk memenuhi target jumlah KPI.

⸻

**23\. KPI Catalogue**

PD-03 harus memiliki controlled KPI catalogue apabila authoritative source  
menetapkannya.

Minimum schema yang diwariskan dari E4 reference framework:

Metric ID

Metric Name

Objective

Success Criterion

KPI Status

Dimension

Indicator Type

Definition

Formula

Target / Criterion

Owner

Evidence Source

Frequency

Status

Version

Namun field values PD-03 belum boleh dianggap frozen tanpa source.

⸻

**24\. KPI Lifecycle**

Canonical lifecycle:

Candidate Metric

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

KPI Selection

&nbsp; ↓

Approval

&nbsp; ↓

Active KPI

&nbsp; ↓

Review

&nbsp; ↓

Modify / Retain

&nbsp; ↓

Retire / Replace

Setiap material KPI change harus memiliki rationale dan version.

⸻

**25\. KPI Approval**

KPI approval harus mengikuti authority yang berlaku.

KPI selection tidak boleh:

- menciptakan governance authority;
- mengubah ownership;
- mengubah objective;
- mengubah architecture;
- mengubah operational execution;
- mengubah approved target secara unilateral.

Jika KPI memiliki material cross-platform implication, applicable governance  
and authority mechanism harus digunakan.

⸻

**26\. KPI Change Control**

Material changes terhadap:

- definition;
- formula;
- target;
- threshold;
- evidence source;
- scope;
- owner;
- frequency;
- classification;

harus controlled.

Historical KPI result tidak boleh dimutasi secara silent.

⸻

**27\. KPI Versioning**

Setiap material KPI definition harus memiliki version.

Contoh:

KPI-GOV-001

v1.0

Jika formula berubah:

KPI-GOV-001

v2.0

Historical results harus tetap dapat dikaitkan dengan version definition yang  
digunakan saat measurement dilakukan.

⸻

**28\. KPI Result vs Performance Health**

KPI result bukan Performance Health.

Canonical chain:

KPI Results

&nbsp; ↓

Dimension Results

&nbsp; ↓

Cross-Dimension Analysis

&nbsp; ↓

Context

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Health

Tidak boleh:

One KPI

&nbsp; ↓

Platform Health

Reference E4 secara eksplisit menetapkan bahwa KPI result bukan Performance  
Health.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

**29\. KPI Result States**

Result state dapat digunakan jika evaluation criteria telah ditetapkan.

Possible states:

Achieved

On Track

At Risk

Below Criterion

Critical

Not Measured

Measurement Pending

Insufficient Evidence

State bukan pengganti actual measurement.

State juga bukan governance decision.

⸻

**30\. Evidence Confidence**

KPI harus mempertahankan evidence confidence.

Possible confidence:

High

Medium

Low

Insufficient Evidence

Confidence mempertimbangkan:

- evidence completeness;
- source reliability;
- measurement quality;
- calculation integrity;
- traceability.

Confidence tidak boleh digunakan untuk memperindah performance result.

⸻

**31\. Insufficient Evidence**

Jika evidence tidak memadai:

Insufficient Evidence

harus menjadi legitimate state.

Tidak diperbolehkan:

No Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Assume Success

atau:

No Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Assume Failure

Kondisi tersebut diteruskan ke Performance Review.

⸻

**32\. KPI and Governance Authority**

KPI tidak menciptakan authority.

Contoh:

KPI:

Authority Path Compliance

Result:

Below Criterion

Finding:

Governance Compliance Gap

Performance Management dapat:

- identify;
- measure;
- report;
- review;
- recommend;
- escalate.

Performance Management tidak otomatis:

- revoke authority;
- invalidate decision;
- impose sanction;
- restructure Platform.

Authority tetap mengikuti Governance Architecture.

⸻

**33\. KPI and Policy**

Policy KPI dapat menunjukkan:

Policy

&nbsp; ↓

Adoption

&nbsp; ↓

Adherence

&nbsp; ↓

Exception

&nbsp; ↓

Effectiveness

Namun KPI tersebut harus tetap berada pada Governance Policy boundary.

⸻

**34\. KPI and Control**

Control KPI dapat menunjukkan:

Control Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Control Operation

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Effectiveness

Tetapi control KPI tidak memberikan PD-03 ownership atas operational execution.

⸻

**35\. KPI and Certification**

Certification KPI dapat menunjukkan:

Certification Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Readiness / Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Certification Condition

&nbsp; ↓

KPI

Certification KPI tidak menjadi implementation KPI.

⸻

**36\. KPI and Compliance**

Compliance KPI dapat menunjukkan:

Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Compliance Condition

&nbsp; ↓

KPI

Compliance ownership tetap mengikuti domain ownership.

⸻

**37\. KPI and Governance Standards**

Governance Standards dapat digunakan sebagai criterion source.

Governance Standard

&nbsp; ↓

Criterion

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

KPI

Technical standard tidak boleh dimasukkan ke PD-03 KPI catalogue hanya karena  
memiliki governance implication.

⸻

**38\. KPI and Governance Baseline**

Governance Baseline dapat digunakan untuk:

- baseline comparison;
- governance maturity evidence;
- compliance condition;
- control condition;
- certification condition.

Namun penggunaan tersebut harus tetap traceable kepada applicable requirement.

⸻

**39\. KPI Overlap Control**

Satu kondisi dapat memengaruhi beberapa dimension.

Namun satu metric tidak boleh dihitung sebagai KPI independen di beberapa  
dimension tanpa rationale.

Minimum mapping:

Metric

&nbsp; ↓

Primary Dimension

&nbsp; ↓

Primary Objective

&nbsp; ↓

Usage Context

Jika diperlukan:

Secondary Dimension

boleh dicatat tanpa menggandakan KPI.

Tujuannya mencegah double counting.

⸻

**40\. KPI Materiality**

KPI harus diklasifikasikan berdasarkan materiality.

Possible classification:

Critical

Material

Supporting

Diagnostic

Classification harus memiliki rationale.

KPI proliferation harus dihindari.

⸻

**41\. KPI Anti-Gaming**

E4 mewarisi anti-gaming controls:

1. KPI definition harus fixed;
2. formula harus traceable;
3. source harus identifiable;
4. target tidak boleh diubah untuk memperbaiki score secara retroaktif;
5. historical result tidak boleh dimutasi secara silent;
6. context harus tersedia;
7. limitation harus terlihat;
8. KPI tidak boleh menjadi satu-satunya evidence of success;
9. supporting/diagnostic metrics dapat digunakan untuk context;
10. material anomalies harus dapat direview.

⸻

**42\. KPI and Reporting**

E7 menggunakan E4 sebagai source KPI information.

Canonical chain:

Objective

&nbsp; ↓

Success Criterion

&nbsp; ↓

KPI

&nbsp; ↓

Target

&nbsp; ↓

Actual

&nbsp; ↓

Variance

&nbsp; ↓

Trend

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence Confidence

&nbsp; ↓

Evaluation

KPI reporting tidak boleh hanya menampilkan:

KPI = 92%

tanpa context.

⸻

**43\. KPI and Performance Review**

E5 menggunakan KPI result sebagai evidence.

E4

KPI / Success Metric

&nbsp; ↓

E5

Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Finding

E4 tidak melakukan final evaluation.

⸻

**44\. KPI and Performance Governance**

E6 mengatur governance atas performance mechanism.

E4

KPI Framework

&nbsp; ↓

E6

Performance Governance

KPI governance harus tetap mengikuti authority architecture.

⸻

**45\. KPI and Performance Improvement**

E8 dapat menggunakan KPI gap sebagai improvement signal.

KPI Gap

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measurement

KPI improvement tidak dianggap berhasil hanya karena KPI kembali naik.

Effectiveness harus diverifikasi melalui E8/E5.

⸻

**46\. KPI and Performance Maturity**

E9 dapat menggunakan kualitas KPI architecture sebagai evidence maturity.

Namun:

KPI Maturity

&nbsp; ≠

Performance Success

A mature KPI system dapat tetap menunjukkan poor Governance & Compliance  
performance.

⸻

**47\. KPI and Performance Success**

E10 dapat menggunakan KPI evidence sebagai salah satu evidence source.

Namun:

KPI Achievement

&nbsp; ≠

Performance Success

Success harus dinilai secara integrated sesuai E10.

⸻

**48\. PD-03 KPI Architecture**

Canonical PD-03 architecture:

Governance & Compliance Objective

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Success Criterion

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Metric

&nbsp; ┌─────┼──────┐

&nbsp; ↓ ↓ ↓

&nbsp; KPI Supporting Diagnostic

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ↓

Target / Expected Condition

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ↓

Actual Measurement

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Review

⸻

**49\. PD-03 KPI Domain Map**

Reference domain map:

| **Domain**           | **Measurement Focus**                          |
| -------------------- | ---------------------------------------------- |
| Governance Policy    | Policy condition / adherence / effectiveness   |
| Governance Control   | Control condition / operation / effectiveness  |
| Certification        | Certification condition / readiness / evidence |
| Compliance           | Compliance condition / exception / evidence    |
| Governance Standards | Standard conformity / governance condition     |
| Governance Baseline  | Baseline conformance                           |

Tabel ini adalah measurement architecture, bukan frozen KPI catalogue.

⸻

**50\. Domain-Specific Metric Rule**

Metric konkret harus ditentukan berdasarkan:

Applicable Objective

&nbsp; +

Success Criterion

&nbsp; +

Measurement Requirement

&nbsp; +

Evidence Source

&nbsp; +

Domain Ownership

Tidak boleh ditentukan hanya berdasarkan nama domain.

⸻

**51\. Source-Gap Protection**

Jika source tidak memberikan:

- KPI;
- metric;
- target;
- threshold;
- formula;
- frequency;
- evidence source;
- owner;

maka E4 tidak boleh mengarangnya.

Gunakan:

SOURCE GAP

atau:

N/A — AUTHORITATIVE SOURCE NOT FOUND

Kemudian teruskan kepada reconciliation.

⸻

**52\. Claude Code Reconciliation Directive**

Claude Code harus melakukan source reconciliation khusus E4 PD-03.

Search target:

1. exact PD-03 E4 definition;
2. PD-03 strategic objectives;
3. PD-03 governance objectives;
4. PD-03 operational objectives;
5. PD-03 capability objectives;
6. PD-03 success criteria;
7. PD-03 KPI catalogue;
8. PD-03 metric catalogue;
9. Policy KPIs;
10. Control KPIs;
11. Certification KPIs;
12. Compliance KPIs;
13. Governance Standards metrics;
14. Governance Baseline metrics;
15. target;
16. threshold;
17. formula;
18. frequency;
19. KPI owner;
20. evidence source;
21. KPI governance;
22. KPI lifecycle;
23. KPI change control.

Setiap finding wajib diklasifikasikan:

SOURCE-SUPPORTED

REFERENCE-FRAMEWORK

BOUNDED SYNTHESIS

SOURCE-GAP

Claude Code MUST NOT infer:

- KPI numbers;
- targets;
- thresholds;
- formulas;
- frequency;
- ownership;
- evidence source;

jika tidak ditemukan authoritative source.

⸻

**53\. Non-Goals**

E4 tidak bertujuan untuk:

- menciptakan strategic objectives;
- menciptakan governance authority;
- mengubah governance mandate;
- mengambil operational ownership;
- mengambil technical ownership;
- mengambil Platform ownership;
- menentukan universal KPI;
- menentukan universal target;
- menentukan universal threshold;
- menggantikan measurement architecture;
- menggantikan performance review;
- menggantikan performance governance;
- menggantikan performance reporting;
- menggantikan performance improvement;
- menentukan final performance health;
- menentukan final performance success.

⸻

**54\. Success Criteria**

E4 PD-03 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1. objective dapat ditelusuri ke success criterion;
2. success criterion dapat ditelusuri ke measurement requirement;
3. measurement requirement dapat ditelusuri ke metric;
4. metric dapat diklasifikasikan sebagai KPI, supporting, atau diagnostic;
5. Policy measurement tetap berada pada Governance Policy boundary;
6. Control measurement tetap berada pada Governance Control boundary;
7. Certification measurement tidak mengambil implementation ownership;
8. Compliance measurement mempertahankan domain ownership;
9. Governance Standards tetap dibedakan dari technical standards;
10. Governance Baseline dapat digunakan sebagai legitimate reference;
11. setiap formal KPI memiliki owner;
12. evidence source dapat ditelusuri;
13. target/criterion terpisah dari actual measurement;
14. leading/lagging distinction dapat diterapkan;
15. KPI overlap dan double counting dapat dikendalikan;
16. KPI lifecycle dapat dikelola;
17. KPI definition memiliki version;
18. material KPI changes memiliki change control;
19. insufficient evidence dapat dinyatakan;
20. KPI result tidak disamakan dengan Performance Health;
21. KPI tidak menciptakan governance authority;
22. KPI tidak memindahkan domain ownership;
23. cross-platform dependency tidak menjadi organizational subordination;
24. KPI dapat digunakan sebagai input E5;
25. KPI dapat digunakan sebagai input E7;
26. KPI dapat digunakan sebagai improvement signal E8;
27. KPI dapat menjadi maturity evidence E9;
28. KPI dapat menjadi evidence E10 tanpa menjadi sole success criterion;
29. framework dapat diwariskan tanpa memaksakan PD-03 metric catalogue ke  
    Platform lain.

⸻

**55\. Source-Fidelity Qualification**

**SOURCE-SUPPORTED**

Source mendukung:

- E4 sebagai KPI & Success Metrics framework;
- objective → criterion → metric → KPI architecture;
- KPI sebagai selected metric;
- supporting/diagnostic metric distinction;
- KPI ownership;
- metric coverage;
- leading/lagging distinction;
- target/measurement separation;
- KPI lifecycle;
- KPI catalogue structure;
- KPI reporting context;
- KPI versus Performance Health distinction.

PD-03 source mendukung:

- Governance & Compliance sebagai primary domain;
- Policy responsibility;
- Control responsibility;
- Certification responsibility;
- Governance Standards;
- Governance Baseline;
- Architecture/Security/Quality sebagai dependencies;
- preservation of other Platform ownership;
- Control ≠ Execution;
- Certification ≠ Implementation.

**BOUNDED CANONICAL SYNTHESIS**

E4 menerapkan reference KPI framework tersebut secara bounded ke:

Governance Policy

Governance Control

Certification

Compliance

Governance Standards

Governance Baseline

Synthesis ini tidak menciptakan organizational ownership baru.

**SOURCE GAP**

Authoritative source yang tersedia belum membekukan:

- final PD-03 KPI catalogue;
- final PD-03 metric catalogue;
- exact KPI definitions;
- exact formulas;
- exact targets;
- exact thresholds;
- exact frequencies;
- exact evidence sources;
- exact KPI owners;
- exact KPI lifecycle implementation.

Semua elemen tersebut harus tetap SOURCE GAP sampai diverifikasi.

⸻

BOUNDED CANONICAL SYNTHESIS

GOLD STANDARD REVIEW

PASS

FREEZE DECISION

APPROVED — SOURCE-BOUNDED

STATUS

FROZEN

**58\. Canonical Freeze Statement**

E4 — KPI & Success Metrics establishes the controlled translation layer through which approved PD-03 Governance & Compliance objectives and success criteria are translated into measurable success metrics, selected KPIs, supporting metrics, and diagnostic metrics.

E4 preserves the distinction between objective, criterion, metric, KPI, target, measurement result, and performance evaluation.

For PD-03, the KPI framework is bounded by the Governance & Compliance domain and its assigned Policy, Control, Certification, Governance Standards, and Governance Baseline responsibilities.

Governance interaction with Architecture, Security, Quality, and other Platform Divisions does not transfer domain ownership. Control measurement does not become execution ownership; Certification measurement does not become implementation ownership.

No PD-03-specific KPI, target, threshold, formula, frequency, evidence source, or final KPI catalogue is fabricated where authoritative source support is absent. Such elements remain controlled SOURCE GAP pending reconciliation.

E4 is therefore FROZEN as the KPI & Success Metrics Architecture for Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

⸻

**Final Status**

E4 — KPI & Success Metrics: FROZEN — SOURCE-BOUNDED

E5

\# Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance

\# E5 — Performance Review

\> \*\*Section ID:\*\* E5

\> \*\*Part:\*\* Part E — Performance Architecture

\> \*\*Status:\*\* FROZEN

\> \*\*Version:\*\* 1.0

\> \*\*Freeze Decision:\*\* APPROVED — SOURCE-BOUNDED

\> \*\*Classification:\*\* Performance Review Architecture

\> \*\*Successor:\*\* E6 — Performance Governance

1\. Purpose

E5 menetapkan bagaimana hasil performance PD-03:

• dievaluasi;

• direview;

• diinterpretasikan;

• diklasifikasikan;

• didokumentasikan;

• dieskalasikan apabila material;

• diterjemahkan menjadi improvement signal.

E5 menjawab:

"Bagaimana performance Governance & Compliance dievaluasi dan direview secara evidence-based?"

E5 tidak menciptakan objective baru.

E5 tidak menciptakan KPI baru.

E5 tidak menciptakan governance authority baru.

E5 tidak mengambil alih operational execution.

⸻

2\. Constitutional Position

Canonical Part E relationship:

E1 — Performance Constitution

&nbsp; ↓

"What is performance?"

&nbsp; ↓

E2 — Performance Model

&nbsp; ↓

"What dimensions describe performance?"

&nbsp; ↓

E3 — Performance Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

"How is performance measured?"

&nbsp; ↓

E4 — KPI & Success Metrics

&nbsp; ↓

"What indicates success?"

&nbsp; ↓

E5 — Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

"How is performance evaluated?"

&nbsp; ↓

E6 — Performance Governance

&nbsp; ↓

"Who governs, owns, reviews, and escalates?"

E5 menggunakan output E3 dan E4.

E5 menghasilkan assessment dan review evidence untuk E6 serta downstream  
improvement/reporting mechanism.

⸻

3\. Core Review Principle

Performance Review converts validated performance evidence into a controlled, traceable, and appropriately bounded assessment of Governance & Compliance performance.

Performance Review bukan sekadar:

• dashboard inspection;

• KPI inspection;

• score aggregation;

• compliance checklist;

• reporting meeting.

Performance Review harus menghasilkan, apabila didukung evidence:

• assessment;

• gap identification;

• interpretation;

• finding;

• review outcome;

• escalation signal;

• improvement signal;

• follow-up requirement.

⸻

4\. Review Boundary

E5 berada setelah measurement dan KPI architecture.

E3 — Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

E4 — KPI / Success Metrics

&nbsp; ↓

E5 — Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment / Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement / Escalation Signal

&nbsp; ↓

E6 — Performance Governance

E5 tidak menjadi:

Governance Authority

Architecture Authority

Operational Authority

Strategic Authority

E5 hanya menjalankan review mechanism dalam authority yang telah diberikan.

⸻

5\. PD-03 Review Scope

PD-03 E5 berfokus pada performance dari domain:

Governance & Compliance

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├── Governance Policy

&nbsp; ├── Governance Control

&nbsp; ├── Certification

&nbsp; ├── Compliance

&nbsp; ├── Governance Standards

&nbsp; └── Governance Baseline

Scope tersebut merupakan domain adaptation dari organizational responsibility  
PD-03.

Policy harus dipahami sebagai Governance Policy, bukan seluruh policy  
dalam AIOS.

Control harus dipahami sebagai Governance Control, bukan seluruh  
operational atau technical control.

Certification tidak sama dengan implementation ownership.

Governance Standards tidak sama dengan seluruh technical standards.

⸻

6\. Review Objective

Setiap PD-03 Performance Review harus memiliki objective yang eksplisit.

Review objective harus menjawab:

0\. apa yang sedang direview;

0\. mengapa direview;

0\. kondisi apa yang ingin dinilai;

0\. criteria apa yang digunakan;

0\. evidence apa yang diperlukan;

0\. siapa yang memiliki review responsibility;

0\. apa potential outcome-nya.

Jika objective tidak dapat ditentukan:

REVIEW BLOCKED

dan jangan membuat conclusion.

⸻

7\. Review Criteria

Review harus memiliki comparison basis.

Possible criteria source:

Approved Objective

&nbsp; ↓

Success Criterion

&nbsp; ↓

KPI / Metric

&nbsp; ↓

Target / Expected Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Observed Result

Untuk Governance & Compliance:

Governance Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Expected Governance Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Observed Condition

Criteria tidak boleh diciptakan secara ad hoc hanya untuk membuat result  
terlihat successful atau failed.

⸻

8\. Evidence Requirement

Setiap material review conclusion harus memiliki evidence.

Minimum chain:

Review ID

&nbsp; ↓

Objective

&nbsp; ↓

Criterion

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Outcome

Evidence harus dapat ditelusuri kembali kepada source yang digunakan.

⸻

9\. Evidence Sufficiency

Review harus menentukan apakah evidence cukup untuk conclusion.

Possible states:

Sufficient Evidence

Partially Sufficient

Insufficient Evidence

Conflicting Evidence

Evidence Pending

Evidence Invalid

Jika evidence insufficient:

Do not manufacture a performance conclusion.

Review dapat menghasilkan:

FURTHER EVIDENCE REQUIRED

atau:

INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE

⸻

10\. Governance Policy Review

Governance Policy Review menilai kondisi Governance Policy berdasarkan  
criteria yang berlaku.

Possible review dimensions:

Policy Applicability

Policy Adoption

Policy Adherence

Policy Exception

Policy Effectiveness

Policy Review Status

Review harus membedakan:

Policy Exists

&nbsp; ≠

Policy Adopted

&nbsp; ≠

Policy Followed

&nbsp; ≠

Policy Effective

PD-03 tidak mengambil ownership atas technical/domain policy yang berada  
pada applicable domain owner.

⸻

11\. Governance Control Review

Governance Control Review menilai kondisi control dalam governance boundary.

Possible dimensions:

Control Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Control Design

&nbsp; ↓

Control Operation

&nbsp; ↓

Control Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Control Effectiveness

Review tidak boleh mengubah:

Governance Control

menjadi:

Operational Control Execution Ownership

Jika finding menunjukkan implementation weakness pada Platform lain:

PD-03 Review Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Domain Review / Corrective Action

Ownership tetap pada domain yang applicable.

⸻

12\. Certification Review

Certification Review menilai certification-related performance.

Possible dimensions:

Certification Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Readiness

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence Completeness

&nbsp; ↓

Certification Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Finding

Review tidak memberikan PD-03 implementation ownership.

Certification

&nbsp; ≠

Implementation

Platform yang menjadi subject certification tetap mempertahankan ownership  
atas domain execution.

⸻

13\. Compliance Review

Compliance Review menilai apakah applicable governance requirement telah  
dipenuhi berdasarkan evidence yang legitimate.

Canonical flow:

Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Applicability

&nbsp; ↓

Expected Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Observed Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Compliance Assessment

Possible result:

Compliant

Partially Compliant

Non-Compliant

Not Applicable

Insufficient Evidence

Under Review

Status tersebut harus memiliki basis yang dapat ditelusuri.

⸻

14\. Governance Standards Review

Governance Standards dapat digunakan sebagai review criteria apabila  
applicable.

Governance Standard

&nbsp; ↓

Expected Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Observed Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

PD-03 tidak otomatis menjadi owner atas technical implementation standard.

Jika standard merupakan technical domain standard:

Governance Reference

&nbsp; +

Domain Ownership

harus tetap dibedakan.

⸻

15\. Governance Baseline Review

Governance Baseline dapat digunakan sebagai canonical reference condition.

Governance Baseline

&nbsp; ↓

Expected Governance State

&nbsp; ↓

Observed State

&nbsp; ↓

Variance

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

E5 tidak mengubah Governance Baseline.

E5 hanya mengevaluasi condition terhadap baseline apabila applicable.

⸻

16\. Review of Governance Effectiveness

Governance Performance tidak cukup dinilai dari keberadaan dokumen.

Review harus membedakan:

Governance Artifact Exists

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Mechanism Operates

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Mechanism Produces Intended Condition

Dengan demikian:

Documentation Completeness

&nbsp; ≠

Governance Effectiveness

⸻

17\. Review Dimensions

PD-03 E5 menggunakan performance dimensions yang diwariskan dari E2,  
dengan domain-specific interpretation.

Strategic

Operational

Governance

Capability

Improvement

Namun Governance & Compliance tetap menjadi primary domain.

Contoh:

Governance Performance

&nbsp; ↓

Policy

Control

Certification

Compliance

Standards

Baseline

Strategic, Operational, Capability, dan Improvement digunakan sebagai  
contextual lenses apabila legitimate dan tidak memindahkan domain ownership.

⸻

18\. Integrated PD-03 Assessment

Canonical model:

Policy Assessment

&nbsp; +

Control Assessment

&nbsp; +

Certification Assessment

&nbsp; +

Compliance Assessment

&nbsp; +

Governance Standards Assessment

&nbsp; +

Governance Baseline Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Integrated Governance & Compliance Assessment

Assessment tidak boleh menggunakan simple average untuk menghapus material  
weakness.

Material weakness harus tetap terlihat.

⸻

19\. Performance Score vs Governance Health

PD-03 harus membedakan:

Performance Score

Nilai hasil pengukuran tertentu.

Governance & Compliance Health

Interpretasi integrated terhadap kondisi Governance & Compliance.

Model:

Metric / KPI Results

&nbsp; ↓

Domain Assessments

&nbsp; ↓

Cross-Domain Analysis

&nbsp; ↓

Materiality

&nbsp; ↓

Context

&nbsp; ↓

Governance & Compliance Health

Satu KPI tidak boleh menjadi satu-satunya dasar Governance & Compliance  
Health.

⸻

20\. Trend Review

Review harus mempertimbangkan temporal condition apabila data tersedia.

Previous Result

&nbsp; ↓

Current Result

&nbsp; ↓

Trend

&nbsp; ↓

Expected Direction

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

Possible trend:

Improving

Stable

Declining

Volatile

Insufficient Evidence

Current success tidak selalu berarti sustainable success.

Current weakness tidak selalu berarti structural failure.

Context harus diperhitungkan.

⸻

21\. Diagnostic Classification

Material performance gap harus dianalisis tanpa premature attribution.

Possible classifications:

Governance Policy Gap

Governance Control Gap

Certification Gap

Compliance Gap

Governance Standards Gap

Governance Baseline Gap

Measurement Gap

Capability Gap

External Constraint

Cross-Platform Dependency

Jika root cause belum diverifikasi:

Potential Cause

Suspected Cause

Cause Under Investigation

jangan gunakan:

Confirmed Root Cause

tanpa evidence yang memadai.

⸻

22\. Governance Failure vs Performance Failure

E5 harus mempertahankan distinction:

Performance Failure

&nbsp; ≠

Governance Failure

Contoh:

Performance Below Target

&nbsp; ↓

Investigation

&nbsp; ├── Measurement Failure

&nbsp; ├── Capability Gap

&nbsp; ├── Operational Issue

&nbsp; ├── Governance Failure

&nbsp; └── External Constraint

Performance Review tidak boleh secara otomatis menyatakan governance violation  
hanya karena KPI rendah.

⸻

23\. Measurement Failure

Jika measurement mechanism invalid:

Measurement Invalid

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Conclusion Blocked

Jangan:

Invalid Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Assume Failure

atau:

Invalid Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Assume Success

Measurement integrity adalah prerequisite untuk trustworthy conclusion.

⸻

24\. Finding Architecture

Setiap material finding harus memiliki:

Finding ID

Review ID

Objective

Criterion

Observed Condition

Expected Condition

Evidence

Materiality

Impact

Trend

Confidence

Potential Cause

Owner

Recommended Response

Status

Finding harus dapat ditelusuri kembali kepada evidence.

⸻

25\. Finding Materiality

Materiality menentukan treatment.

Possible classification:

Informational

Minor

Material

Critical

Materiality harus memiliki rationale.

Critical finding tidak boleh disembunyikan oleh positive findings lain.

⸻

26\. Finding Confidence

Finding dapat memiliki confidence:

High

Medium

Low

Insufficient Evidence

Confidence harus mencerminkan evidence quality.

Confidence tidak boleh digunakan untuk memperkuat conclusion yang evidence-nya  
lemah.

⸻

27\. Review Outcome

Possible outcomes:

No Action Required

Continue Monitoring

Further Evidence Required

Corrective Action Required

Improvement Action Required

Governance Review Required

Cross-Platform Review Required

Escalation Required

Architecture Review Required

Strategic Review Required

Outcome harus sesuai evidence dan authority boundary.

⸻

28\. Improvement Signal

Performance Review dapat menghasilkan improvement signal.

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Need

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Signal

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Improvement Owner

E5 tidak otomatis mengeksekusi improvement.

Implementation mengikuti applicable owner dan authority.

⸻

29\. Escalation Signal

Material findings dapat menghasilkan escalation signal.

Material Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Authority / Ownership Analysis

&nbsp; ↓

Escalation Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Authority

E5 dapat mengidentifikasi kebutuhan escalation.

E5 tidak menciptakan escalation authority baru.

⸻

30\. Cross-Platform Finding

Jika PD-03 review menemukan condition yang berada pada domain Platform lain:

PD-03 Review

&nbsp; ↓

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Cross-Platform Dependency

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Domain Review

PD-03 tetap menjadi governance review owner dalam scope-nya.

Cross-platform interaction tidak berarti ownership transfer.

⸻

31\. Architecture Dependency

Jika Governance & Compliance finding menunjukkan architecture dependency:

PD-03 Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Architecture Dependency

&nbsp; ↓

Architecture Review

&nbsp; ↓

Architecture Decision

E5 tidak mengubah architecture.

Architecture authority tetap berada pada applicable architecture authority.

⸻

32\. Governance Dependency

Jika finding menunjukkan governance mechanism weakness:

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Concern

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Authority

E5 tidak menggantikan Governance Architecture.

E5 menyediakan evidence dan assessment.

⸻

33\. Review Roles

Minimum logical roles:

Role tidak otomatis berarti authority.

Formal authority tetap mengikuti governance instrument yang berlaku.

⸻

34\. Performance Owner

Performance Owner dapat:

• menyiapkan review;

• mengelola evidence;

• melakukan assessment;

• mengidentifikasi gap;

• menghasilkan finding;

• menghasilkan recommendation;

• menghasilkan improvement signal;

• menginisiasi escalation.

Performance Owner tidak otomatis dapat:

• mengubah governance policy;

• mengubah governance authority;

• mengubah architecture;

• mengubah strategic direction;

• mengambil alih operational execution;

• memindahkan ownership.

⸻

35\. Domain Owner

Domain Owner bertanggung jawab menyediakan:

• evidence;

• context;

• explanation;

• domain result;

• response information.

Domain Owner tetap mempertahankan ownership atas domain yang diukur.

Performance failure tidak otomatis memindahkan accountability.

⸻

36\. Reviewer Independence

Reviewer memberikan:

• challenge;

• validation;

• evidence scrutiny;

• assessment challenge;

• conclusion review.

Independence harus proporsional terhadap:

• materiality;

• sensitivity;

• authority;

• conflict risk.

Jika reviewer memiliki conflict yang material:

REVIEW INDEPENDENCE CONCERN

harus dicatat.

⸻

37\. Executive Review Interface

Performance Review memiliki interface dengan Executive Review.

Canonical model:

PD-03 Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Integrated Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Material Findings

&nbsp; ↓

Executive Review

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Response

Performance Review menyediakan evidence dan assessment.

Executive authority menentukan response apabila matter berada dalam authority  
boundary-nya.

Reference review model menetapkan Performance Review sebagai formal review  
type dengan Performance Owner sebagai primary owner dan Executive Authority  
sebagai final authority.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

38\. Review Independence from Governance Decision

Performance Review:

Measure

Evaluate

Review

Recommend

Verify

Governance authority:

Decide

Authorize

Direct

Recommendation tidak boleh ditulis sebagai decision.

Gunakan status:

Recommendation

Decision Pending

Awaiting Authority Decision

apabila final authority belum bertindak.

⸻

39\. Review Cycle

Reference E5 menyediakan review cycle architecture:

Continuous Review

&nbsp; ↓

Periodic Review

&nbsp; ↓

Strategic / Executive Review

&nbsp; ↓

Event-Based Review

Untuk PD-03, exact cadence belum dibekukan oleh authoritative PD-03 source  
yang tersedia.

Karena itu:

PD-03 Review Cadence

\= SOURCE GAP

Jangan mengarang:

• weekly;

• monthly;

• quarterly;

• annual;

sebagai mandatory PD-03 cadence sebelum source authoritative ditemukan.

⸻

40\. Continuous Review

Continuous Review dapat digunakan apabila material signal muncul.

Possible trigger:

Critical KPI Deterioration

Material Compliance Gap

Certification Concern

Governance Control Failure

Significant Policy Exception

Material Evidence Anomaly

Critical Trend Change

Continuous Review tidak otomatis berarti governance decision.

⸻

41\. Event-Based Review

Event-based review dapat dipicu oleh:

• material governance change;

• certification event;

• major compliance concern;

• critical control failure;

• material policy change;

• major cross-platform governance issue;

• significant performance anomaly.

Event trigger harus legitimate dan traceable.

⸻

42\. Review Lifecycle

Canonical lifecycle:

Planned

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence Collection

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Validation

&nbsp; ↓

Review

&nbsp; ↓

Outcome

&nbsp; ↓

Action / Escalation

&nbsp; ↓

Follow-up

&nbsp; ↓

Closed

Possible blocked states:

Blocked

Deferred

Escalated

Evidence Pending

Authority Pending

Blocked status harus memiliki reason.

⸻

43\. Review Closure

Review tidak boleh ditutup hanya karena meeting selesai.

Closure membutuhkan:

Review Completed

&nbsp; +

Required Findings Recorded

&nbsp; +

Outcome Recorded

&nbsp; +

Actions / Escalations Recorded

&nbsp; +

Follow-up Requirement Determined

Material unresolved condition tidak boleh disembunyikan dengan status  
Closed.

⸻

44\. Follow-Up Review

Follow-up digunakan untuk memverifikasi:

• action completion;

• result change;

• effectiveness;

• residual gap;

• recurrence;

• regression.

Canonical:

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Action

&nbsp; ↓

Implementation

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Follow-Up Review

&nbsp; ↓

Effectiveness Assessment

Completion tidak sama dengan effectiveness.

Action Completed

&nbsp; ≠

Condition Improved

⸻

45\. Review Documentation

Setiap formal review wajib mempertahankan:

• Review Identifier;

• Review Type;

• Review Owner;

• Scope;

• Objective;

• Criteria;

• Evidence;

• Assessment;

• Findings;

• Outcome;

• Recommendation;

• Decision status;

• Improvement Actions;

• Escalation;

• Follow-up;

• Completion Status;

• Review History.

Dokumentasi harus dapat diaudit.

⸻

46\. Review Traceability

Minimum traceability:

Review

&nbsp;↓

Objective

&nbsp;↓

Criterion

&nbsp;↓

Metric / KPI

&nbsp;↓

Evidence

&nbsp;↓

Measurement

&nbsp;↓

Assessment

&nbsp;↓

Finding

&nbsp;↓

Outcome

&nbsp;↓

Action / Escalation

&nbsp;↓

Follow-Up

Jika satu link tidak tersedia, gap traceability harus terlihat.

⸻

47\. Auditability

Review record harus mempertahankan historical integrity.

Material correction harus memiliki audit trail.

Historical result tidak boleh dimutasi secara silent.

Jika conclusion berubah:

Original Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Revision Reason

&nbsp; ↓

New Evidence / Correction

&nbsp; ↓

Revised Assessment

harus dapat ditelusuri.

⸻

48\. Review Quality Gate

Setiap PD-03 Performance Review harus melewati:

Q-01 — Evidence Sufficiency

Apakah evidence cukup?

Q-02 — Criteria Validity

Apakah criteria legitimate?

Q-03 — Measurement Integrity

Apakah measurement valid?

Q-04 — Context Completeness

Apakah material context tersedia?

Q-05 — Independence

Apakah review memiliki independence yang memadai?

Q-06 — Materiality

Apakah finding material?

Q-07 — Authority

Apakah outcome berada dalam authority yang benar?

Q-08 — Traceability

Apakah conclusion dapat ditelusuri?

⸻

49\. Review Failure States

Conclusion harus ditahan apabila:

Insufficient Evidence

Measurement Invalid

Criteria Missing

Objective Ambiguous

Authority Unclear

Material Context Missing

Review Independence Compromised

Output:

REVIEW BLOCKED

atau:

INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE

sesuai condition aktual.

⸻

50\. Anti-Gaming

PD-03 Performance Review harus mencegah:

• target manipulation;

• selective evidence;

• denominator manipulation;

• hiding exceptions;

• retrospective KPI redefinition;

• silent historical correction;

• aggregation masking;

• premature closure;

• false root-cause attribution;

• recommendation presented as decision.

Review harus mempertahankan context dan limitation.

⸻

51\. Governance Review vs Performance Review

Performance Review tidak menggantikan Governance Review.

⸻

52\. Performance Review vs Architecture Review

Jika finding menunjukkan architecture dependency:

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Architecture Dependency

&nbsp; ↓

Architecture Review

PD-03 Performance Review tidak boleh:

• mengubah architecture;

• mengubah architecture decision;

• mengubah architecture authority.

⸻

53\. Performance Review vs Operational Execution

Performance Review:

Identify

Evaluate

Review

Recommend

Verify

Operational owner:

Plan

Execute

Correct

Deliver

Boundary:

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Operational Execution

&nbsp; ↓

New Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measurement

Performance Management bukan operational executor.

54\. Performance Review vs Certification Decision

Certification-related performance evidence dapat digunakan oleh review.

Namun:

&nbsp; ≠

Certification Decision

Compliance assessment dapat menjadi output review.

&nbsp; ≠

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Governance Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Decision

57\. Performance Review vs Control Change

Jika review menemukan control weakness:

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Control Improvement Signal

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Control Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Change

PD-03 tidak otomatis menjalankan implementation.

58\. Performance Review vs Certification Improvement

Certification gap:

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Certification Improvement Signal

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Action

&nbsp; ↓

Re-evaluation

59\. Performance Review Health Statement

PD-03 E5 dapat menghasilkan integrated health statement apabila evidence  
cukup.

Contoh structure:

Governance & Compliance Health

&nbsp; ↓

Policy Condition

Control Condition

Certification Condition

Compliance Condition

Standards Condition

Baseline Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Materiality

&nbsp; ↓

Trend

&nbsp; ↓

Confidence

60\. Review Confidence

Review conclusion dapat memiliki confidence:

High

Medium

Low

Insufficient Evidence

Confidence harus berdasarkan:

• evidence quality;

• evidence completeness;

• measurement reliability;

• source reliability;

• review independence;

• context completeness.

⸻

61\. Conditional Assessment

Jika sebagian domain evidence tersedia:

CONDITIONALLY ASSESSED

dapat digunakan.

Conditional assessment tidak boleh disajikan sebagai full-confidence conclusion.

⸻

62\. Review Result States

Possible states:

Meets Expectation

Partially Meets Expectation

Below Expectation

Material Concern

Critical Concern

Insufficient Evidence

Not Applicable

Under Review

State harus mempunyai evidence basis.

⸻

63\. Review Escalation Threshold

Exact numerical threshold tidak dibekukan pada E5 PD-03 apabila source  
authoritative tidak menetapkannya.

Karena itu:

Numerical Threshold

\= SOURCE GAP

Materiality dan escalation criteria harus berasal dari applicable authoritative  
governance mechanism.

Jangan mengarang angka threshold.

⸻

64\. Review Cadence Source Gap

PD-03-specific cadence belum authoritative pada source yang tersedia.

Gunakan:

Cadence

\= SOURCE GAP

Reference cadence boleh digunakan sebagai implementation pattern, tetapi tidak  
boleh dipresentasikan sebagai mandatory PD-03 rule.

⸻

PD-03 review catalogue harus dibangun dari source authoritative.

Domain

Trigger

Outcome

Escalation Path

Status

Nilai konkret harus diverifikasi sebelum freeze.

67\. Non-Goals

E5 tidak bertujuan untuk:

• mengubah governance mandate;

68\. Success Criteria

E5 PD-03 dianggap berhasil apabila:

0\. setiap formal review memiliki objective;

0\. setiap review memiliki legitimate criteria;

0\. evidence dapat ditelusuri;

0\. measurement integrity diverifikasi;

0\. insufficient evidence dapat menghentikan conclusion;

0\. Governance Policy dapat direview dalam scope yang benar;

0\. Governance Control dapat direview tanpa mengambil implementation ownership;

0\. Certification dapat direview tanpa mengambil implementation ownership;

0\. Compliance dapat direview berdasarkan applicable requirement;

0\. Governance Standards dapat digunakan sebagai criteria apabila applicable;

0\. Governance Baseline dapat digunakan sebagai reference condition;

0\. material findings dapat diklasifikasikan;

0\. potential cause dibedakan dari confirmed cause;

0\. performance failure dibedakan dari governance failure;

0\. measurement failure dibedakan dari execution failure;

0\. cross-platform finding mempertahankan ownership boundary;

0\. architecture dependency dapat diteruskan ke Architecture Review;

0\. governance concern dapat diteruskan ke Governance Review;

0\. material findings dapat dieskalasikan;

0\. recommendation dapat dibedakan dari decision;

0\. review authority dapat dibedakan dari review ownership;

0\. review records dapat diaudit;

0\. historical correction memiliki audit trail;

0\. follow-up dapat memverifikasi effectiveness;

0\. completion dibedakan dari effectiveness;

0\. review dapat diblokir apabila evidence tidak memadai;

0\. anti-gaming controls tersedia;

0\. exact PD-03 cadence tidak diinvent;

0\. exact PD-03 thresholds tidak diinvent;

0\. framework dapat diwariskan tanpa memindahkan authority atau ownership.

⸻

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Policy

Control

Certification

Compliance

Governance Standards

Governance Baseline

tanpa memperluas authority atau execution ownership.
