72\. Canonical Freeze Statement

E5 — Performance Review establishes the controlled mechanism through which PD-03 Governance & Compliance evaluates measured performance evidence against legitimate criteria, produces traceable assessments and findings, identifies improvement or escalation signals, and preserves the authority and ownership boundaries of Governance & Compliance and other AIOS Platforms.

E5 covers Governance Policy, Governance Control, Certification, Compliance, Governance Standards, and Governance Baseline only within their established PD-03 boundaries. Governance responsibility does not become implementation ownership.

Performance Review does not create governance authority, architecture authority, strategic authority, or operational authority. Recommendation is not decision; assessment is not authorization; finding is not sanction.

Where evidence is insufficient, criteria are invalid, authority is unclear, or material context is missing, E5 must withhold or qualify the conclusion rather than manufacture a result.

PD-03-specific cadence, thresholds, reviewer assignments, KPI catalogue, and other implementation details that are not supported by authoritative source remain controlled SOURCE GAP and must not be invented.

E5 is therefore FROZEN as the Performance Review Architecture for Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

⸻

Final Status

E5 — Performance Review: FROZEN — SOURCE-BOUNDED

**E5**

\# Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance

\# Part E — Performance Architecture

\# E5 — Performance Review

\> \*\*Section ID:\*\* E5

\> \*\*Section Name:\*\* Performance Review

\> \*\*Platform ID:\*\* PD-03

\> \*\*Official Name:\*\* Governance & Compliance

\> \*\*Volume:\*\* Volume 3

\> \*\*Part:\*\* Part E — Performance Architecture

\> \*\*Status:\*\* FROZEN

\> \*\*Version:\*\* 1.0

\> \*\*Gold Standard Review:\*\* PASS

\> \*\*Freeze Decision:\*\* APPROVED — SOURCE-BOUNDED

\> \*\*Classification:\*\* Performance Review Architecture

\> \*\*Reference Role:\*\* Domain-Adapted Reference Implementation

\> \*\*Parent Sections:\*\* E1 — Performance Constitution; E2 — Performance Model; E3 — Performance Measurement; E4 — KPI & Success Metrics

\> \*\*Successor:\*\* E6 — Performance Governance

**1\. Purpose**

E5 menetapkan bagaimana hasil performance PD-03:

- dievaluasi;
- direview;
- diinterpretasikan;
- diklasifikasikan;
- didokumentasikan;
- dieskalasikan apabila material;
- diterjemahkan menjadi improvement signal.

E5 menjawab:

"Bagaimana performance Governance & Compliance dievaluasi dan direview secara evidence-based?"

E5 tidak menciptakan objective baru.

E5 tidak menciptakan KPI baru.

E5 tidak menciptakan governance authority baru.

E5 tidak mengambil alih operational execution.

⸻

**2\. Constitutional Position**

Canonical Part E relationship:

E1 — Performance Constitution

&nbsp; ↓

"What is performance?"

&nbsp; ↓

E2 — Performance Model

&nbsp; ↓

"What dimensions describe performance?"

&nbsp; ↓

E3 — Performance Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

"How is performance measured?"

&nbsp; ↓

E4 — KPI & Success Metrics

&nbsp; ↓

"What indicates success?"

&nbsp; ↓

E5 — Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

"How is performance evaluated?"

&nbsp; ↓

E6 — Performance Governance

&nbsp; ↓

"Who governs, owns, reviews, and escalates?"

E5 menggunakan output E3 dan E4.

E5 menghasilkan assessment dan review evidence untuk E6 serta downstream  
improvement/reporting mechanism.

⸻

**3\. Core Review Principle**

Performance Review converts validated performance evidence into a controlled, traceable, and appropriately bounded assessment of Governance & Compliance performance.

Performance Review bukan sekadar:

- dashboard inspection;
- KPI inspection;
- score aggregation;
- compliance checklist;
- reporting meeting.

Performance Review harus menghasilkan, apabila didukung evidence:

- assessment;
- gap identification;
- interpretation;
- finding;
- review outcome;
- escalation signal;
- improvement signal;
- follow-up requirement.

⸻

**4\. Review Boundary**

E5 berada setelah measurement dan KPI architecture.

E3 — Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

E4 — KPI / Success Metrics

&nbsp; ↓

E5 — Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment / Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement / Escalation Signal

&nbsp; ↓

E6 — Performance Governance

E5 tidak menjadi:

Governance Authority

Architecture Authority

Operational Authority

Strategic Authority

E5 hanya menjalankan review mechanism dalam authority yang telah diberikan.

⸻

**5\. PD-03 Review Scope**

PD-03 E5 berfokus pada performance dari domain:

Governance & Compliance

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├── Governance Policy

&nbsp; ├── Governance Control

&nbsp; ├── Certification

&nbsp; ├── Compliance

&nbsp; ├── Governance Standards

&nbsp; └── Governance Baseline

Scope tersebut merupakan domain adaptation dari organizational responsibility  
PD-03.

Policy harus dipahami sebagai Governance Policy, bukan seluruh policy  
dalam AIOS.

Control harus dipahami sebagai Governance Control, bukan seluruh  
operational atau technical control.

Certification tidak sama dengan implementation ownership.

Governance Standards tidak sama dengan seluruh technical standards.

⸻

**6\. Review Objective**

Setiap PD-03 Performance Review harus memiliki objective yang eksplisit.

Review objective harus menjawab:

1. apa yang sedang direview;
2. mengapa direview;
3. kondisi apa yang ingin dinilai;
4. criteria apa yang digunakan;
5. evidence apa yang diperlukan;
6. siapa yang memiliki review responsibility;
7. apa potential outcome-nya.

Jika objective tidak dapat ditentukan:

REVIEW BLOCKED

dan jangan membuat conclusion.

⸻

**7\. Review Criteria**

Review harus memiliki comparison basis.

Possible criteria source:

Approved Objective

&nbsp; ↓

Success Criterion

&nbsp; ↓

KPI / Metric

&nbsp; ↓

Target / Expected Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Observed Result

Untuk Governance & Compliance:

Governance Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Expected Governance Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Observed Condition

Criteria tidak boleh diciptakan secara ad hoc hanya untuk membuat result  
terlihat successful atau failed.

⸻

**8\. Evidence Requirement**

Setiap material review conclusion harus memiliki evidence.

Minimum chain:

Review ID

&nbsp; ↓

Objective

&nbsp; ↓

Criterion

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Outcome

Evidence harus dapat ditelusuri kembali kepada source yang digunakan.

⸻

**9\. Evidence Sufficiency**

Review harus menentukan apakah evidence cukup untuk conclusion.

Possible states:

Sufficient Evidence

Partially Sufficient

Insufficient Evidence

Conflicting Evidence

Evidence Pending

Evidence Invalid

Jika evidence insufficient:

Do not manufacture a performance conclusion.

Review dapat menghasilkan:

FURTHER EVIDENCE REQUIRED

atau:

INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE

⸻

**10\. Governance Policy Review**

Governance Policy Review menilai kondisi Governance Policy berdasarkan  
criteria yang berlaku.

Possible review dimensions:

Policy Applicability

Policy Adoption

Policy Adherence

Policy Exception

Policy Effectiveness

Policy Review Status

Review harus membedakan:

Policy Exists

&nbsp; ≠

Policy Adopted

&nbsp; ≠

Policy Followed

&nbsp; ≠

Policy Effective

PD-03 tidak mengambil ownership atas technical/domain policy yang berada  
pada applicable domain owner.

⸻

**11\. Governance Control Review**

Governance Control Review menilai kondisi control dalam governance boundary.

Possible dimensions:

Control Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Control Design

&nbsp; ↓

Control Operation

&nbsp; ↓

Control Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Control Effectiveness

Review tidak boleh mengubah:

Governance Control

menjadi:

Operational Control Execution Ownership

Jika finding menunjukkan implementation weakness pada Platform lain:

PD-03 Review Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Domain Review / Corrective Action

Ownership tetap pada domain yang applicable.

⸻

**12\. Certification Review**

Certification Review menilai certification-related performance.

Possible dimensions:

Certification Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Readiness

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence Completeness

&nbsp; ↓

Certification Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Finding

Review tidak memberikan PD-03 implementation ownership.

Certification

&nbsp; ≠

Implementation

Platform yang menjadi subject certification tetap mempertahankan ownership  
atas domain execution.

⸻

**13\. Compliance Review**

Compliance Review menilai apakah applicable governance requirement telah  
dipenuhi berdasarkan evidence yang legitimate.

Canonical flow:

Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Applicability

&nbsp; ↓

Expected Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Observed Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Compliance Assessment

Possible result:

Compliant

Partially Compliant

Non-Compliant

Not Applicable

Insufficient Evidence

Under Review

Status tersebut harus memiliki basis yang dapat ditelusuri.

⸻

**14\. Governance Standards Review**

Governance Standards dapat digunakan sebagai review criteria apabila  
applicable.

Governance Standard

&nbsp; ↓

Expected Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Observed Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

PD-03 tidak otomatis menjadi owner atas technical implementation standard.

Jika standard merupakan technical domain standard:

Governance Reference

&nbsp; +

Domain Ownership

harus tetap dibedakan.

⸻

**15\. Governance Baseline Review**

Governance Baseline dapat digunakan sebagai canonical reference condition.

Governance Baseline

&nbsp; ↓

Expected Governance State

&nbsp; ↓

Observed State

&nbsp; ↓

Variance

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

E5 tidak mengubah Governance Baseline.

E5 hanya mengevaluasi condition terhadap baseline apabila applicable.

⸻

**16\. Review of Governance Effectiveness**

Governance Performance tidak cukup dinilai dari keberadaan dokumen.

Review harus membedakan:

Governance Artifact Exists

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Mechanism Operates

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Mechanism Produces Intended Condition

Dengan demikian:

Documentation Completeness

&nbsp; ≠

Governance Effectiveness

⸻

**17\. Review Dimensions**

PD-03 E5 menggunakan performance dimensions yang diwariskan dari E2,  
dengan domain-specific interpretation.

Strategic

Operational

Governance

Capability

Improvement

Namun Governance & Compliance tetap menjadi primary domain.

Contoh:

Governance Performance

&nbsp; ↓

Policy

Control

Certification

Compliance

Standards

Baseline

Strategic, Operational, Capability, dan Improvement digunakan sebagai  
contextual lenses apabila legitimate dan tidak memindahkan domain ownership.

⸻

**18\. Integrated PD-03 Assessment**

Canonical model:

Policy Assessment

&nbsp; +

Control Assessment

&nbsp; +

Certification Assessment

&nbsp; +

Compliance Assessment

&nbsp; +

Governance Standards Assessment

&nbsp; +

Governance Baseline Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Integrated Governance & Compliance Assessment

Assessment tidak boleh menggunakan simple average untuk menghapus material  
weakness.

Material weakness harus tetap terlihat.

⸻

**19\. Performance Score vs Governance Health**

PD-03 harus membedakan:

Performance Score

Nilai hasil pengukuran tertentu.

Governance & Compliance Health

Interpretasi integrated terhadap kondisi Governance & Compliance.

Model:

Metric / KPI Results

&nbsp; ↓

Domain Assessments

&nbsp; ↓

Cross-Domain Analysis

&nbsp; ↓

Materiality

&nbsp; ↓

Context

&nbsp; ↓

Governance & Compliance Health

Satu KPI tidak boleh menjadi satu-satunya dasar Governance & Compliance  
Health.

⸻

**20\. Trend Review**

Review harus mempertimbangkan temporal condition apabila data tersedia.

Previous Result

&nbsp; ↓

Current Result

&nbsp; ↓

Trend

&nbsp; ↓

Expected Direction

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

Possible trend:

Improving

Stable

Declining

Volatile

Insufficient Evidence

Current success tidak selalu berarti sustainable success.

Current weakness tidak selalu berarti structural failure.

Context harus diperhitungkan.

⸻

**21\. Diagnostic Classification**

Material performance gap harus dianalisis tanpa premature attribution.

Possible classifications:

Governance Policy Gap

Governance Control Gap

Certification Gap

Compliance Gap

Governance Standards Gap

Governance Baseline Gap

Measurement Gap

Capability Gap

External Constraint

Cross-Platform Dependency

Jika root cause belum diverifikasi:

Potential Cause

Suspected Cause

Cause Under Investigation

jangan gunakan:

Confirmed Root Cause

tanpa evidence yang memadai.

⸻

**22\. Governance Failure vs Performance Failure**

E5 harus mempertahankan distinction:

Performance Failure

&nbsp; ≠

Governance Failure

Contoh:

Performance Below Target

&nbsp; ↓

Investigation

&nbsp; ├── Measurement Failure

&nbsp; ├── Capability Gap

&nbsp; ├── Operational Issue

&nbsp; ├── Governance Failure

&nbsp; └── External Constraint

Performance Review tidak boleh secara otomatis menyatakan governance violation  
hanya karena KPI rendah.

⸻

**23\. Measurement Failure**

Jika measurement mechanism invalid:

Measurement Invalid

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Conclusion Blocked

Jangan:

Invalid Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Assume Failure

atau:

Invalid Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Assume Success

Measurement integrity adalah prerequisite untuk trustworthy conclusion.

⸻

**24\. Finding Architecture**

Setiap material finding harus memiliki:

Finding ID

Review ID

Objective

Criterion

Observed Condition

Expected Condition

Evidence

Materiality

Impact

Trend

Confidence

Potential Cause

Owner

Recommended Response

Status

Finding harus dapat ditelusuri kembali kepada evidence.

⸻

**25\. Finding Materiality**

Materiality menentukan treatment.

Possible classification:

Informational

Minor

Material

Critical

Materiality harus memiliki rationale.

Critical finding tidak boleh disembunyikan oleh positive findings lain.

⸻

**26\. Finding Confidence**

Finding dapat memiliki confidence:

High

Medium

Low

Insufficient Evidence

Confidence harus mencerminkan evidence quality.

Confidence tidak boleh digunakan untuk memperkuat conclusion yang evidence-nya  
lemah.

⸻

**27\. Review Outcome**

Possible outcomes:

No Action Required

Continue Monitoring

Further Evidence Required

Corrective Action Required

Improvement Action Required

Governance Review Required

Cross-Platform Review Required

Escalation Required

Architecture Review Required

Strategic Review Required

Outcome harus sesuai evidence dan authority boundary.

⸻

**28\. Improvement Signal**

Performance Review dapat menghasilkan improvement signal.

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Need

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Signal

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Improvement Owner

E5 tidak otomatis mengeksekusi improvement.

Implementation mengikuti applicable owner dan authority.

⸻

**29\. Escalation Signal**

Material findings dapat menghasilkan escalation signal.

Material Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Authority / Ownership Analysis

&nbsp; ↓

Escalation Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Authority

E5 dapat mengidentifikasi kebutuhan escalation.

E5 tidak menciptakan escalation authority baru.

⸻

**30\. Cross-Platform Finding**

Jika PD-03 review menemukan condition yang berada pada domain Platform lain:

PD-03 Review

&nbsp; ↓

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Cross-Platform Dependency

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Domain Review

PD-03 tetap menjadi governance review owner dalam scope-nya.

Cross-platform interaction tidak berarti ownership transfer.

⸻

**31\. Architecture Dependency**

Jika Governance & Compliance finding menunjukkan architecture dependency:

PD-03 Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Architecture Dependency

&nbsp; ↓

Architecture Review

&nbsp; ↓

Architecture Decision

E5 tidak mengubah architecture.

Architecture authority tetap berada pada applicable architecture authority.

⸻

**32\. Governance Dependency**

Jika finding menunjukkan governance mechanism weakness:

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Concern

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Authority

E5 tidak menggantikan Governance Architecture.

E5 menyediakan evidence dan assessment.

⸻

**33\. Review Roles**

Minimum logical roles:

| **Role**             | **Responsibility**                 |
| -------------------- | ---------------------------------- |
| Performance Owner    | Mengelola review mechanism         |
| Domain Owner         | Menyediakan evidence dan context   |
| Reviewer             | Independent validation / challenge |
| Governance Owner     | Context dan governance response    |
| Executive Leadership | Menilai result dan response        |
| Executive Authority  | Final authority apabila applicable |
| Custodian            | Menjaga review record              |

Role tidak otomatis berarti authority.

Formal authority tetap mengikuti governance instrument yang berlaku.

⸻

**34\. Performance Owner**

Performance Owner dapat:

- menyiapkan review;
- mengelola evidence;
- melakukan assessment;
- mengidentifikasi gap;
- menghasilkan finding;
- menghasilkan recommendation;
- menghasilkan improvement signal;
- menginisiasi escalation.

Performance Owner tidak otomatis dapat:

- mengubah governance policy;
- mengubah governance authority;
- mengubah architecture;
- mengubah strategic direction;
- mengambil alih operational execution;
- memindahkan ownership.

⸻

**35\. Domain Owner**

Domain Owner bertanggung jawab menyediakan:

- evidence;
- context;
- explanation;
- domain result;
- response information.

Domain Owner tetap mempertahankan ownership atas domain yang diukur.

Performance failure tidak otomatis memindahkan accountability.

⸻

**36\. Reviewer Independence**

Reviewer memberikan:

- challenge;
- validation;
- evidence scrutiny;
- assessment challenge;
- conclusion review.

Independence harus proporsional terhadap:

- materiality;
- sensitivity;
- authority;
- conflict risk.

Jika reviewer memiliki conflict yang material:

REVIEW INDEPENDENCE CONCERN

harus dicatat.

⸻

**37\. Executive Review Interface**

Performance Review memiliki interface dengan Executive Review.

Canonical model:

PD-03 Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Integrated Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Material Findings

&nbsp; ↓

Executive Review

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Response

Performance Review menyediakan evidence dan assessment.

Executive authority menentukan response apabila matter berada dalam authority  
boundary-nya.

Reference review model menetapkan Performance Review sebagai formal review  
type dengan Performance Owner sebagai primary owner dan Executive Authority  
sebagai final authority.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

**38\. Review Independence from Governance Decision**

Performance Review:

Measure

Evaluate

Review

Recommend

Verify

Governance authority:

Decide

Authorize

Direct

Recommendation tidak boleh ditulis sebagai decision.

Gunakan status:

Recommendation

Decision Pending

Awaiting Authority Decision

apabila final authority belum bertindak.

⸻

**39\. Review Cycle**

Reference E5 menyediakan review cycle architecture:

Continuous Review

&nbsp; ↓

Periodic Review

&nbsp; ↓

Strategic / Executive Review

&nbsp; ↓

Event-Based Review

Untuk PD-03, exact cadence belum dibekukan oleh authoritative PD-03 source  
yang tersedia.

Karena itu:

PD-03 Review Cadence

\= SOURCE GAP

Jangan mengarang:

- weekly;
- monthly;
- quarterly;
- annual;

sebagai mandatory PD-03 cadence sebelum source authoritative ditemukan.

⸻

**40\. Continuous Review**

Continuous Review dapat digunakan apabila material signal muncul.

Possible trigger:

Critical KPI Deterioration

Material Compliance Gap

Certification Concern

Governance Control Failure

Significant Policy Exception

Material Evidence Anomaly

Critical Trend Change

Continuous Review tidak otomatis berarti governance decision.

⸻

**41\. Event-Based Review**

Event-based review dapat dipicu oleh:

- material governance change;
- certification event;
- major compliance concern;
- critical control failure;
- material policy change;
- major cross-platform governance issue;
- significant performance anomaly.

Event trigger harus legitimate dan traceable.

⸻

**42\. Review Lifecycle**

Canonical lifecycle:

Planned

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence Collection

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Validation

&nbsp; ↓

Review

&nbsp; ↓

Outcome

&nbsp; ↓

Action / Escalation

&nbsp; ↓

Follow-up

&nbsp; ↓

Closed

Possible blocked states:

Blocked

Deferred

Escalated

Evidence Pending

Authority Pending

Blocked status harus memiliki reason.

⸻

**43\. Review Closure**

Review tidak boleh ditutup hanya karena meeting selesai.

Closure membutuhkan:

Review Completed

&nbsp; +

Required Findings Recorded

&nbsp; +

Outcome Recorded

&nbsp; +

Actions / Escalations Recorded

&nbsp; +

Follow-up Requirement Determined

Material unresolved condition tidak boleh disembunyikan dengan status  
Closed.

⸻

**44\. Follow-Up Review**

Follow-up digunakan untuk memverifikasi:

- action completion;
- result change;
- effectiveness;
- residual gap;
- recurrence;
- regression.

Canonical:

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Action

&nbsp; ↓

Implementation

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Follow-Up Review

&nbsp; ↓

Effectiveness Assessment

Completion tidak sama dengan effectiveness.

Action Completed

&nbsp; ≠

Condition Improved

⸻

**45\. Review Documentation**

Setiap formal review wajib mempertahankan:

- Review Identifier;
- Review Type;
- Review Owner;
- Scope;
- Objective;
- Criteria;
- Evidence;
- Assessment;
- Findings;
- Outcome;
- Recommendation;
- Decision status;
- Improvement Actions;
- Escalation;
- Follow-up;
- Completion Status;
- Review History.

Dokumentasi harus dapat diaudit.

⸻

**46\. Review Traceability**

Minimum traceability:

Review

&nbsp;↓

Objective

&nbsp;↓

Criterion

&nbsp;↓

Metric / KPI

&nbsp;↓

Evidence

&nbsp;↓

Measurement

&nbsp;↓

Assessment

&nbsp;↓

Finding

&nbsp;↓

Outcome

&nbsp;↓

Action / Escalation

&nbsp;↓

Follow-Up

Jika satu link tidak tersedia, gap traceability harus terlihat.

⸻

**47\. Auditability**

Review record harus mempertahankan historical integrity.

Material correction harus memiliki audit trail.

Historical result tidak boleh dimutasi secara silent.

Jika conclusion berubah:

Original Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Revision Reason

&nbsp; ↓

New Evidence / Correction

&nbsp; ↓

Revised Assessment

harus dapat ditelusuri.

⸻

**48\. Review Quality Gate**

Setiap PD-03 Performance Review harus melewati:

Q-01 — Evidence Sufficiency

Apakah evidence cukup?

Q-02 — Criteria Validity

Apakah criteria legitimate?

Q-03 — Measurement Integrity

Apakah measurement valid?

Q-04 — Context Completeness

Apakah material context tersedia?

Q-05 — Independence

Apakah review memiliki independence yang memadai?

Q-06 — Materiality

Apakah finding material?

Q-07 — Authority

Apakah outcome berada dalam authority yang benar?

Q-08 — Traceability

Apakah conclusion dapat ditelusuri?

⸻

**49\. Review Failure States**

Conclusion harus ditahan apabila:

Insufficient Evidence

Measurement Invalid

Criteria Missing

Objective Ambiguous

Authority Unclear

Material Context Missing

Review Independence Compromised

Output:

REVIEW BLOCKED

atau:

INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE

sesuai condition aktual.

⸻

**50\. Anti-Gaming**

PD-03 Performance Review harus mencegah:

- target manipulation;
- selective evidence;
- denominator manipulation;
- hiding exceptions;
- retrospective KPI redefinition;
- silent historical correction;
- aggregation masking;
- premature closure;
- false root-cause attribution;
- recommendation presented as decision.

Review harus mempertahankan context dan limitation.

⸻

**51\. Governance Review vs Performance Review**

| **Aspect**       | **Performance Review**                       | **Governance Review**                                                        |
| ---------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Primary Question | Bagaimana performance terhadap expectation?  | Apakah governance mechanism/authority/control berjalan sebagaimana mestinya? |
| Input            | Metrics, evidence, outcomes                  | Governance evidence, policy, control, authority                              |
| Output           | Assessment, gap, finding, improvement signal | Governance assessment / governance outcome                                   |
| Authority        | Performance evaluation                       | Governance authority                                                         |
| Relationship     | Dapat memicu Governance Review               | Dapat menggunakan Performance evidence                                       |

Performance Review tidak menggantikan Governance Review.

⸻

**52\. Performance Review vs Architecture Review**

Jika finding menunjukkan architecture dependency:

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Architecture Dependency

&nbsp; ↓

Architecture Review

PD-03 Performance Review tidak boleh:

- mengubah architecture;
- mengubah architecture decision;
- mengubah architecture authority.

⸻

**53\. Performance Review vs Operational Execution**

Performance Review:

Identify

Evaluate

Review

Recommend

Verify

Operational owner:

Plan

Execute

Correct

Deliver

Boundary:

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Operational Execution

&nbsp; ↓

New Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measurement

Performance Management bukan operational executor.

⸻

**54\. Performance Review vs Certification Decision**

Certification-related performance evidence dapat digunakan oleh review.

Namun:

Performance Assessment

&nbsp; ≠

Certification Decision

Certification decision harus mengikuti applicable certification authority.

⸻

**55\. Performance Review vs Compliance Decision**

Compliance assessment dapat menjadi output review.

Namun:

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ≠

Automatic Sanction

Jika compliance condition membutuhkan formal governance action:

Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Governance Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Decision

⸻

**56\. Performance Review vs Policy Change**

Jika review menemukan policy weakness:

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Policy Improvement Signal

&nbsp; ↓

Policy Governance Process

E5 tidak mengubah policy secara unilateral.

⸻

**57\. Performance Review vs Control Change**

Jika review menemukan control weakness:

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Control Improvement Signal

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Control Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Change

PD-03 tidak otomatis menjalankan implementation.

⸻

**58\. Performance Review vs Certification Improvement**

Certification gap:

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Certification Improvement Signal

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Action

&nbsp; ↓

Re-evaluation

Completion harus dibedakan dari certification effectiveness.

⸻

**59\. Performance Review Health Statement**

PD-03 E5 dapat menghasilkan integrated health statement apabila evidence  
cukup.

Contoh structure:

Governance & Compliance Health

&nbsp; ↓

Policy Condition

Control Condition

Certification Condition

Compliance Condition

Standards Condition

Baseline Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Materiality

&nbsp; ↓

Trend

&nbsp; ↓

Confidence

&nbsp; ↓

Integrated Assessment

Health statement tidak boleh menggunakan unsupported numerical weighting.

⸻

**60\. Review Confidence**

Review conclusion dapat memiliki confidence:

High

Medium

Low

Insufficient Evidence

Confidence harus berdasarkan:

- evidence quality;
- evidence completeness;
- measurement reliability;
- source reliability;
- review independence;
- context completeness.

⸻

**61\. Conditional Assessment**

Jika sebagian domain evidence tersedia:

CONDITIONALLY ASSESSED

dapat digunakan.

Conditional assessment tidak boleh disajikan sebagai full-confidence conclusion.

⸻

**62\. Review Result States**

Possible states:

Meets Expectation

Partially Meets Expectation

Below Expectation

Material Concern

Critical Concern

Insufficient Evidence

Not Applicable

Under Review

State harus mempunyai evidence basis.

⸻

**63\. Review Escalation Threshold**

Exact numerical threshold tidak dibekukan pada E5 PD-03 apabila source  
authoritative tidak menetapkannya.

Karena itu:

Numerical Threshold

\= SOURCE GAP

Materiality dan escalation criteria harus berasal dari applicable authoritative  
governance mechanism.

Jangan mengarang angka threshold.

⸻

**64\. Review Cadence Source Gap**

PD-03-specific cadence belum authoritative pada source yang tersedia.

Gunakan:

Cadence

\= SOURCE GAP

Reference cadence boleh digunakan sebagai implementation pattern, tetapi tidak  
boleh dipresentasikan sebagai mandatory PD-03 rule.

⸻

**67\. Non-Goals**

E5 tidak bertujuan untuk:

- menciptakan governance authority;
- mengubah governance mandate;
- mengubah policy secara unilateral;
- menjalankan control implementation;
- menjalankan certification implementation;
- menjalankan operational execution;
- mengubah architecture;
- mengubah strategy;
- menciptakan KPI baru;
- menciptakan target baru;
- menetapkan numerical threshold tanpa source;
- memindahkan accountability;
- menggantikan Governance Review;
- menggantikan Architecture Review;
- menggantikan Strategic Review;
- menggantikan E6 Performance Governance.

⸻

**68\. Success Criteria**

E5 PD-03 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1. setiap formal review memiliki objective;
2. setiap review memiliki legitimate criteria;
3. evidence dapat ditelusuri;
4. measurement integrity diverifikasi;
5. insufficient evidence dapat menghentikan conclusion;
6. Governance Policy dapat direview dalam scope yang benar;
7. Governance Control dapat direview tanpa mengambil implementation ownership;
8. Certification dapat direview tanpa mengambil implementation ownership;
9. Compliance dapat direview berdasarkan applicable requirement;
10. Governance Standards dapat digunakan sebagai criteria apabila applicable;
11. Governance Baseline dapat digunakan sebagai reference condition;
12. material findings dapat diklasifikasikan;
13. potential cause dibedakan dari confirmed cause;
14. performance failure dibedakan dari governance failure;
15. measurement failure dibedakan dari execution failure;
16. cross-platform finding mempertahankan ownership boundary;
17. architecture dependency dapat diteruskan ke Architecture Review;
18. governance concern dapat diteruskan ke Governance Review;
19. material findings dapat dieskalasikan;
20. recommendation dapat dibedakan dari decision;
21. review authority dapat dibedakan dari review ownership;
22. review records dapat diaudit;
23. historical correction memiliki audit trail;
24. follow-up dapat memverifikasi effectiveness;
25. completion dibedakan dari effectiveness;
26. review dapat diblokir apabila evidence tidak memadai;
27. anti-gaming controls tersedia;
28. exact PD-03 cadence tidak diinvent;
29. exact PD-03 thresholds tidak diinvent;
30. framework dapat diwariskan tanpa memindahkan authority atau ownership.

⸻

**69\. Source-Fidelity Qualification**

**SOURCE-SUPPORTED**

Source mendukung:

- E5 sebagai Performance Review layer;
- measured evidence → assessment → review → finding → improvement signal;
- review objective/owner/criteria/evidence;
- Platform Review;
- Executive Review interface;
- review documentation;
- review auditability;
- review quality controls;
- insufficient evidence blocking conclusion;
- review authority boundary;
- distinction between recommendation and decision;
- improvement follow-up;
- re-measurement.

**PD-03 SOURCE-SUPPORTED**

PD-03 source mendukung:

- Governance & Compliance sebagai domain;
- Governance Policy;
- Governance Control;
- Certification;
- Governance Standards;
- Governance Baseline;
- preservation of domain ownership;
- distinction between governance responsibility and execution ownership.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

**BOUNDED CANONICAL SYNTHESIS**

E5 menerapkan review framework tersebut terhadap PD-03 domain:

Policy

Control

Certification

Compliance

Governance Standards

Governance Baseline

tanpa memperluas authority atau execution ownership.

**SOURCE GAP**

Authoritative PD-03-specific source yang tersedia belum membekukan secara  
eksplisit:

- exact E5 review catalogue;
- exact review cadence;
- exact numerical materiality threshold;
- exact escalation threshold;
- exact KPI catalogue;
- exact reviewer assignment;
- exact local review participant list;
- exact local review procedure.

Elemen tersebut tetap SOURCE GAP sampai authoritative reconciliation  
dilakukan.

**72\. Canonical Freeze Statement**

E5 — Performance Review establishes the controlled mechanism through which PD-03 Governance & Compliance evaluates measured performance evidence against legitimate criteria, produces traceable assessments and findings, identifies improvement or escalation signals, and preserves the authority and ownership boundaries of Governance & Compliance and other AIOS Platforms.

E5 covers Governance Policy, Governance Control, Certification, Compliance, Governance Standards, and Governance Baseline only within their established PD-03 boundaries. Governance responsibility does not become implementation ownership.

Performance Review does not create governance authority, architecture authority, strategic authority, or operational authority. Recommendation is not decision; assessment is not authorization; finding is not sanction.

Where evidence is insufficient, criteria are invalid, authority is unclear, or material context is missing, E5 must withhold or qualify the conclusion rather than manufacture a result.

PD-03-specific cadence, thresholds, reviewer assignments, KPI catalogue, and other implementation details that are not supported by authoritative source remain controlled SOURCE GAP and must not be invented.

E5 is therefore FROZEN as the Performance Review Architecture for Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

⸻

**Final Status**

E5 — Performance Review: FROZEN — SOURCE-BOUNDED

\> \*\*Successor:\*\* E7 — Performance Reporting

⸻

1\. Purpose

E6 menetapkan bagaimana performance governance PD-03 dikelola, dimiliki, direview, dipertanggungjawabkan, dan dieskalasikan tanpa mengubah authority boundary Governance & Compliance maupun mengambil alih ownership domain lain.

E6 menjawab:

"Siapa mengelola performance capability, siapa bertanggung jawab atas performance evidence dan result, siapa memiliki review authority, dan ke mana kondisi material harus dieskalasikan?"

E6 mencakup:

0\. Performance Ownership;

0\. Performance Authority;

0\. Review Authority;

0\. Accountability;

0\. Escalation;

0\. Governance Controls;

0\. Decision Boundary;

0\. Cross-Platform Performance Governance.

⸻

2\. Constitutional Position

E6 merupakan predecessor langsung dari E7.

E1 — Performance Constitution

&nbsp; ↓

"What is performance?"

&nbsp; ↓

E2 — Performance Model

&nbsp; ↓

"What dimensions describe performance?"

&nbsp; ↓

E3 — Performance Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

"How is performance measured?"

&nbsp; ↓

E4 — KPI & Success Metrics

&nbsp; ↓

"What indicates success?"

&nbsp; ↓

E5 — Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

"How is performance evaluated?"

&nbsp; ↓

E6 — Performance Governance

&nbsp; ↓

"Who owns, governs, reviews, escalates, and remains accountable?"

&nbsp; ↓

E7 — Performance Reporting

&nbsp; ↓

"How is performance communicated?"

E6 tidak mengubah definisi performance yang telah ditetapkan E1–E5.

E6 mengatur governance atas performance-management capability dan response path, bukan governance atas seluruh domain yang diukur.

⸻

3\. PD-03 Governance Context

PD-03 adalah:

PD-03

Governance & Compliance

Primary organizational responsibility:

Governance & Compliance

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├── Governance Policy

&nbsp; ├── Governance Control

&nbsp; ├── Certification

&nbsp; └── Governance Standards

Baseline juga menetapkan bahwa technical design bukan ownership PD-03.

Karena itu Performance Governance PD-03 harus tetap berada dalam boundary:

Performance Governance

&nbsp; ↓

Governance & Compliance Performance

dan bukan:

Performance Governance

&nbsp; ↓

All AIOS Governance

&nbsp; ↓

All Platform Execution

⸻

4\. Core Performance Governance Principle

Performance Governance establishes ownership and accountability for the performance-management capability while preserving the authority and ownership of Governance & Compliance and all other domains being measured.

Implikasinya:

Performance Governance

&nbsp; ↓

governs performance capability

&nbsp; ↓

does not become owner of every measured domain

Performance Governance tidak mengambil alih:

• Governance Authority;

• Architecture Authority;

• Strategic Authority;

• Operational Authority;

• domain execution ownership;

• certification implementation ownership;

• technical implementation ownership.

Reference E6 secara eksplisit mempertahankan separation antara performance governance capability dan domain ownership.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

Canonical PD-03 model:

6\. Performance Management Capability Ownership

Reference model menetapkan ESD-08 / Performance Management Office sebagai owner atas performance-management capability.

Dengan demikian:

ESD-08 / Performance Management Office

Performance Management Capability

• measurement;

• improvement;

• performance assessment.

Capability Ownership

PD-03 Domain Ownership

7\. PD-03 Domain Ownership

PD-03 mempertahankan ownership atas:

Governance & Compliance

termasuk governance responsibilities yang secara authoritative diberikan kepada PD-03:

Governance Policy

Governance Control

Certification

Governance Standards

Dengan demikian:

ESD-08

Performance Capability Owner

&nbsp; +

PD-03

Governance & Compliance Domain Owner

Keduanya memiliki responsibility yang berbeda.

⸻

8\. Performance Ownership vs Domain Ownership

E6 harus mempertahankan distinction:

Model:

Performance Management

&nbsp; ↓

measures / evaluates / reviews

&nbsp; ↓

Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

owns domain outcome

Performance Management tidak menjadi domain owner hanya karena mengukur domain tersebut.

⸻

9\. Performance Authority

Performance Authority berarti:

delegated authority untuk menjalankan fungsi performance governance/review dalam scope yang telah diberikan.

Performance Authority bukan authority baru yang berada di atas Governance Authority.

Reference E6 secara eksplisit menegaskan hal ini.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Canonical boundary:

Governance Authority

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├── defines applicable authority

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ▼

Delegated Performance Authority

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├── operates performance mechanism

&nbsp; ├── conducts review

&nbsp; ├── coordinates escalation

&nbsp; └── maintains performance governance

Delegated Performance Authority tidak dapat melampaui delegation instrument.

⸻

10\. PD-03 Performance Authority Boundary

Untuk PD-03, performance authority hanya berlaku terhadap:

PD-03 Performance Management

dan tidak otomatis berlaku terhadap:

Governance Authority

Architecture Authority

Strategic Authority

Operational Authority

Other Platform Domain Authority

Jika performance issue membutuhkan decision di luar delegated authority:

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Authority Analysis

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Decision / Governance Action

⸻

11\. Source Gap — Local Performance Authority

Authoritative sources yang tersedia mendukung model delegated Performance Authority, tetapi belum memberikan delegation instrument PD-03 yang menetapkan secara eksplisit role/person lokal yang menerima authority tersebut.

Karena itu:

PD-03 Local Performance Authority

\= SOURCE GAP

Jangan menetapkan secara sepihak:

• nama role;

• nama individu;

• authority level;

• delegation scope;

• approval right.

Detail tersebut harus direkonsiliasi dari authoritative PD-03 governance instrument.

⸻

12\. Review Ownership

E6 harus membedakan:

Review Ownership

&nbsp; ≠

Review Authority

Reference model menetapkan:

Performance Owner

&nbsp; ↓

mengelola Performance Review

Executive Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Final Authority untuk Performance Review

⸻

13\. PD-03 Performance Review Authority

E5 telah menetapkan Performance Review sebagai mechanism.

E6 menetapkan governance boundary-nya.

Canonical flow:

Performance Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Prepare / Manage Review

&nbsp; ↓

Reviewer

&nbsp; ↓

Validate / Challenge

&nbsp; ↓

Executive Leadership

&nbsp; ↓

Assess / Determine Response

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Final Authority

Final authority harus mengikuti approved governance model.

E6 tidak menciptakan hierarchy baru.

⸻

14\. Governance Review vs Performance Review

PD-03 memiliki Governance Authority.

Performance Review tidak menggantikan Governance Review.

Perbedaannya:

Relationship:

Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Finding / Signal

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Concern

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Governance Response

Performance Review dapat memicu Governance Review.

Performance Review tidak mengambil alih Governance Review.

⸻

15\. Policy Performance Governance

PD-03 memiliki responsibility terhadap Governance Policy.

Performance Governance dapat menilai:

• policy performance;

• policy adherence;

• policy effectiveness;

• policy exception;

• policy-related performance gap;

• policy review signal.

Namun:

Performance Assessment

&nbsp; ≠

Policy Change Authority

Jika policy change diperlukan:

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Policy Improvement Signal

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Policy Process

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Governance Decision

⸻

16\. Governance Control Performance

PD-03 memiliki responsibility terhadap Governance Control.

Performance Governance dapat menilai:

Control Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Control Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Control Finding

Tetapi:

Governance Control Ownership

&nbsp; ≠

Control Implementation Ownership

Jika control implementation berada pada Platform lain:

PD-03

Governance Control Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Implementation

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Assessment

⸻

17\. Certification Performance Governance

PD-03 memiliki organizational responsibility terhadap Certification.

Performance Governance dapat menilai:

• certification readiness;

• evidence completeness;

• certification condition;

• certification gap;

• certification status;

• certification-related performance trend.

Namun:

Certification Governance

&nbsp; ≠

Certification Implementation

Subject Platform tetap mempertahankan domain execution ownership.

⸻

18\. Governance Standards Performance

Governance Standards merupakan primary output PD-03.

Performance Governance dapat mengevaluasi:

• adoption;

• applicability;

• adherence;

• effectiveness;

• exception;

• implementation evidence.

Namun:

Governance Standards

&nbsp; ≠

All Technical Standards

Technical implementation standard tetap berada pada applicable domain owner.

⸻

19\. Governance Baseline Relationship

Governance Baseline dapat menjadi reference condition untuk performance assessment apabila applicable.

Governance Baseline

&nbsp; ↓

Expected Governance Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Observed Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Variance / Finding

E6 tidak mengubah Governance Baseline.

Perubahan baseline harus mengikuti applicable governance/change-control process.

⸻

20\. Accountability Principle

Performance failure does not automatically transfer accountability.

Canonical chain:

Objective

&nbsp; ↓

Accountability

&nbsp; ↓

Execution

&nbsp; ↓

Result

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Accountability Review

Jika result buruk:

Performance Failure

&nbsp; ↓

Diagnostic Analysis

&nbsp; ↓

Determine Actual Responsibility

bukan:

Performance Failure

&nbsp; ↓

Automatically blame Performance Owner

⸻

21\. Governance Accountability

PD-03 Performance Governance harus dapat menjawab:

0\. Siapa memiliki performance-management capability?

0\. Siapa memiliki PD-03 domain outcome?

0\. Siapa menyediakan evidence?

0\. Siapa melakukan assessment?

0\. Siapa melakukan review?

0\. Siapa memiliki authority untuk response?

0\. Siapa menerima escalation?

0\. Siapa accountable atas improvement action?

0\. Siapa memverifikasi improvement effectiveness?

Jika salah satu tidak jelas:

ACCOUNTABILITY GAP

harus dicatat.

⸻

22\. Measurement Accountability

Measurement accountability harus dapat ditelusuri.

Minimum:

Metric

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence Source

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Method

&nbsp; ↓

Result

&nbsp; ↓

Review

Measurement failure harus dibedakan dari:

• governance failure;

• execution failure;

• domain failure.

⸻

23\. Governance Failure vs Performance Failure

E6 mempertahankan distinction:

Performance Failure

&nbsp; ≠

Governance Failure

Contoh:

KPI Below Target

&nbsp; ↓

Investigation

&nbsp; ├── Measurement Failure

&nbsp; ├── Execution Failure

&nbsp; ├── Capability Gap

&nbsp; ├── Governance Failure

&nbsp; └── External Constraint

Tidak boleh langsung menyimpulkan governance violation hanya karena performance rendah.

⸻

24\. Escalation Principle

Escalate the condition to the authority capable of resolving it, not merely to a higher reporting level.

Escalation harus mempertimbangkan:

• materiality;

• urgency;

• impact;

• risk;

• authority;

• dependency;

• accountability;

• cross-platform effect.

Reference E6 menggunakan principle tersebut sebagai core escalation mechanism.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

25\. Escalation Triggers

Minimum baseline-supported triggers:

• governance violation;

• architecture deviation;

• unresolved accountability conflict;

• strategic misalignment;

• critical performance gap.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Untuk PD-03, additional triggers dapat digunakan hanya jika authoritative source menetapkannya.

Jangan menciptakan numerical threshold secara sepihak.

⸻

26\. PD-03 Escalation Categories

Potential classification:

E1 — Performance Issue

E2 — Measurement Issue

E3 — Governance Issue

E4 — Architecture Dependency

E5 — Strategic Issue

E6 — Accountability Conflict

E7 — Cross-Platform Governance Issue

E8 — Certification Concern

E9 — Control Concern

E10 — Policy Concern

Classification harus digunakan sebagai routing aid.

Classification tidak menciptakan authority baru.

⸻

27\. Escalation Routing

Performance Gap

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Review

Governance Concern

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Review

Architecture Concern

&nbsp; ↓

Architecture Review

Strategic Concern

&nbsp; ↓

Strategic Review

Accountability Conflict

&nbsp; ↓

Governance / Executive Review

Cross-Platform Concern

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Cross-Platform Authority

Performance Governance hanya melakukan routing sesuai established authority.

⸻

28\. Escalation Record

Formal escalation harus memiliki:

Escalation ID

Source Review

Trigger

Observed Condition

Materiality

Impact

Evidence

Current Owner

Required Authority

Destination

Status

Decision / Outcome

Follow-Up

Closure Evidence

Jika field belum memiliki authoritative PD-03 implementation specification, structure ini adalah bounded synthesis, bukan klaim bahwa semua field tersebut sudah ditetapkan secara formal PD-03.

⸻

29\. Decision vs Recommendation

E6 harus menjaga:

Recommendation

&nbsp; ≠

Decision

Performance Governance dapat:

• recommend;

• signal;

• assess;

• escalate;

• provide evidence.

Decision tetap dilakukan oleh authority yang legitimate.

Status:

Recommendation

Decision Pending

Awaiting Authority Decision

Authorized Decision

tidak boleh dicampur.

⸻

30\. Performance Governance Decision Boundary

Performance Governance dapat:

• govern performance-management mechanism;

• maintain performance framework;

• maintain review mechanism;

• enforce evidence discipline;

• coordinate escalation;

• maintain accountability mapping;

• maintain performance governance records;

• verify performance-management controls.

Performance Governance tidak dapat secara otomatis:

• change Governance Authority;

• change Architecture Authority;

• change Strategy;

• change operational ownership;

• change policy;

• change architecture;

• authorize domain execution;

• impose organizational sanction;

• create authority not granted by governance.

⸻

31\. Cross-Platform Performance Governance

PD-03 dapat membutuhkan evidence dari:

PD-02 Architecture

PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence

PD-05 Runtime & Execution

PD-06 AI Engineering

PD-07 Infrastructure & Platform

PD-08 Security

PD-09 Quality & Evaluation

PD-10 Developer Experience

Relationship:

PD-03

Governance & Compliance

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├── evidence dependency

&nbsp; ├── governance requirement

&nbsp; ├── control relationship

&nbsp; ├── certification relationship

&nbsp; └── compliance relationship

Dependency tidak berarti ownership transfer.

⸻

32\. Cross-Platform Ownership Preservation

Jika PD-03 menemukan issue pada Platform lain:

PD-03 Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Cross-Platform Dependency

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Domain Review / Action

PD-03 tetap mempertahankan Governance & Compliance responsibility.

Platform lain tetap mempertahankan domain ownership.

33\. Architecture Dependency

Jika performance finding menunjukkan architecture dependency:

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Architecture Dependency

&nbsp; ↓

Architecture Review

&nbsp; ↓

Architecture Decision

PD-03 Performance Governance tidak dapat mengubah Architecture Baseline.

Architecture authority tetap mengikuti Architecture Governance.

34\. Governance Dependency

Jika performance finding menunjukkan governance weakness:

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Concern

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Response

E6 tidak menjadi pengganti Governance Architecture.

35\. Operational Dependency

Jika performance finding menunjukkan operational issue:

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Operational Dependency

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Operational Response

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measurement

Performance Governance tidak mengambil alih operational execution.

36\. Improvement Governance

E6 menyediakan governance boundary bagi E8.

Canonical flow:

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Signal

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Action

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Effectiveness Review

Performance Management memverifikasi outcome.

Action Owner tetap accountable atas execution.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

37\. Improvement Accountability

Improvement memiliki minimal dua accountability:

Action Accountability

Siapa melaksanakan action?

Effectiveness Accountability

Siapa memastikan action menghasilkan expected improvement?

Keduanya harus eksplisit.

Action Completed

&nbsp; ≠

Improvement Effective

⸻

38\. Performance Governance Controls

Minimum reference control set:

PG-01 — Ownership Control

Setiap material performance mechanism memiliki owner.

PG-02 — Authority Control

Setiap formal review memiliki authority path.

PG-03 — Evidence Control

Performance conclusion memiliki evidence.

PG-04 — Accountability Control

Performance result tetap terhubung dengan responsible owner.

PG-05 — Escalation Control

Material condition memiliki escalation route.

PG-06 — Decision Control

Recommendation dipisahkan dari decision.

PG-07 — Change Control

Performance governance model tidak berubah tanpa controlled change.

PG-08 — Independence Control

Material review memiliki independence yang memadai.

Control set ini merupakan reference architecture pattern; local PD-03 control identifiers harus direkonsiliasi terhadap authoritative governance source.

⸻

39\. Review Independence

Reviewer harus mampu memberikan:

• challenge;

• validation;

• evidence scrutiny;

• assessment challenge;

• conclusion review.

Independence harus proporsional terhadap:

• materiality;

• sensitivity;

• conflict risk.

Jika reviewer memiliki conflicting execution interest:

Conflict of Interest

&nbsp; ↓

Additional Reviewer

OR

Independent Validation

OR

Escalation

⸻

40\. Authority Conflict

Jika terjadi conflict antara:

• Performance Owner;

• PD-03 Domain Owner;

• Reviewer;

• Governance Owner;

• Executive Leadership;

jangan memperluas Performance Authority secara sepihak.

Canonical:

Performance Conflict

&nbsp; ↓

Authority Analysis

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Governance Mechanism

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Resolution

Reference E6 secara eksplisit melarang penyelesaian conflict dengan menciptakan authority baru.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

41\. Performance Governance Failure States

E6 harus dapat mendeteksi:

Owner Undefined

Authority Undefined

Reviewer Undefined

Accountability Conflict

Escalation Path Undefined

Decision Authority Unclear

Evidence Responsibility Unclear

Review Conflict of Interest

Governance Boundary Conflict

Jika material:

PERFORMANCE GOVERNANCE BLOCKED

dan issue harus dieskalasikan ke applicable authority.

Jangan manufacture ownership atau authority.

⸻

Exact Local Record Catalogue

43\. Performance Governance Change Control

Perubahan material terhadap E6 harus melalui Architecture Change Control.

Material changes meliputi:

• performance ownership;

• delegated performance authority;

• review authority;

• final authority;

• escalation path;

• accountability model;

• cross-platform governance interface;

• governance control model;

• reference implementation inheritance.

Performance metric/KPI domain-specific change tidak otomatis berarti perubahan E6.

44\. Governance Change Boundary

E6 tidak boleh digunakan sebagai bypass untuk:

Governance Change

Architecture Change

Strategic Change

Authority Change

&nbsp; ↓

Change Signal

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Change-Control Mechanism

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Decision

45\. Performance Governance and Strategy

Performance Governance menerima strategic objectives sebagai input.

&nbsp; ↓

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Framework

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Review

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Assessment

Namun:

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ≠

Strategic Decision

Strategic change tetap berada pada Strategic / Executive Authority.

46\. Performance Governance and Architecture

Performance Governance dapat mengidentifikasi:

• architecture dependency;

• architecture constraint;

• architecture deviation;

• capability limitation.

Namun:

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ≠

Architecture Change Authorization

Architecture decision tetap mengikuti Architecture Governance.

Performance Governance tidak menjalankan operation.

Decision

Operation

Result

&nbsp; ↓

Performance

&nbsp; ↓

Review

Applicable Operational Owner remains responsible for execution.

Performance Management melakukan verification.

55\. Non-Goals

E6 tidak bertujuan untuk:

• menciptakan Performance Authority baru di atas Governance Authority;

• mengambil alih Governance Authority;

• mengambil alih PD-03 domain execution;

• mengambil alih technical design;

• mengambil alih Architecture Authority;

• mengambil alih Strategic Authority;

• mengambil alih Operational Authority;

• menetapkan KPI baru;

• menetapkan target baru;

• menetapkan threshold tanpa source;

• menetapkan cadence tanpa source;

• menetapkan local delegation tanpa source;

• menjalankan certification implementation;

• menjalankan control implementation;

• mengubah policy secara unilateral;

• mengubah Architecture Baseline;

• memindahkan accountability hanya karena performance failure.

56\. Success Criteria

E6 PD-03 dianggap berhasil apabila:

0\. performance-management capability memiliki owner yang jelas;

0\. relationship ESD-08 dengan PD-03 dapat ditelusuri;

0\. PD-03 tetap menjadi domain owner Governance & Compliance;

0\. Performance Authority dibedakan dari Governance Authority;

0\. Performance Authority dibatasi oleh delegation;

0\. review ownership dibedakan dari review authority;

0\. final authority mengikuti approved governance model;

0\. accountability tidak berpindah hanya karena performance failure;

0\. measurement accountability dapat ditelusuri;

0\. material finding memiliki escalation route;

0\. escalation diarahkan kepada authority yang capable menyelesaikan condition;

0\. governance failure dibedakan dari performance failure;

0\. measurement failure dibedakan dari execution failure;

0\. recommendation dibedakan dari decision;

0\. improvement action memiliki accountable owner;

0\. improvement effectiveness dapat diverifikasi;

0\. Policy performance tetap berada dalam Governance & Compliance boundary;

0\. Control performance tidak mengambil alih implementation ownership;

0\. Certification performance tidak mengambil alih implementation ownership;

0\. Governance Standards tidak diperluas menjadi seluruh technical standards;

0\. cross-platform dependency tidak menjadi ownership transfer;

0\. architecture dependency diarahkan ke Architecture Governance;

0\. strategic dependency diarahkan ke Strategic Governance;

0\. operational dependency diarahkan ke applicable operational owner;

0\. governance conflicts tidak diselesaikan dengan fabricated authority;

0\. performance governance records dapat diaudit;

0\. material governance changes mengikuti controlled change;

0\. unsupported local thresholds tetap source gap;

0\. unsupported local cadence tetap source gap;

0\. unsupported local delegation tetap source gap;

0\. framework dapat diwariskan tanpa memaksakan implementation detail domain-specific.

⸻

57\. Source-Fidelity Qualification

SOURCE-SUPPORTED

Source mendukung:

• Performance Governance sebagai E6;

• performance ownership;

• performance authority boundary;

• review authority;

• accountability;

• escalation;

• decision/recommendation separation;

• governance controls;

• review independence;

• change control;

• ESD-08 ownership atas performance-management capability;

• Executive Authority sebagai final authority untuk Performance Review.

PD-03 SOURCE-SUPPORTED

PD-03 source mendukung:

• Governance & Compliance identity;

• Governance Policy;

• Governance Control;

• Certification;

• Governance Standards;

• Governance Baseline relationship;

• domain ownership preservation;

• separation between governance responsibility and implementation ownership;

• cross-platform dependency without ownership transfer.

Policy

Control

Certification

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Governance

&nbsp; ↓

Review / Accountability / Escalation

SOURCE GAP

60\. Canonical Freeze Statement

E6 — Performance Governance establishes the governance mechanism through which the performance-management capability is owned, governed, reviewed, escalated, and held accountable while preserving the established authority and ownership boundaries of PD-03 Governance & Compliance and all other AIOS domains.

For PD-03, Performance Governance applies to the performance of Governance & Compliance, including Governance Policy, Governance Control, Certification, and Governance Standards within their established boundaries.

Performance-management capability ownership does not become Governance & Compliance domain ownership, and Governance & Compliance ownership does not become ownership of implementation performed by other Platforms.

Performance Authority is delegated authority for performance-management functions. It is not an authority above Governance Authority, Architecture Authority, Strategic Authority, or Operational Authority.

Performance Review ownership is distinct from review authority, and recommendation is distinct from decision. Final authority remains governed by the approved governance model.

Performance failure does not automatically transfer accountability. Governance failure, measurement failure, execution failure, capability gap, and external constraint must remain diagnostically distinct.

Material conditions are escalated to the authority capable of resolving them. Performance Governance must not manufacture ownership, authority, escalation destination, threshold, cadence, or delegation where authoritative source is absent.

PD-03-specific local delegation, reviewer assignment, threshold, cadence, and record catalogue remain controlled SOURCE GAP until authoritative reconciliation is completed.

E6 — Performance Governance is therefore FROZEN as the source-bounded Performance Governance Architecture for Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

⸻

Final Status

E6 — Performance Governance: FROZEN — SOURCE-BOUNDED

**1\. Purpose**

E6 menetapkan bagaimana performance governance PD-03 dikelola, dimiliki, direview, dipertanggungjawabkan, dan dieskalasikan tanpa mengubah authority boundary Governance & Compliance maupun mengambil alih ownership domain lain.

E6 menjawab:

"Siapa mengelola performance capability, siapa bertanggung jawab atas performance evidence dan result, siapa memiliki review authority, dan ke mana kondisi material harus dieskalasikan?"

E6 mencakup:

1. Performance Ownership;
2. Performance Authority;
3. Review Authority;
4. Accountability;
5. Escalation;
6. Governance Controls;
7. Decision Boundary;
8. Cross-Platform Performance Governance.

⸻

**2\. Constitutional Position**

E6 merupakan predecessor langsung dari E7.

E1 — Performance Constitution

&nbsp; ↓

"What is performance?"

&nbsp; ↓

E2 — Performance Model

&nbsp; ↓

"What dimensions describe performance?"

&nbsp; ↓

E3 — Performance Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

"How is performance measured?"

&nbsp; ↓

E4 — KPI & Success Metrics

&nbsp; ↓

"What indicates success?"

&nbsp; ↓

E5 — Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

"How is performance evaluated?"

&nbsp; ↓

E6 — Performance Governance

&nbsp; ↓

"Who owns, governs, reviews, escalates, and remains accountable?"

&nbsp; ↓

E7 — Performance Reporting

&nbsp; ↓

"How is performance communicated?"

E6 tidak mengubah definisi performance yang telah ditetapkan E1–E5.

E6 mengatur governance atas performance-management capability dan response path, bukan governance atas seluruh domain yang diukur.

⸻

**3\. PD-03 Governance Context**

PD-03 adalah:

PD-03

Governance & Compliance

Primary organizational responsibility:

Governance & Compliance

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├── Governance Policy

&nbsp; ├── Governance Control

&nbsp; ├── Certification

&nbsp; └── Governance Standards

Baseline juga menetapkan bahwa technical design bukan ownership PD-03.

Karena itu Performance Governance PD-03 harus tetap berada dalam boundary:

Performance Governance

&nbsp; ↓

Governance & Compliance Performance

dan bukan:

Performance Governance

&nbsp; ↓

All AIOS Governance

&nbsp; ↓

All Platform Execution

⸻

**4\. Core Performance Governance Principle**

Performance Governance establishes ownership and accountability for the performance-management capability while preserving the authority and ownership of Governance & Compliance and all other domains being measured.

Implikasinya:

Performance Governance

&nbsp; ↓

governs performance capability

&nbsp; ↓

does not become owner of every measured domain

Performance Governance tidak mengambil alih:

- Governance Authority;
- Architecture Authority;
- Strategic Authority;
- Operational Authority;
- domain execution ownership;
- certification implementation ownership;
- technical implementation ownership.

Reference E6 secara eksplisit mempertahankan separation antara performance governance capability dan domain ownership.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

**5\. Performance Governance Architecture**

Canonical PD-03 model:

&nbsp; APPLICABLE GOVERNANCE AUTHORITY

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ▼

&nbsp; PERFORMANCE GOVERNANCE

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ┌───────────────┼────────────────┐

&nbsp; ▼ ▼ ▼

&nbsp; Performance Review Accountability

&nbsp; Ownership Authority │

&nbsp; │ │ │

&nbsp; └───────────────┼────────────────┘

&nbsp; ▼

&nbsp; Governance & Compliance

&nbsp; Performance

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ┌────────────────┼────────────────┐

&nbsp; ▼ ▼ ▼

&nbsp; Policy Control Certification

&nbsp; │ │ │

&nbsp; └────────────────┼────────────────┘

&nbsp; ▼

&nbsp; Evidence

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ▼

&nbsp; Assessment

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ▼

&nbsp; Finding

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ┌────────┴────────┐

&nbsp; ▼ ▼

&nbsp; Improvement Escalation

E6 mengatur governance layer.

E6 tidak menjalankan Policy, Control, atau Certification implementation.

**6\. Performance Management Capability Ownership**

Reference model menetapkan ESD-08 / Performance Management Office sebagai owner atas performance-management capability.

Dengan demikian:

ESD-08 / Performance Management Office

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Management Capability

Capability ownership mencakup framework dan mechanism untuk:

- measurement;
- review;
- governance;
- reporting;
- improvement;
- performance assessment.

Namun:

Capability Ownership

&nbsp; ≠

PD-03 Domain Ownership

⸻

**7\. PD-03 Domain Ownership**

PD-03 mempertahankan ownership atas:

Governance & Compliance

termasuk governance responsibilities yang secara authoritative diberikan kepada PD-03:

Governance Policy

Governance Control

Certification

Governance Standards

Dengan demikian:

ESD-08

Performance Capability Owner

&nbsp; +

PD-03

Governance & Compliance Domain Owner

Keduanya memiliki responsibility yang berbeda.

⸻

**8\. Performance Ownership vs Domain Ownership**

E6 harus mempertahankan distinction:

| **Ownership**                    | **Scope**                         |
| -------------------------------- | --------------------------------- |
| Performance Management Ownership | Performance-management capability |
| PD-03 Domain Ownership           | Governance & Compliance domain    |
| Domain Owner Platform lain       | Domain masing-masing              |
| Operational Ownership            | Execution masing-masing domain    |

Model:

Performance Management

&nbsp; ↓

measures / evaluates / reviews

&nbsp; ↓

Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

owns domain outcome

Performance Management tidak menjadi domain owner hanya karena mengukur domain tersebut.

⸻

**9\. Performance Authority**

Performance Authority berarti:

delegated authority untuk menjalankan fungsi performance governance/review dalam scope yang telah diberikan.

Performance Authority bukan authority baru yang berada di atas Governance Authority.

Reference E6 secara eksplisit menegaskan hal ini.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Canonical boundary:

Governance Authority

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├── defines applicable authority

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ▼

Delegated Performance Authority

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├── operates performance mechanism

&nbsp; ├── conducts review

&nbsp; ├── coordinates escalation

&nbsp; └── maintains performance governance

Delegated Performance Authority tidak dapat melampaui delegation instrument.

⸻

**10\. PD-03 Performance Authority Boundary**

Untuk PD-03, performance authority hanya berlaku terhadap:

PD-03 Performance Management

dan tidak otomatis berlaku terhadap:

Governance Authority

Architecture Authority

Strategic Authority

Operational Authority

Other Platform Domain Authority

Jika performance issue membutuhkan decision di luar delegated authority:

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Authority Analysis

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Decision / Governance Action

**11\. Source Gap — Local Performance Authority**

Authoritative sources yang tersedia mendukung model delegated Performance Authority, tetapi belum memberikan delegation instrument PD-03 yang menetapkan secara eksplisit role/person lokal yang menerima authority tersebut.

Karena itu:

PD-03 Local Performance Authority

\= SOURCE GAP

Jangan menetapkan secara sepihak:

- nama role;
- nama individu;
- authority level;
- delegation scope;
- approval right.

Detail tersebut harus direkonsiliasi dari authoritative PD-03 governance instrument.

**12\. Review Ownership**

Review Ownership

&nbsp; ≠

mengelola Performance Review

**13\. PD-03 Performance Review Authority**

**14\. Governance Review vs Performance Review**

PD-03 memiliki Governance Authority.

Performance Review tidak menggantikan Governance Review.

Perbedaannya:

| **Mechanism**      | **Primary Question**                                                                                                       |
| ------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Performance Review | Bagaimana performance terhadap expectation?                                                                                |
| Governance Review  | Apakah governance mechanism, authority, policy, control, atau compliance condition berjalan sesuai governance requirement? |

Relationship:

Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Finding / Signal

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Concern

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Governance Response

Performance Review dapat memicu Governance Review.

Performance Review tidak mengambil alih Governance Review.

⸻

**15\. Policy Performance Governance**

PD-03 memiliki responsibility terhadap Governance Policy.

Performance Governance dapat menilai:

- policy performance;
- policy adherence;
- policy effectiveness;
- policy exception;
- policy-related performance gap;
- policy review signal.

Namun:

Performance Assessment

&nbsp; ≠

Policy Change Authority

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Policy Process

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Control Requirement

Variance / Finding

**20\. Accountability Principle**

Performance failure does not automatically transfer accountability.

Canonical chain:

Objective

&nbsp; ↓

Accountability

&nbsp; ↓

Execution

&nbsp; ↓

Result

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Accountability Review

Jika result buruk:

Performance Failure

&nbsp; ↓

Diagnostic Analysis

&nbsp; ↓

Determine Actual Responsibility

bukan:

Performance Failure

&nbsp; ↓

Automatically blame Performance Owner

⸻

**21\. Governance Accountability**

PD-03 Performance Governance harus dapat menjawab:

1. Siapa memiliki performance-management capability?
2. Siapa memiliki PD-03 domain outcome?
3. Siapa menyediakan evidence?
4. Siapa melakukan assessment?
5. Siapa melakukan review?
6. Siapa memiliki authority untuk response?
7. Siapa menerima escalation?
8. Siapa accountable atas improvement action?
9. Siapa memverifikasi improvement effectiveness?

Jika salah satu tidak jelas:

ACCOUNTABILITY GAP

harus dicatat.

⸻

**22\. Measurement Accountability**

Measurement accountability harus dapat ditelusuri.

Minimum:

Metric

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence Source

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Method

&nbsp; ↓

Result

&nbsp; ↓

Review

Measurement failure harus dibedakan dari:

- governance failure;
- execution failure;
- domain failure.

⸻

**23\. Governance Failure vs Performance Failure**

E6 mempertahankan distinction:

Performance Failure

&nbsp; ≠

Governance Failure

Contoh:

KPI Below Target

&nbsp; ↓

Investigation

&nbsp; ├── Measurement Failure

&nbsp; ├── Execution Failure

&nbsp; ├── Capability Gap

&nbsp; ├── Governance Failure

&nbsp; └── External Constraint

Tidak boleh langsung menyimpulkan governance violation hanya karena performance rendah.

⸻

**24\. Escalation Principle**

Escalate the condition to the authority capable of resolving it, not merely to a higher reporting level.

Escalation harus mempertimbangkan:

- materiality;
- urgency;
- impact;
- risk;
- authority;
- dependency;
- accountability;
- cross-platform effect.

Reference E6 menggunakan principle tersebut sebagai core escalation mechanism.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

**25\. Escalation Triggers**

Minimum baseline-supported triggers:

- governance violation;
- architecture deviation;
- unresolved accountability conflict;
- strategic misalignment;
- critical performance gap.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Untuk PD-03, additional triggers dapat digunakan hanya jika authoritative source menetapkannya.

Jangan menciptakan numerical threshold secara sepihak.

⸻

**26\. PD-03 Escalation Categories**

Potential classification:

E1 — Performance Issue

E2 — Measurement Issue

E3 — Governance Issue

E4 — Architecture Dependency

E5 — Strategic Issue

E6 — Accountability Conflict

E7 — Cross-Platform Governance Issue

E8 — Certification Concern

E9 — Control Concern

E10 — Policy Concern

Classification harus digunakan sebagai routing aid.

Classification tidak menciptakan authority baru.

**27\. Escalation Routing**

Performance Gap

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Review

Governance Concern

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Review

Architecture Concern

&nbsp; ↓

Architecture Review

Strategic Concern

&nbsp; ↓

Strategic Review

Accountability Conflict

&nbsp; ↓

Governance / Executive Review

Cross-Platform Concern

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Cross-Platform Authority

Performance Governance hanya melakukan routing sesuai established authority.

**28\. Escalation Record**

Formal escalation harus memiliki:

Escalation ID

Source Review

Trigger

Observed Condition

Materiality

Impact

Evidence

Current Owner

Required Authority

Destination

Status

Decision / Outcome

Follow-Up

Closure Evidence

Jika field belum memiliki authoritative PD-03 implementation specification, structure ini adalah bounded synthesis, bukan klaim bahwa semua field tersebut sudah ditetapkan secara formal PD-03.

⸻

**29\. Decision vs Recommendation**

E6 harus menjaga:

Recommendation

&nbsp; ≠

Decision

Performance Governance dapat:

- recommend;
- signal;
- assess;
- escalate;
- provide evidence.

Decision tetap dilakukan oleh authority yang legitimate.

Status:

Recommendation

Decision Pending

Awaiting Authority Decision

Authorized Decision

tidak boleh dicampur.

⸻

**30\. Performance Governance Decision Boundary**

Performance Governance dapat:

- govern performance-management mechanism;
- maintain performance framework;
- maintain review mechanism;
- enforce evidence discipline;
- coordinate escalation;
- maintain accountability mapping;
- maintain performance governance records;
- verify performance-management controls.

Performance Governance tidak dapat secara otomatis:

- change Governance Authority;
- change Architecture Authority;
- change Strategy;
- change operational ownership;
- change policy;
- change architecture;
- authorize domain execution;
- impose organizational sanction;
- create authority not granted by governance.

PD-08 Security

Relationship:

PD-03

Governance & Compliance

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├── evidence dependency

&nbsp; ├── governance requirement

&nbsp; ├── certification relationship

&nbsp; └── compliance relationship

Dependency tidak berarti ownership transfer.

**36\. Improvement Governance**

E6 menyediakan governance boundary bagi E8.

Canonical flow:

&nbsp; ↓

&nbsp; ↓

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Action

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Architecture Review.txt

**37\. Improvement Accountability**

Improvement memiliki minimal dua accountability:

Action Accountability

Siapa melaksanakan action?

Effectiveness Accountability

Siapa memastikan action menghasilkan expected improvement?

Keduanya harus eksplisit.

Action Completed

&nbsp; ≠

Improvement Effective

⸻

**38\. Performance Governance Controls**

Minimum reference control set:

PG-01 — Ownership Control

Setiap material performance mechanism memiliki owner.

PG-02 — Authority Control

Setiap formal review memiliki authority path.

PG-03 — Evidence Control

Performance conclusion memiliki evidence.

PG-04 — Accountability Control

Performance result tetap terhubung dengan responsible owner.

PG-05 — Escalation Control

Material condition memiliki escalation route.

PG-06 — Decision Control

Recommendation dipisahkan dari decision.

PG-07 — Change Control

Performance governance model tidak berubah tanpa controlled change.

PG-08 — Independence Control

Material review memiliki independence yang memadai.

Control set ini merupakan reference architecture pattern; local PD-03 control identifiers harus direkonsiliasi terhadap authoritative governance source.

⸻

**39\. Review Independence**

Reviewer harus mampu memberikan:

- challenge;
- validation;
- evidence scrutiny;
- assessment challenge;
- conclusion review.

Independence harus proporsional terhadap:

- materiality;
- sensitivity;
- conflict risk.

Jika reviewer memiliki conflicting execution interest:

Conflict of Interest

&nbsp; ↓

Additional Reviewer

OR

Independent Validation

OR

Escalation

⸻

**40\. Authority Conflict**

Jika terjadi conflict antara:

- Performance Owner;
- PD-03 Domain Owner;
- Reviewer;
- Governance Owner;
- Executive Leadership;

jangan memperluas Performance Authority secara sepihak.

Canonical:

Performance Conflict

&nbsp; ↓

Authority Analysis

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Governance Mechanism

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Resolution

Reference E6 secara eksplisit melarang penyelesaian conflict dengan menciptakan authority baru.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

**41\. Performance Governance Failure States**

E6 harus dapat mendeteksi:

Owner Undefined

Authority Undefined

Reviewer Undefined

Accountability Conflict

Escalation Path Undefined

Decision Authority Unclear

Evidence Responsibility Unclear

Review Conflict of Interest

Governance Boundary Conflict

Jika material:

PERFORMANCE GOVERNANCE BLOCKED

dan issue harus dieskalasikan ke applicable authority.

Jangan manufacture ownership atau authority.

Namun exact PD-03 record names belum authoritative pada source yang tersedia.

Karena itu:

Exact Local Record Catalogue

\= SOURCE GAP

Claude Code harus merekonsiliasi nama dan schema final dari source repository.

**43\. Performance Governance Change Control**

Perubahan material terhadap E6 harus melalui Architecture Change Control.

Material changes meliputi:

- performance ownership;
- delegated performance authority;
- review authority;
- final authority;
- escalation path;
- accountability model;
- cross-platform governance interface;
- governance control model;
- reference implementation inheritance.

Performance metric/KPI domain-specific change tidak otomatis berarti perubahan E6.

**44\. Governance Change Boundary**

E6 tidak boleh digunakan sebagai bypass untuk:

Governance Change

**45\. Performance Governance and Strategy**

Performance Governance menerima strategic objectives sebagai input.

&nbsp; ↓

&nbsp; ↓

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Review

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Assessment

&nbsp; ≠

**46\. Performance Governance and Architecture**

Performance Governance dapat mengidentifikasi:

- architecture dependency;
- architecture constraint;
- architecture deviation;

Namun:

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ≠

Architecture Change Authorization

Architecture decision tetap mengikuti Architecture Governance.

**47\. Performance Governance and Operation**

Performance Governance tidak menjalankan operation.

Governance

&nbsp; ↓

Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Operation

&nbsp; ↓

Result

&nbsp; ↓

Performance

&nbsp; ↓

Review

Jika improvement memerlukan operational execution:

Applicable Operational Owner remains responsible for execution.

Performance Management melakukan verification.

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

&nbsp; ↓

Re-assessment

PD-03 tidak otomatis menjadi implementation owner.

Requirement
