**55\. Non-Goals**

E6 tidak bertujuan untuk:

- menciptakan Performance Authority baru di atas Governance Authority;
- mengambil alih Governance Authority;
- mengambil alih PD-03 domain execution;
- mengambil alih technical design;
- mengambil alih Architecture Authority;
- mengambil alih Strategic Authority;
- mengambil alih Operational Authority;
- menetapkan KPI baru;
- menetapkan target baru;
- menetapkan threshold tanpa source;
- menetapkan cadence tanpa source;
- menetapkan local delegation tanpa source;
- menjalankan certification implementation;
- menjalankan control implementation;
- mengubah policy secara unilateral;
- mengubah Architecture Baseline;
- memindahkan accountability hanya karena performance failure.

⸻

**56\. Success Criteria**

E6 PD-03 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1. performance-management capability memiliki owner yang jelas;
2. relationship ESD-08 dengan PD-03 dapat ditelusuri;
3. PD-03 tetap menjadi domain owner Governance & Compliance;
4. Performance Authority dibedakan dari Governance Authority;
5. Performance Authority dibatasi oleh delegation;
6. review ownership dibedakan dari review authority;
7. final authority mengikuti approved governance model;
8. accountability tidak berpindah hanya karena performance failure;
9. measurement accountability dapat ditelusuri;
10. material finding memiliki escalation route;
11. escalation diarahkan kepada authority yang capable menyelesaikan condition;
12. governance failure dibedakan dari performance failure;
13. measurement failure dibedakan dari execution failure;
14. recommendation dibedakan dari decision;
15. improvement action memiliki accountable owner;
16. improvement effectiveness dapat diverifikasi;
17. Policy performance tetap berada dalam Governance & Compliance boundary;
18. Control performance tidak mengambil alih implementation ownership;
19. Certification performance tidak mengambil alih implementation ownership;
20. Governance Standards tidak diperluas menjadi seluruh technical standards;
21. cross-platform dependency tidak menjadi ownership transfer;
22. architecture dependency diarahkan ke Architecture Governance;
23. strategic dependency diarahkan ke Strategic Governance;
24. operational dependency diarahkan ke applicable operational owner;
25. governance conflicts tidak diselesaikan dengan fabricated authority;
26. performance governance records dapat diaudit;
27. material governance changes mengikuti controlled change;
28. unsupported local thresholds tetap source gap;
29. unsupported local cadence tetap source gap;
30. unsupported local delegation tetap source gap;
31. framework dapat diwariskan tanpa memaksakan implementation detail domain-specific.

⸻

**57\. Source-Fidelity Qualification**

**SOURCE-SUPPORTED**

Source mendukung:

- Performance Governance sebagai E6;
- performance ownership;
- performance authority boundary;
- review authority;
- accountability;
- escalation;
- decision/recommendation separation;
- governance controls;
- review independence;
- change control;
- ESD-08 ownership atas performance-management capability;
- Executive Authority sebagai final authority untuk Performance Review.

**PD-03 SOURCE-SUPPORTED**

PD-03 source mendukung:

- Governance & Compliance identity;
- Governance Policy;
- Governance Control;
- Certification;
- Governance Standards;
- Governance Baseline relationship;
- domain ownership preservation;
- separation between governance responsibility and implementation ownership;
- cross-platform dependency without ownership transfer.

**BOUNDED CANONICAL SYNTHESIS**

E6 menerapkan reference Performance Governance model kepada PD-03:

Performance Management Capability

&nbsp; +

Governance & Compliance Domain

&nbsp; ↓

Policy

Control

Certification

Governance Standards

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Governance

&nbsp; ↓

Review / Accountability / Escalation

Synthesis ini tidak menciptakan authority atau ownership baru.

**SOURCE GAP**

Belum ditemukan authoritative PD-03 source yang secara eksplisit menetapkan:

- local Performance Owner;
- local Performance Authority delegate;
- local reviewer;
- local escalation recipient;
- local numerical thresholds;
- local governance cadence;
- exact local governance record catalogue;
- domain-specific delegation instrument.

Seluruhnya tetap SOURCE GAP sampai authoritative reconciliation.

**60\. Canonical Freeze Statement**

E6 — Performance Governance establishes the governance mechanism through which the performance-management capability is owned, governed, reviewed, escalated, and held accountable while preserving the established authority and ownership boundaries of PD-03 Governance & Compliance and all other AIOS domains.

For PD-03, Performance Governance applies to the performance of Governance & Compliance, including Governance Policy, Governance Control, Certification, and Governance Standards within their established boundaries.

Performance-management capability ownership does not become Governance & Compliance domain ownership, and Governance & Compliance ownership does not become ownership of implementation performed by other Platforms.

Performance Authority is delegated authority for performance-management functions. It is not an authority above Governance Authority, Architecture Authority, Strategic Authority, or Operational Authority.

Performance Review ownership is distinct from review authority, and recommendation is distinct from decision. Final authority remains governed by the approved governance model.

Performance failure does not automatically transfer accountability. Governance failure, measurement failure, execution failure, capability gap, and external constraint must remain diagnostically distinct.

Material conditions are escalated to the authority capable of resolving them. Performance Governance must not manufacture ownership, authority, escalation destination, threshold, cadence, or delegation where authoritative source is absent.

PD-03-specific local delegation, reviewer assignment, threshold, cadence, and record catalogue remain controlled SOURCE GAP until authoritative reconciliation is completed.

E6 — Performance Governance is therefore FROZEN as the source-bounded Performance Governance Architecture for Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

⸻

**Final Status**

E6 — Performance Governance: FROZEN — SOURCE-BOUNDED

\# 1. Purpose

E7 menetapkan bagaimana informasi performance PD-03 dikemas, dikomunikasikan, didistribusikan, dan digunakan untuk mendukung review, governance response, serta authorized decision-making.

E7 menjawab:

\> \*\*"Bagaimana performance Governance & Compliance dikomunikasikan kepada consumer yang berwenang?"\*\*

E7 merupakan communication layer antara:

\`\`\`text

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Evaluation

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Reporting

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Consumer

&nbsp; ↓

Governance / Executive / Domain Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Response

E7 tidak menggantikan:

• measurement;

• evaluation;

• performance review;

• governance authority;

• architecture authority;

• strategic authority;

• operational authority.

⸻

2\. Constitutional Position

Canonical Part E relationship:

E1 — Performance Constitution

&nbsp; ↓

"What is performance?"

&nbsp; ↓

E2 — Performance Model

&nbsp; ↓

"What dimensions describe performance?"

&nbsp; ↓

E3 — Performance Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

"How is performance measured?"

&nbsp; ↓

E4 — KPI & Success Metrics

&nbsp; ↓

"What indicates success?"

&nbsp; ↓

E5 — Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

"How is performance evaluated?"

&nbsp; ↓

E6 — Performance Governance

&nbsp; ↓

"Who owns, governs, reviews, escalates?"

&nbsp; ↓

E7 — Performance Reporting

&nbsp; ↓

"How is performance communicated?"

&nbsp; ↓

E8 — Performance Improvement

E7 tidak mendefinisikan ulang predecessor.

E7 menyajikan hasil yang telah dihasilkan oleh E3–E6 kepada consumer yang legitimate.

⸻

3\. Core Reporting Principle

Performance reporting translates validated performance evidence and approved evaluation into the appropriate level of information for the appropriate consumer, while preserving context, provenance, accountability, and authority boundaries.

Reporting harus:

• accurate;

• traceable;

• contextual;

• proportionate;

• audience-appropriate;

• timely;

• decision-useful;

• access-controlled;

• historically consistent.

Reporting tidak boleh menjadi:

• data dump;

• KPI wall;

• dashboard decoration;

• unsupported executive summary;

• selective presentation;

• hidden re-evaluation mechanism.

⸻

4\. PD-03 Reporting Context

PD-03 memiliki primary organizational responsibility atas:

Governance & Compliance

dengan responsibility yang telah ditetapkan terhadap:

Policy

Control

Certification

Governance Standards

E7 karena itu terutama melaporkan performance yang berkaitan dengan Governance & Compliance, bukan seluruh performance execution AIOS.

Canonical boundary:

PD-03 Performance Reporting

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├── Governance Policy Performance

&nbsp; ├── Governance Control Performance

&nbsp; ├── Certification Performance

&nbsp; └── Governance Standards Performance

⸻

5\. Reporting Architecture

E3 — Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

E4 — KPI / Success Metrics

&nbsp; ↓

E5 — Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

E6 — Governance

&nbsp; ↓

Validated Performance Evidence

&nbsp; +

Approved Evaluation / Finding

&nbsp; ↓

E7 — Performance Reporting

&nbsp; ↓

┌────────────────────┬────────────────────┬───────────────────┐

│ │ │

▼ ▼ ▼

Governance Platform Executive / Cross-

Reporting Reporting Platform Reporting

│ │ │

└────────────────────┼────────────────────┘

&nbsp; ▼

&nbsp; Authorized Consumer

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ▼

&nbsp; Authorized Response

Reporting hierarchy tidak menjadi authority hierarchy.

⸻

6\. Reporting vs Measurement

E3 menjawab:

"Apa yang diukur dan apa hasil pengukurannya?"

E7 menjawab:

"Bagaimana hasil tersebut dikomunikasikan?"

Canonical:

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Actual Result

&nbsp; ↓

Validation

&nbsp; ↓

Record

&nbsp; ↓

Reporting

E7 tidak boleh mengubah actual result untuk menghasilkan presentation yang lebih favorable.

Jika measurement memiliki limitation:

Result

-

Limitation

-

Confidence

harus tetap terlihat ketika material terhadap interpretation.

⸻

7\. Reporting vs Evaluation

Measurement

"What happened?"

&nbsp; ↓

Evaluation

"What does it mean?"

&nbsp; ↓

Reporting

"How is it communicated?"

Contoh:

Actual:

Certification readiness = 82%

Evaluation:

Below approved readiness condition

Reporting:

Certification readiness is below the approved

condition, with identified evidence gaps.

E7 tidak boleh mengubah evaluation conclusion tanpa kembali ke applicable review mechanism.

⸻

8\. Reporting vs Performance Review

Performance Review menghasilkan:

• assessment;

• gap;

• finding;

• outcome;

• improvement signal.

Performance Reporting menyajikan hasil tersebut.

Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Approved / Recorded Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Report

Jika report membutuhkan interpretation baru yang materially changes the conclusion:

Return to E5

Performance Review

E7 tidak boleh menjadi hidden review mechanism.

⸻

9\. Reporting vs Governance

E6 menjawab:

"Siapa memiliki ownership, authority, accountability, dan escalation path?"

E7 menjawab:

"Informasi apa yang harus dikomunikasikan kepada siapa?"

Canonical:

E6 — Governance

&nbsp; ↓

Authority / Ownership / Accountability

&nbsp; ↓

E7 — Reporting

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Information

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Consumer

Reporting tidak menciptakan governance authority.

⸻

10\. PD-03 Reporting Scope

E7 PD-03 mencakup reporting atas:

10.1 Governance Policy

10.2 Governance Control

10.3 Certification

10.4 Governance Standards

• standards-related findings.

dan:

24\. Transparency Principle

Transparency means appropriate visibility to legitimate consumers, not unrestricted disclosure.

Reporting harus mempertimbangkan:

• classification;

• sensitivity;

• legitimate need;

• governance requirements;

• confidentiality;

• access boundary.

Informasi restricted tetap harus memiliki traceability.

Audience

Classification

Distribution Scope

Publication Status

Owner

Publication Date

Jangan menganggap:

Higher Organizational Position

&nbsp; =

Automatic Access

Access tetap mengikuti applicable governance requirements.

⸻

26\. Evidence and Provenance

Setiap material report claim harus dapat ditelusuri:

Source

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Metric / KPI

&nbsp; ↓

Evaluation

&nbsp; ↓

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Report

&nbsp; ↓

Decision / Action

Jika provenance tidak tersedia:

Claim Confidence ↓

dan limitation harus dinyatakan.

⸻

27\. Reporting Claim Types

Report harus membedakan:

Fact

Direct evidence-supported statement.

Measurement

Recorded result.

Evaluation

Interpretation against approved criterion.

Finding

Material conclusion.

Recommendation

Proposed response.

Decision

Authorized conclusion.

Forecast

Forward-looking estimate.

Forecast tidak boleh disajikan sebagai actual.

⸻

28\. Reporting Confidence

Material report claims harus dapat mengkomunikasikan confidence ketika uncertainty relevan.

Example:

Certification Readiness:

At Risk

Confidence:

Medium

Reason:

Required evidence set is incomplete for one applicable control.

Confidence tidak menggantikan evidence.

⸻

29\. Reporting Exceptions

Report harus menyediakan explicit treatment untuk:

• missing data;

• incomplete evidence;

• delayed evidence;

• invalid measurement;

• anomalous result;

• disputed result;

• pending validation;

• unresolved attribution.

Example:

Exception:

Control evidence incomplete.

Impact:

Control performance conclusion has reduced confidence.

Status:

Open.

⸻

30\. Materiality and Aggregation

Aggregation tidak boleh menyembunyikan material exceptions.

Canonical:

Platform Aggregate

&nbsp; +

Material Exception

&nbsp; ↓

Must remain visible

Jika aggregated status = GREEN tetapi critical exception exists:

GREEN

&nbsp; ≠

No Material Issue

Material exception harus dipresentasikan secara explicit.

⸻

31\. Dashboard

Performance Dashboard merupakan salah satu primary performance reporting outputs dalam reference E7 architecture.

Untuk PD-03, dashboard dapat digunakan sebagai controlled information surface untuk:

Governance Overview

&nbsp; ↓

Policy

&nbsp; ↓

Control

&nbsp; ↓

Certification

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Standards

&nbsp; ↓

Material Alerts

&nbsp; ↓

Trend

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence / Confidence

&nbsp; ↓

Drill-down

Dashboard bukan performance reality.

Dashboard bukan authority.

Dashboard tidak boleh menjadi mekanisme untuk menghilangkan material exceptions.

⸻

32\. Dashboard Drill-Down

Progressive disclosure:

Level 1

PD-03 Governance Summary

&nbsp; ↓

Level 2

Governance Area

&nbsp; ↓

KPI / Metric

&nbsp; ↓

Level 4

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Report

&nbsp; ↓

Finding / Gap

E7 tidak menjalankan improvement action secara otomatis.

⸻

43\. Reporting Lifecycle

Canonical lifecycle:

Prepare

&nbsp; ↓

Validate

&nbsp; ↓

Review

&nbsp; ↓

Approve / Authorize Publication

&nbsp; ↓

Publish

&nbsp; ↓

Distribute

&nbsp; ↓

Consume

&nbsp; ↓

Amend if Required

&nbsp; ↓

Archive

Exact approval authority untuk PD-03 reporting remains SOURCE GAP unless specified by authoritative governance source.

⸻

44\. Report Quality Gates

Formal report publication harus memenuhi:

RQ-01 — Source Validation

Source data validated.

RQ-02 — Evaluation Traceability

Evaluation basis identifiable.

RQ-03 — Material Exception Disclosure

Material exceptions disclosed.

RQ-04 — Audience Classification

Audience and classification confirmed.

RQ-05 — Authority Boundary

Report does not create authority.

RQ-06 — Traceability

Material claims traceable.

RQ-07 — Decision/Recommendation Separation

Recommendation and decision are distinct.

Jika gate gagal secara material:

Hold

OR

Qualified Report

OR

Explicit Limitation

⸻

45\. Report States

Recommended canonical state model:

DRAFT

PENDING VALIDATION

PENDING REVIEW

QUALIFIED

PUBLISHED

CORRECTED

AMENDED

SUPERSEDED

WITHDRAWN

ARCHIVED

QUALIFIED digunakan ketika report tetap perlu dikomunikasikan tetapi memiliki limitation yang telah dinyatakan.

CLOSED tidak boleh digunakan untuk menyembunyikan unresolved material condition.

⸻

46\. Historical Integrity

Report history harus mempertahankan:

• original version;

• publication date;

• correction;

• amendment;

• reason;

• responsible owner;

• applicable evidence;

• supersession relationship.

Historical report tidak boleh diam-diam ditulis ulang untuk menghapus unfavorable result.

⸻

47\. Material Correction

Jika material error ditemukan:

Original Report

&nbsp; ↓

Correction Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Correction / Amendment

&nbsp; ↓

Updated Report

&nbsp; ↓

Audit Trail

Correction tidak boleh menghapus original historical state tanpa traceability.

⸻

48\. Anti-Gaming Principle

Reporting harus mencegah:

• selective KPI presentation;

• cherry-picking period;

• hiding failed controls;

• hiding certification gaps;

• denominator manipulation;

• threshold manipulation;

• unexplained metric definition changes;

• suppressing negative trend;

• relabeling failure as "closed".

Performance reporting harus mencerminkan condition yang dapat dipertanggungjawabkan.

⸻

49\. Context Integrity

Setiap material performance claim harus mempertahankan context yang diperlukan.

Minimal:

Actual

Target / Criterion

Variance

Period

Trend

Confidence

Limitation

Owner

Context boleh disederhanakan untuk executive consumer hanya apabila simplification tidak mengubah material meaning.

⸻

50\. Decision Support

Decision-support package dapat berisi:

Decision Required

Issue

Evidence

Evaluation

Impact

Risk

Options

Recommendation

Confidence

Dependencies

Authority

Tetapi:

Decision Support

&nbsp; ≠

Decision

Decision remains with legitimate authority.

⸻

51\. Reporting and Accountability

Material report harus dapat menunjukkan, apabila applicable:

Objective Owner

Domain Owner

Performance Owner

Decision Owner

Action Owner

Role tersebut tidak boleh dicampur hanya karena satu report mengandung semua informasi.

Performance reporting tidak memindahkan accountability.

⸻

52\. Reporting Ownership

Reference model menetapkan Performance Management Office sebagai owner atas performance-management capability.

Untuk PD-03:

Performance Capability Owner

&nbsp; ↓

ESD-08 / Performance Management Office

sementara:

PD-03 Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Governance & Compliance Domain

&nbsp; ≠

Governance & Compliance Domain Ownership

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Governance Reporting

&nbsp; ↓

Other Platform Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Other Platform Domain Owner

63\. Canonical Boundary Statement

Performance Reporting is not authority.

Performance Reporting is not measurement.

Performance Reporting is not evaluation.

Performance Reporting is not review.

Performance Reporting is not improvement execution.

Performance Reporting communicates validated performance information to legitimate consumers while preserving provenance, context, accountability, confidentiality, and authority boundaries.

For PD-03 specifically:

Performance Reporting communicates the performance of Governance & Compliance — including Governance Policy, Governance Control, Certification, and Governance Standards — without transferring ownership of other Platform domains or creating authority beyond the approved governance architecture.

\# 65. Canonical Freeze Statement

\> \*\*E7 — Performance Reporting establishes the communication architecture through which validated performance evidence, approved evaluation, findings, and decision-support information relating to PD-03 Governance & Compliance are communicated to legitimate consumers.\*\*

\> \*\*E7 preserves the distinction between measurement, evaluation, review, governance, reporting, and decision. Reporting does not modify measurement results, create new evaluation conclusions, create authority, transfer accountability, or execute improvement.\*\*

\> \*\*For PD-03, reporting is bounded to Governance & Compliance performance, including Governance Policy, Governance Control, Certification, and Governance Standards, while preserving the ownership and execution responsibility of other AIOS Platform Divisions.\*\*

\> \*\*Local PD-03 reporting catalogue, cadence, audience matrix, publication authority, classification, distribution rules, and repository schema remain SOURCE GAP until authoritative implementation sources are reconciled.\*\*

\> \*\*E7 — Performance Reporting is therefore FROZEN as a source-bounded, domain-adapted Performance Reporting Architecture for Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance.\*\*

\# 1. Purpose

E7 menetapkan bagaimana informasi performance PD-03 dikemas, dikomunikasikan, didistribusikan, dan digunakan untuk mendukung review, governance response, serta authorized decision-making.

E7 menjawab:

\> \*\*"Bagaimana performance Governance & Compliance dikomunikasikan kepada consumer yang berwenang?"\*\*

E7 merupakan communication layer antara:

\`\`\`text

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Evaluation

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Reporting

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Consumer

&nbsp; ↓

Governance / Executive / Domain Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Response

E7 tidak menggantikan:

- measurement;
- evaluation;
- performance review;
- governance authority;
- architecture authority;
- strategic authority;
- operational authority.

⸻

**2\. Constitutional Position**

Canonical Part E relationship:

E1 — Performance Constitution

&nbsp; ↓

"What is performance?"

&nbsp; ↓

E2 — Performance Model

&nbsp; ↓

"What dimensions describe performance?"

&nbsp; ↓

E3 — Performance Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

"How is performance measured?"

&nbsp; ↓

E4 — KPI & Success Metrics

&nbsp; ↓

"What indicates success?"

&nbsp; ↓

E5 — Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

"How is performance evaluated?"

&nbsp; ↓

E6 — Performance Governance

&nbsp; ↓

"Who owns, governs, reviews, escalates?"

&nbsp; ↓

E7 — Performance Reporting

&nbsp; ↓

"How is performance communicated?"

&nbsp; ↓

E8 — Performance Improvement

E7 tidak mendefinisikan ulang predecessor.

E7 menyajikan hasil yang telah dihasilkan oleh E3–E6 kepada consumer yang legitimate.

⸻

**3\. Core Reporting Principle**

Performance reporting translates validated performance evidence and approved evaluation into the appropriate level of information for the appropriate consumer, while preserving context, provenance, accountability, and authority boundaries.

Reporting harus:

- accurate;
- traceable;
- contextual;
- proportionate;
- audience-appropriate;
- timely;
- decision-useful;
- access-controlled;
- historically consistent.

Reporting tidak boleh menjadi:

- data dump;
- KPI wall;
- dashboard decoration;
- unsupported executive summary;
- selective presentation;
- hidden re-evaluation mechanism.

⸻

**4\. PD-03 Reporting Context**

PD-03 memiliki primary organizational responsibility atas:

Governance & Compliance

dengan responsibility yang telah ditetapkan terhadap:

Policy

Control

Certification

Governance Standards

E7 karena itu terutama melaporkan performance yang berkaitan dengan Governance & Compliance, bukan seluruh performance execution AIOS.

Canonical boundary:

PD-03 Performance Reporting

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├── Governance Policy Performance

&nbsp; ├── Governance Control Performance

&nbsp; ├── Certification Performance

&nbsp; └── Governance Standards Performance

⸻

**5\. Reporting Architecture**

E3 — Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

E4 — KPI / Success Metrics

&nbsp; ↓

E5 — Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

E6 — Governance

&nbsp; ↓

Validated Performance Evidence

&nbsp; +

Approved Evaluation / Finding

&nbsp; ↓

E7 — Performance Reporting

&nbsp; ↓

┌────────────────────┬────────────────────┬───────────────────┐

│ │ │

▼ ▼ ▼

Governance Platform Executive / Cross-

Reporting Reporting Platform Reporting

│ │ │

└────────────────────┼────────────────────┘

&nbsp; ▼

&nbsp; Authorized Consumer

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ▼

&nbsp; Authorized Response

Reporting hierarchy tidak menjadi authority hierarchy.

⸻

**6\. Reporting vs Measurement**

E3 menjawab:

"Apa yang diukur dan apa hasil pengukurannya?"

E7 menjawab:

"Bagaimana hasil tersebut dikomunikasikan?"

Canonical:

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Actual Result

&nbsp; ↓

Validation

&nbsp; ↓

Record

&nbsp; ↓

Reporting

E7 tidak boleh mengubah actual result untuk menghasilkan presentation yang lebih favorable.

Jika measurement memiliki limitation:

Result

-

Limitation

-

Confidence

harus tetap terlihat ketika material terhadap interpretation.

⸻

**7\. Reporting vs Evaluation**

Measurement

"What happened?"

&nbsp; ↓

Evaluation

"What does it mean?"

&nbsp; ↓

Reporting

"How is it communicated?"

Contoh:

Actual:

Certification readiness = 82%

Evaluation:

Below approved readiness condition

Reporting:

Certification readiness is below the approved

condition, with identified evidence gaps.

E7 tidak boleh mengubah evaluation conclusion tanpa kembali ke applicable review mechanism.

⸻

**8\. Reporting vs Performance Review**

Performance Review menghasilkan:

- assessment;
- gap;
- finding;
- outcome;
- improvement signal.

Performance Reporting menyajikan hasil tersebut.

Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Approved / Recorded Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Report

Jika report membutuhkan interpretation baru yang materially changes the conclusion:

Return to E5

Performance Review

E7 tidak boleh menjadi hidden review mechanism.

⸻

**9\. Reporting vs Governance**

E6 menjawab:

"Siapa memiliki ownership, authority, accountability, dan escalation path?"

E7 menjawab:

"Informasi apa yang harus dikomunikasikan kepada siapa?"

Canonical:

E6 — Governance

&nbsp; ↓

Authority / Ownership / Accountability

&nbsp; ↓

E7 — Reporting

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Information

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Consumer

Reporting tidak menciptakan governance authority.

⸻

**10\. PD-03 Reporting Scope**

E7 PD-03 mencakup reporting atas:

10.1 Governance Policy

Performance information mengenai:

- policy effectiveness;
- policy adherence;
- policy exceptions;
- policy-related findings;
- policy improvement signals.

10.2 Governance Control

Performance information mengenai:

- control condition;
- control effectiveness;
- control evidence;
- control exceptions;
- control findings.

10.3 Certification

Performance information mengenai:

- certification status;
- readiness;
- evidence completeness;
- certification finding;
- certification gap;
- remediation status.

10.4 Governance Standards

Performance information mengenai:

- standards adoption;
- standards adherence;
- standards effectiveness;
- standards exceptions;
- standards-related findings.

⸻

**11\. Governance Policy Reporting**

Policy reporting harus membedakan:

Policy Definition

&nbsp; ↓

Policy Applicability

&nbsp; ↓

Policy Adoption

&nbsp; ↓

Policy Adherence

&nbsp; ↓

Policy Effectiveness

&nbsp; ↓

Policy Finding

PD-03 tidak boleh menggunakan performance reporting sebagai mekanisme unilateral untuk mengubah policy.

Jika policy change diperlukan:

Performance Report

&nbsp; ↓

Policy Implication

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Policy Decision

⸻

**12\. Governance Control Reporting**

Control reporting harus mempertahankan distinction:

Governance Control Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Control Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Finding

PD-03 memiliki governance responsibility.

Namun:

Governance Control Ownership

&nbsp; ≠

Operational Control Execution

Platform/domain owner tetap accountable terhadap execution yang berada dalam domainnya.

⸻

**13\. Certification Reporting**

Certification reporting harus menyediakan:

Certification Status

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence Condition

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Gap

&nbsp; ↓

Required Response

Certification status tidak boleh disajikan sebagai successful hanya karena:

- documentation exists;
- action completed;
- dashboard status green;
- evidence volume high.

Conclusion harus berbasis applicable evidence dan review.

⸻

**14\. Governance Standards Reporting**

Governance Standards reporting harus membedakan:

- standard existence;
- applicability;
- adoption;
- adherence;
- effectiveness;
- exception;
- remediation.

Governance Standards:

Governance Standard

&nbsp; ≠

All Technical Standards

Technical standard performance tetap berada pada applicable technical/domain owner.

⸻

**15\. Reporting Hierarchy**

PD-03 dapat menggunakan decision-context reporting hierarchy:

Level 1 — Evidence / Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Level 2 — Governance Domain

&nbsp; ↓

Level 3 — PD-03 Platform

&nbsp; ↓

Level 4 — Executive / Cross-Platform

Hierarchy tersebut menentukan:

- aggregation;
- context;
- audience;
- decision-support need.

Hierarchy tersebut tidak menentukan authority.

⸻

**16\. Level 1 — Evidence Reporting**

Purpose:

Menyediakan evidence dan measurement result yang dapat ditelusuri.

Typical content:

Metric ID

Measurement Period

Actual

Unit

Source

Validation Status

Evidence Reference

Method Version

Confidence

Limitation

Consumers dapat mencakup:

- Performance Management;
- Metric Owner;
- PD-03 Domain Owner;
- Reviewer;
- authorized analyst.

⸻

**17\. Level 2 — Governance Domain Reporting**

Purpose:

Menjelaskan performance Governance & Compliance dalam konteks responsibility PD-03.

Typical content:

Governance Area

Current State

Expected Condition

Variance

Trend

Evidence Confidence

Material Finding

Exception

Action

Escalation

Governance domain reporting dapat dipisahkan menjadi:

Policy

Control

Certification

Governance Standards

⸻

**18\. Level 3 — PD-03 Platform Reporting**

Platform report harus memberikan integrated view:

PD-03 Performance

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├── Governance Policy

&nbsp; ├── Governance Control

&nbsp; ├── Certification

&nbsp; └── Governance Standards

Jika E2 menetapkan performance dimensions yang lebih luas untuk inherited reference model, PD-03-specific implementation harus menggunakannya hanya sejauh applicable dan tanpa mengubah PD-03 domain boundary.

⸻

**19\. Platform Performance Report**

Minimum structure:

\# PD-03 Performance Report

\## Executive Summary

\## Governance & Compliance Status

\## Governance Policy Performance

\## Governance Control Performance

\## Certification Performance

\## Governance Standards Performance

\## Material Findings

\## Material Variance

\## Trend

\## Exceptions

\## Cross-Platform Dependencies

\## Open Actions

\## Escalations

\## Review Requirements

\## Evidence Confidence

\## Data / Measurement Limitations

\## Source / Traceability

Actual PD-03 report catalogue remains SOURCE GAP until authoritative local implementation source is identified.

⸻

**20\. Level 4 — Executive / Cross-Platform Reporting**

Executive reporting harus memprioritaskan:

- materiality;
- strategic/governance significance;
- risk;
- unresolved condition;
- cross-platform impact;
- decision relevance.

Minimum:

Executive Summary

&nbsp; ↓

Material Governance Signals

&nbsp; ↓

Critical Exceptions

&nbsp; ↓

Certification / Compliance Signals

&nbsp; ↓

Cross-Platform Dependencies

&nbsp; ↓

Required Review

&nbsp; ↓

Decision Support

Executive reporting tidak menciptakan Executive Authority.

⸻

**21\. Executive Reporting Boundary**

Report dapat:

- inform;
- summarize;
- identify;
- signal;
- recommend;
- escalate.

Report tidak dapat:

- decide;
- authorize;
- change governance;
- change architecture;
- change strategy;
- change operational ownership.

Canonical:

Performance Report

&nbsp; ↓

Decision Support

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Decision Maker

&nbsp; ↓

Decision

⸻

**22\. Decision-Support Boundary**

E7 dapat menyediakan:

Decision Context

Evidence

Evaluation

Finding

Risk

Impact

Recommendation

Decision Required

Namun:

Recommendation

&nbsp; ≠

Decision

dan:

Decision Support

&nbsp; ≠

Decision Authority

⸻

**23\. Reporting Consumer Model**

Potential consumers:

PD-03 Governance Authority

PD-03 Domain Owner

Performance Management

Executive Authority

Applicable Platform Owner

Architecture Authority

Security Authority

Quality Authority

Authorized Reviewer

Consumer harus ditentukan berdasarkan legitimate need.

Exact PD-03 audience matrix belum ditemukan dalam authoritative source.

Therefore:

PD-03 Audience Matrix

\= SOURCE GAP

⸻

**24\. Transparency Principle**

Transparency means appropriate visibility to legitimate consumers, not unrestricted disclosure.

Reporting harus mempertimbangkan:

- classification;
- sensitivity;
- legitimate need;
- governance requirements;
- confidentiality;
- access boundary.

Informasi restricted tetap harus memiliki traceability.

⸻

**25\. Access Boundary**

Report distribution harus dapat menunjukkan:

Audience

Classification

Distribution Scope

Publication Status

Owner

Publication Date

Jangan menganggap:

Higher Organizational Position

&nbsp; =

Automatic Access

Access tetap mengikuti applicable governance requirements.

⸻

**26\. Evidence and Provenance**

Setiap material report claim harus dapat ditelusuri:

Source

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Metric / KPI

&nbsp; ↓

Evaluation

&nbsp; ↓

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Report

&nbsp; ↓

Decision / Action

Jika provenance tidak tersedia:

Claim Confidence ↓

dan limitation harus dinyatakan.

⸻

**27\. Reporting Claim Types**

Report harus membedakan:

Fact

Direct evidence-supported statement.

Measurement

Recorded result.

Evaluation

Interpretation against approved criterion.

Finding

Material conclusion.

Recommendation

Proposed response.

Decision

Authorized conclusion.

Forecast

Forward-looking estimate.

Forecast tidak boleh disajikan sebagai actual.

⸻

**28\. Reporting Confidence**

Material report claims harus dapat mengkomunikasikan confidence ketika uncertainty relevan.

Example:

Certification Readiness:

At Risk

Confidence:

Medium

Reason:

Required evidence set is incomplete for one applicable control.

Confidence tidak menggantikan evidence.

⸻

**29\. Reporting Exceptions**

Report harus menyediakan explicit treatment untuk:

- missing data;
- incomplete evidence;
- delayed evidence;
- invalid measurement;
- anomalous result;
- disputed result;
- pending validation;
- unresolved attribution.

Example:

Exception:

Control evidence incomplete.

Impact:

Control performance conclusion has reduced confidence.

Status:

Open.

⸻

**30\. Materiality and Aggregation**

Aggregation tidak boleh menyembunyikan material exceptions.

Canonical:

Platform Aggregate

&nbsp; +

Material Exception

&nbsp; ↓

Must remain visible

Jika aggregated status = GREEN tetapi critical exception exists:

GREEN

&nbsp; ≠

No Material Issue

Material exception harus dipresentasikan secara explicit.

⸻

**31\. Dashboard**

Performance Dashboard merupakan salah satu primary performance reporting outputs dalam reference E7 architecture.

Untuk PD-03, dashboard dapat digunakan sebagai controlled information surface untuk:

Governance Overview

&nbsp; ↓

Policy

&nbsp; ↓

Control

&nbsp; ↓

Certification

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Standards

&nbsp; ↓

Material Alerts

&nbsp; ↓

Trend

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence / Confidence

&nbsp; ↓

Drill-down

Dashboard bukan performance reality.

Dashboard bukan authority.

Dashboard tidak boleh menjadi mekanisme untuk menghilangkan material exceptions.

⸻

**32\. Dashboard Drill-Down**

Progressive disclosure:

Level 1

PD-03 Governance Summary

&nbsp; ↓

Level 2

Governance Area

&nbsp; ↓

Level 3

KPI / Metric

&nbsp; ↓

Level 4

Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Level 5

Evidence

Access ke lower levels mengikuti legitimate access boundary.

KPI Report dapat menggunakan:

KPI ID

Objective

Success Criterion

KPI

Target

Actual

Variance

Trend

Status

Period

Evaluation

Finding

Action

E7 tidak membuat KPI baru.

Evaluation Result harus berbeda dari raw measurement.

Minimum:

Evaluation ID

Objective

Criterion

Actual

Expected

Gap

Trend

Context

Assessment

Materiality

Confidence

Finding

Recommendation

Review Reference

E7 menyajikan evaluation result.

Canonical package:

PD-03 Executive Performance Package

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; └── Evidence References

Exact PD-03 package structure remains SOURCE GAP until authoritative source confirms it.

**36\. Cross-Platform Reporting**

PD-03 dapat melaporkan governance-related performance conditions yang bergantung pada:

PD-02 Architecture

PD-08 Security

PD-09 Quality

Other Applicable Platforms

Relationship:

PD-03 Governance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Cross-Platform Dependency

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Domain Owner

Cross-platform reporting tidak memindahkan ownership.

⸻

**37\. Architecture Reporting Boundary**

Jika report menunjukkan architecture-related concern:

PD-03 Performance Report

&nbsp; ↓

Architecture Signal

&nbsp; ↓

PD-02 Architecture Review

E7 tidak dapat mengubah architecture.

Architecture decision tetap mengikuti Architecture Governance.

⸻

**38\. Security Reporting Boundary**

Jika report menunjukkan security-related concern:

PD-03 Performance Report

&nbsp; ↓

Security Signal

&nbsp; ↓

PD-08 Review / Applicable Authority

PD-03 tidak menjadi security execution owner.

⸻

**39\. Quality Reporting Boundary**

Jika report menunjukkan quality-related concern:

PD-03 Performance Report

&nbsp; ↓

Quality Signal

&nbsp; ↓

PD-09 Review / Applicable Authority

PD-03 tidak menjadi quality execution owner.

⸻

**40\. Certification Reporting and Subject Ownership**

Certification report dapat mengidentifikasi gap pada Platform yang menjadi subject.

Namun:

Certification Finding

&nbsp; ≠

PD-03 Execution Ownership

Subject Platform tetap accountable terhadap execution dalam domainnya.

⸻

**41\. Reporting and Escalation**

E7 dapat memicu escalation.

Canonical:

Performance Report

&nbsp; ↓

Material Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Escalation Trigger

&nbsp; ↓

E6 Governance

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Authority

Reporting tidak mengambil alih escalation authority.

⸻

**42\. Reporting and Improvement**

E7 dapat menghasilkan improvement signal.

Performance Report

&nbsp; ↓

Finding / Gap

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Signal

&nbsp; ↓

E8 — Performance Improvement

E8 kemudian mengatur improvement mechanism.

E7 tidak menjalankan improvement action secara otomatis.

⸻

**43\. Reporting Lifecycle**

Canonical lifecycle:

Prepare

&nbsp; ↓

Validate

&nbsp; ↓

Review

&nbsp; ↓

Approve / Authorize Publication

&nbsp; ↓

Publish

&nbsp; ↓

Distribute

&nbsp; ↓

Consume

&nbsp; ↓

Amend if Required

&nbsp; ↓

Archive

Exact approval authority untuk PD-03 reporting remains SOURCE GAP unless specified by authoritative governance source.

⸻

**44\. Report Quality Gates**

Formal report publication harus memenuhi:

RQ-01 — Source Validation

Source data validated.

RQ-02 — Evaluation Traceability

Evaluation basis identifiable.

RQ-03 — Material Exception Disclosure

Material exceptions disclosed.

RQ-04 — Audience Classification

Audience and classification confirmed.

RQ-05 — Authority Boundary

Report does not create authority.

RQ-06 — Traceability

Material claims traceable.

RQ-07 — Decision/Recommendation Separation

Recommendation and decision are distinct.

Jika gate gagal secara material:

Hold

OR

Qualified Report

OR

Explicit Limitation

⸻

**45\. Report States**

Recommended canonical state model:

DRAFT

PENDING VALIDATION

PENDING REVIEW

QUALIFIED

PUBLISHED

CORRECTED

AMENDED

SUPERSEDED

WITHDRAWN

ARCHIVED

QUALIFIED digunakan ketika report tetap perlu dikomunikasikan tetapi memiliki limitation yang telah dinyatakan.

CLOSED tidak boleh digunakan untuk menyembunyikan unresolved material condition.

⸻

**46\. Historical Integrity**

Report history harus mempertahankan:

- original version;
- publication date;
- correction;
- amendment;
- reason;
- responsible owner;
- applicable evidence;
- supersession relationship.

Historical report tidak boleh diam-diam ditulis ulang untuk menghapus unfavorable result.

⸻

**47\. Material Correction**

Jika material error ditemukan:

Original Report

&nbsp; ↓

Correction Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Correction / Amendment

&nbsp; ↓

Updated Report

&nbsp; ↓

Audit Trail

Correction tidak boleh menghapus original historical state tanpa traceability.

⸻

**48\. Anti-Gaming Principle**

Reporting harus mencegah:

- selective KPI presentation;
- cherry-picking period;
- hiding failed controls;
- hiding certification gaps;
- denominator manipulation;
- threshold manipulation;
- unexplained metric definition changes;
- suppressing negative trend;
- relabeling failure as "closed".

Performance reporting harus mencerminkan condition yang dapat dipertanggungjawabkan.

⸻

**49\. Context Integrity**

Setiap material performance claim harus mempertahankan context yang diperlukan.

Minimal:

Actual

Target / Criterion

Variance

Period

Trend

Confidence

Limitation

Owner

Context boleh disederhanakan untuk executive consumer hanya apabila simplification tidak mengubah material meaning.

⸻

**50\. Decision Support**

Decision-support package dapat berisi:

Decision Required

Issue

Evidence

Evaluation

Impact

Risk

Options

Recommendation

Confidence

Dependencies

Authority

Tetapi:

Decision Support

&nbsp; ≠

Decision

Decision remains with legitimate authority.

⸻

**51\. Reporting and Accountability**

Material report harus dapat menunjukkan, apabila applicable:

Objective Owner

Domain Owner

Performance Owner

Decision Owner

Action Owner

Role tersebut tidak boleh dicampur hanya karena satu report mengandung semua informasi.

Performance reporting tidak memindahkan accountability.

⸻

**52\. Reporting Ownership**

Reference model menetapkan Performance Management Office sebagai owner atas performance-management capability.

Untuk PD-03:

Performance Capability Owner

&nbsp; ↓

ESD-08 / Performance Management Office

sementara:

PD-03 Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Governance & Compliance Domain

Relationship:

Performance Capability Ownership

&nbsp; ≠

Governance & Compliance Domain Ownership

⸻

**53\. Domain Evidence Ownership**

Domain owner tetap memiliki ownership atas domain evidence/result.

Untuk PD-03:

PD-03

&nbsp; ↓

Governance & Compliance Evidence

Untuk cross-platform concern:

PD-03 Governance Reporting

&nbsp; ↓

Other Platform Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Other Platform Domain Owner

PD-03 tidak mengklaim ownership atas evidence hanya karena evidence tersebut muncul dalam governance report.

⸻

**54\. Reporting Governance**

E7 berada di bawah governance E6.

Relationship:

E6 — Performance Governance

&nbsp; ↓

Ownership / Authority / Accountability

&nbsp; ↓

E7 — Performance Reporting

&nbsp; ↓

Information Communication

Changes affecting:

- reporting authority;
- publication authority;
- access boundary;
- accountability;
- governance interface;

must follow applicable change control.

⸻

**55\. Reporting Change Control**

Material changes include:

- reporting hierarchy;
- authority relationship;
- access boundary;
- report governance;
- material reporting lifecycle;
- decision-support authority;
- cross-platform governance interface.

Non-material formatting changes may follow operational document control if permitted by governance.

E7 itself must not be materially changed outside Architecture Change Control.

⸻

**56\. Local PD-03 Source Gaps**

The authoritative sources reviewed do not explicitly establish:

1. exact PD-03 report catalogue;
2. exact PD-03 dashboard catalogue;
3. exact PD-03 reporting cadence;
4. exact PD-03 audience matrix;
5. exact PD-03 publication approval chain;
6. exact PD-03 report classification matrix;
7. exact PD-03 reporting thresholds;
8. exact PD-03 distribution rules;
9. exact PD-03 reporting repository schema.

Therefore:

PD-03 Local Reporting Implementation

\= SOURCE GAP

These gaps must not be silently converted into mandatory architecture.

⸻

PD-03 Reporting Catalogue

PD-03 Dashboard

REFERENCE-INHERITED

SOURCE GAP

must remain explicit.

⸻

**58\. Non-Goals**

E7 does not:

- create new KPI;
- create new performance criteria;
- modify measurement result;
- modify target;
- create evaluation conclusion;
- create governance authority;
- create architecture authority;
- create strategic authority;
- create operational authority;
- assign organizational sanction;
- transfer domain ownership;
- transfer accountability;
- execute improvement;
- change policy;
- change governance baseline;
- change architecture baseline;
- replace E5 review;
- replace E6 governance.

⸻

**59\. Success Criteria**

E7 PD-03 is successful when:

1. material PD-03 performance information has an identifiable legitimate consumer;
2. reporting can distinguish evidence from evaluation;
3. reporting can distinguish evaluation from decision;
4. Governance Policy performance can be reported;
5. Governance Control performance can be reported;
6. Certification performance can be reported;
7. Governance Standards performance can be reported;
8. material exceptions remain visible;
9. reporting claims retain evidence provenance;
10. confidence and limitation can be communicated;
11. report access follows legitimate boundaries;
12. reporting hierarchy does not become authority hierarchy;
13. reporting does not transfer accountability;
14. cross-platform reporting preserves domain ownership;
15. architecture concerns are routed to Architecture Governance;
16. security concerns are routed to applicable Security authority;
17. quality concerns are routed to applicable Quality authority;
18. material findings can trigger escalation through E6;
19. improvement signals can feed E8;
20. historical report integrity is preserved;
21. material corrections are auditable;
22. anti-gaming controls are maintained;
23. recommendation and decision remain distinct;
24. source gaps remain explicit;
25. local implementation can be added without changing the frozen reporting principles.

⸻

**60\. Source-Fidelity Qualification**

**SOURCE-SUPPORTED**

The reviewed sources support:

- E7 as Performance Reporting;
- reporting as communication/translation layer;
- reporting hierarchy;
- executive/platform/domain reporting;
- transparency with legitimate access boundaries;
- decision-support boundary;
- provenance and traceability;
- anti-gaming/context integrity;
- historical integrity;
- Performance Dashboard;
- KPI Report;
- Evaluation Result;
- E6 governance relationship;
- preservation of domain ownership.

**PD-03 SOURCE-SUPPORTED**

PD-03 baseline supports:

Governance & Compliance

&nbsp; ├── Policy

&nbsp; ├── Control

&nbsp; ├── Certification

&nbsp; └── Governance Standards

and preserves:

Architecture ≠ PD-03 ownership

Security ≠ PD-03 ownership

Quality ≠ PD-03 ownership

Technical Design ≠ PD-03 ownership

**BOUNDED SYNTHESIS**

This section adapts the frozen E7 reference model to:

PD-03 Governance & Compliance

The adaptation is bounded to:

Policy

Control

Certification

Governance Standards

No new authority is created.

**SOURCE GAP**

The following remain unresolved until authoritative PD-03 implementation sources are found:

Local Report Catalogue

Local Dashboard Catalogue

Local Audience Matrix

Local Reporting Cadence

Local Publication Authority

Local Classification Matrix

Local Distribution Rules

Local Reporting Repository Schema

Local Reporting Thresholds

⸻

**63\. Canonical Boundary Statement**

Performance Reporting is not authority.

Performance Reporting is not measurement.

Performance Reporting is not evaluation.

Performance Reporting is not review.

Performance Reporting is not improvement execution.

Performance Reporting communicates validated performance information to legitimate consumers while preserving provenance, context, accountability, confidentiality, and authority boundaries.

For PD-03 specifically:

Performance Reporting communicates the performance of Governance & Compliance — including Governance Policy, Governance Control, Certification, and Governance Standards — without transferring ownership of other Platform domains or creating authority beyond the approved governance architecture.

\# 65. Canonical Freeze Statement

\> \*\*E7 — Performance Reporting establishes the communication architecture through which validated performance evidence, approved evaluation, findings, and decision-support information relating to PD-03 Governance & Compliance are communicated to legitimate consumers.\*\*

\> \*\*E7 preserves the distinction between measurement, evaluation, review, governance, reporting, and decision. Reporting does not modify measurement results, create new evaluation conclusions, create authority, transfer accountability, or execute improvement.\*\*

\> \*\*For PD-03, reporting is bounded to Governance & Compliance performance, including Governance Policy, Governance Control, Certification, and Governance Standards, while preserving the ownership and execution responsibility of other AIOS Platform Divisions.\*\*

\> \*\*Local PD-03 reporting catalogue, cadence, audience matrix, publication authority, classification, distribution rules, and repository schema remain SOURCE GAP until authoritative implementation sources are reconciled.\*\*

\> \*\*E7 — Performance Reporting is therefore FROZEN as a source-bounded, domain-adapted Performance Reporting Architecture for Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance.\*\*

\---

⸻

1\. Purpose

E8 menetapkan bagaimana hasil performance PD-03 digunakan untuk:

• mengidentifikasi performance gap;

• menentukan improvement response;

• mengelola corrective action;

• mengelola improvement initiative;

• melakukan re-measurement;

• menilai effectiveness;

• mengidentifikasi residual gap;

• menghasilkan learning;

• melakukan optimization;

• mengembalikan hasil ke performance cycle berikutnya.

E8 menjawab:

"Bagaimana performance Governance & Compliance digunakan untuk melakukan improvement secara evidence-based dan terkontrol?"

E8 bukan sekadar action tracker.

⸻

2\. Constitutional Position

Canonical Part E:

E1 — Performance Constitution

&nbsp; ↓

E2 — Performance Model

&nbsp; ↓

E3 — Performance Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

E4 — KPI & Success Metrics

&nbsp; ↓

E5 — Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

E6 — Performance Governance

&nbsp; ↓

E7 — Performance Reporting

&nbsp; ↓

E8 — Performance Improvement

&nbsp; ↓

E9 — Performance Maturity

&nbsp; ↓

E10 — Performance Success Constitution

E8 menerima output dari E5 dan E7, menggunakan governance boundary E6, lalu menghasilkan feedback menuju measurement dan performance cycle berikutnya.

Reference architecture menetapkan E8 sebagai layer yang menjawab "How is performance used to improve?" dan mengembalikan hasil ke re-measurement/next performance cycle.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

3\. Core Improvement Principle

Performance improvement is an evidence-driven process that converts validated performance gaps and findings into authorized responses, verifies their effectiveness through re-measurement, and feeds the resulting learning into the next performance cycle.

Karena itu:

Performance Gap

&nbsp; ≠

Improvement Action

Action Completed

&nbsp; ≠

Improvement Effective

Improvement Effective

&nbsp; =

Evidence of improved condition

-

Re-measurement

-

Effectiveness Assessment

Pemisahan antara action completion dan improvement effectiveness merupakan invariant canonical E8.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

4\. PD-03 Improvement Boundary

E8 PD-03 berlaku terhadap performance dalam domain:

Governance & Compliance

dengan primary responsibility yang mencakup:

Governance Policy

Governance Control

Certification

Governance Standards

PD-03 tidak memperoleh ownership atas execution Platform lain hanya karena improvement membutuhkan tindakan pada Platform tersebut.

Canonical boundary:

PD-03

Governance & Compliance

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Requirement / Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Execution

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Improvement Verification

PD-03 source secara eksplisit mempertahankan prinsip Governance Control ≠ Execution dan Certification ≠ Implementation.

⸻

&nbsp; ↓

┌────────────────────────────┐

│ │

▼ ▼

└─────────────┬──────────────┘

6\. Improvement vs Review

E5 bertanggung jawab terhadap formal performance review.

E5 dapat menghasilkan:

• finding;

• gap;

• improvement signal;

• corrective action requirement;

• escalation requirement.

E8 mengelola lifecycle response terhadap signal tersebut.

E5

Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Signal

&nbsp; ↓

E8

Improvement Lifecycle

E8 tidak melakukan hidden re-review.

⸻

7\. Improvement vs Reporting

E7 mengkomunikasikan:

• improvement status;

• action status;

• material gap;

• effectiveness;

• blocked action;

• recurring issue;

• improvement outcome.

E8 menghasilkan information yang kemudian dapat digunakan oleh E7.

E8 Improvement

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement State

&nbsp; ↓

E7 Reporting

Reporting tidak mengubah improvement state.

⸻

8\. Improvement vs Governance

E6 menentukan:

• authority;

• accountability;

• ownership;

• escalation;

• decision rights.

E8 menggunakan governance architecture tersebut.

E8 tidak menciptakan:

Improvement Authority

yang berada di luar governance architecture.

Canonical:

Improvement Need

&nbsp; ↓

Authority Check

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Response

&nbsp; ↓

Accountable Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Execution

&nbsp; ↓

Verification

Reference E6 secara eksplisit mensyaratkan improvement action memiliki accountable owner dan effectiveness dapat diverifikasi.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

9\. Improvement vs Execution

Performance Management tidak mengambil alih execution.

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Execution

&nbsp; ↓

New Result

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Verification

Jika improvement membutuhkan perubahan pada:

• architecture;

• strategy;

• governance;

• security;

• quality;

• operational execution;

perubahan tersebut tetap mengikuti authority domain masing-masing.

Reference E8 menetapkan bahwa architecture, strategy, dan governance changes tetap berada pada authority domain masing-masing.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

10\. Performance Gap Identification

Performance gap adalah perbedaan material antara:

Expected Condition

&nbsp; vs

Actual Condition

Gap assessment harus mempertimbangkan:

• expected condition;

• actual result;

• evidence quality;

• context;

• trend;

• materiality;

• confidence.

Expected

-

Actual

-

Evidence

-

Context

-

Trend

&nbsp; ↓

Gap Assessment

Tidak setiap variance otomatis menjadi improvement requirement.

⸻

11\. Gap Classification

Gap dapat diklasifikasikan sebagai:

No Material Gap

Ordinary Variance

Material Gap

Recurring Gap

Critical Gap

Measurement Gap

Evidence Gap

Governance Gap

Capability Gap

Execution Gap

External Constraint

Unknown Cause

Classification harus berbasis evidence.

⸻

12\. Cause Attribution

E8 tidak boleh melakukan premature attribution.

Cause harus dibedakan menjadi:

Verified Cause

Potential Cause

Unknown

Jika evidence belum cukup:

Cause Under Investigation

harus digunakan.

Jangan mengubah correlation menjadi causation.

⸻

13\. Improvement Signal

Improvement Signal adalah output yang menunjukkan bahwa performance condition membutuhkan response.

Minimum:

Improvement Signal ID

Source Finding

Observed Condition

Expected Condition

Gap

Materiality

Evidence

Confidence

Potential Cause

Recommended Response

Required Authority

Owner Requirement

Improvement signal tidak sama dengan authorized action.

⸻

14\. Improvement Decision

Possible outcomes:

Maintain

Monitor

Investigate

Re-measure

Improve

Correct

Escalate

Decision harus mengikuti authority yang berlaku.

E8 tidak boleh menganggap:

Recommendation = Authorization

⸻

15\. Improvement Initiative

Improvement Initiative digunakan ketika response membutuhkan perubahan yang lebih luas daripada corrective action sederhana.

Minimum:

Initiative ID

Objective

Problem Statement

Baseline

Expected Result

Success Criterion

Owner

Authority

Dependencies

Actions

Timeline

Risk

Measurement Method

Review Point

Exact PD-03 improvement portfolio belum tersedia dalam source.

Karena itu catalogue implementation-specific tetap:

SOURCE GAP

⸻

16\. Corrective Action

Corrective Action digunakan untuk merespons kondisi yang telah ditemukan dan membutuhkan correction.

Model:

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Cause / Contributing Factor

&nbsp; ↓

Corrective Action

&nbsp; ↓

Implementation

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Effectiveness

Corrective Action tidak boleh dianggap efektif hanya karena statusnya Completed.

⸻

17\. Improvement vs Corrective Action

Keduanya dapat coexist.

⸻

18\. Optimization

Optimization berbeda dari correction.

Correction

&nbsp; =

Mengembalikan condition ke required state

Improvement

&nbsp; =

Meningkatkan performance

Optimization

&nbsp; =

Mencari performance yang lebih baik

tanpa merusak required conditions

Optimization tidak boleh:

• mengorbankan compliance;

• mengurangi governance integrity;

• melemahkan control;

• menghapus evidence requirement;

• mengubah authority secara informal.

⸻

19\. Governance Policy Improvement

PD-03 dapat mengidentifikasi improvement terhadap Governance Policy.

Canonical:

Policy Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Policy Performance

&nbsp; ↓

Gap

&nbsp; ↓

Policy Improvement Signal

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Policy Review

&nbsp; ↓

Approved Change

&nbsp; ↓

Implementation

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measurement

E8 tidak dapat mengubah policy secara unilateral.

⸻

20\. Governance Control Improvement

Control improvement harus membedakan:

Control Design

&nbsp; vs

Control Execution

&nbsp; vs

Control Effectiveness

PD-03 dapat memiliki governance responsibility terhadap control.

Applicable domain owner tetap menjalankan implementation apabila control tersebut berada dalam domainnya.

Governance Control Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Domain

&nbsp; ↓

Control Implementation

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Effectiveness Assessment

21\. Certification Improvement

Certification improvement dapat mencakup:

• evidence completeness;

• finding remediation;

• readiness improvement;

• certification process improvement;

• recurring certification gap reduction.

Namun:

Certification Improvement

&nbsp; ≠

Subject Platform Ownership

Certification improvement tidak memindahkan execution ownership.

22\. Governance Standards Improvement

Governance Standards improvement dapat mencakup:

• standard clarity;

• applicability;

• adoption;

• adherence;

• effectiveness;

• exception reduction;

• standard lifecycle.

Technical standards tetap berada pada applicable technical/domain authority.

23\. Improvement Prioritization

Prioritization harus mempertimbangkan:

Materiality

Impact

Risk

Urgency

Recurrence

Strategic Relevance

Governance Significance

Dependency

Effort

Expected Benefit

Priority tidak boleh ditentukan hanya berdasarkan:

• visibility;

• executive preference;

• ease of completion;

• number of complaints.

Exact PD-03 prioritization threshold:

SOURCE GAP

⸻

24\. Improvement Ownership

Setiap material improvement harus memiliki:

Improvement Owner

Action Owner

Evidence Owner

Verification Owner

Decision Authority

Role tersebut dapat berbeda.

Satu role tidak boleh otomatis diasumsikan memegang seluruh responsibility.

⸻

25\. Accountability Boundary

Performance Management memiliki accountability terhadap performance-management capability.

Domain owner memiliki accountability terhadap domain execution.

Improvement action owner memiliki accountability terhadap action.

Effectiveness reviewer memiliki accountability terhadap assessment.

Canonical:

Performance Management

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Framework

Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Execution

Action Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Action Completion

Reviewer

&nbsp; ↓

Effectiveness Assessment

⸻

26\. Improvement Authorization

Sebelum material action dijalankan:

Improvement Need

&nbsp; ↓

Scope Check

&nbsp; ↓

Authority Check

&nbsp; ↓

Authorization

&nbsp; ↓

Action

Jika action berada di luar authority:

Escalate

bukan:

Proceed Anyway

⸻

27\. Improvement Dependencies

Material improvement harus mencatat dependencies.

Contoh:

Architecture Dependency

Security Dependency

Quality Dependency

Operational Dependency

Infrastructure Dependency

Governance Dependency

External Dependency

Dependency tidak berarti ownership.

PD-03 tetap mempertahankan boundary Governance & Compliance. Source PD-03 secara eksplisit menyatakan bahwa Architecture, Security, dan Quality adalah dependencies, bukan subordinate units.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

28\. Improvement Implementation

Implementation state harus dapat dibedakan dari effectiveness.

Recommended states:

Not Started

Planned

Authorized

In Progress

Implemented

Pending Re-measurement

Under Effectiveness Review

Effective

Partially Effective

Ineffective

Closed

Exception states:

Blocked

Deferred

Cancelled

Escalated

Implemented tidak sama dengan Effective.

⸻

29\. Re-measurement

Re-measurement merupakan control utama E8.

Canonical:

Baseline

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement

&nbsp; ↓

Post-Improvement Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Comparison

&nbsp; ↓

Effectiveness Assessment

Jika measurement method berubah, perubahan harus didokumentasikan dan effectiveness conclusion harus mempertimbangkannya.

⸻

30\. Effectiveness Assessment

Effectiveness menjawab:

"Apakah improvement menghasilkan perubahan yang diharapkan?"

Minimum assessment:

Expected Result

Actual Result

Baseline

Post-Improvement Result

Variance

Evidence

Confidence

Residual Gap

Side Effect

Conclusion

Possible conclusion:

Effective

Partially Effective

Ineffective

Inconclusive

⸻

31\. Effective Improvement

Improvement dapat dinyatakan Effective apabila:

0\. implementation selesai;

0\. evidence tersedia;

0\. post-improvement measurement tersedia;

0\. expected result dapat dibandingkan;

0\. improvement effect dapat dibuktikan;

0\. material residual gap telah dinilai;

0\. material negative side effect tidak diabaikan;

0\. effectiveness assessment telah dilakukan oleh role yang sesuai.

⸻

32\. Partially Effective

Partially Effective digunakan ketika:

• target hanya tercapai sebagian;

• improvement bekerja pada sebagian scope;

• residual gap masih material;

• benefit belum stabil;

• dependency masih unresolved.

Partial success tidak boleh dipresentasikan sebagai full success.

⸻

33\. Ineffective Improvement

Jika improvement ineffective:

Ineffective

&nbsp; ↓

Re-diagnosis

&nbsp; ↓

Cause Reassessment

&nbsp; ↓

New Improvement Signal

&nbsp; ↓

New / Modified Initiative

Ineffectiveness adalah evidence.

Jangan menutup action hanya untuk menghapus negative result.

⸻

34\. Inconclusive Improvement

Inconclusive digunakan jika:

• evidence insufficient;

• measurement unreliable;

• observation period terlalu pendek;

• confounding factor material;

• baseline tidak comparable.

Inconclusive bukan Effective.

⸻

35\. Residual Gap

Setelah improvement:

Original Gap

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement

&nbsp; ↓

Residual Gap

Residual gap harus diklasifikasikan.

Possible:

No Material Gap

Reduced Gap

Persistent Gap

New Gap

Worsened Gap

Unknown

⸻

36\. Regression Detection

Improvement harus memeriksa apakah improvement menghasilkan regression pada dimension lain.

Contoh:

Compliance ↑

&nbsp; BUT

Operational Reliability ↓

atau:

Control Coverage ↑

&nbsp; BUT

Execution Burden ↑ materially

Optimization tidak boleh mengoptimalkan satu metric dengan merusak material performance dimension lain.

⸻

37\. Improvement Closure

Improvement dapat ditutup hanya jika:

0\. implementation selesai;

0\. evidence tersedia;

0\. required measurement dilakukan;

0\. effectiveness assessed;

0\. residual material issue ditangani;

0\. applicable owner/authority menerima closure;

0\. historical record preserved.

Implemented

&nbsp; ≠

Closed

Reference E8 secara eksplisit melarang penggunaan Closed untuk menyembunyikan unresolved material condition.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

38\. Conditional Closure

Conditional closure dapat digunakan jika:

• improvement effective;

• residual monitoring diperlukan;

• sustainability belum sepenuhnya terbukti;

• follow-up review dijadwalkan.

Status harus tetap menunjukkan condition tersebut.

⸻

39\. Improvement Failure

Failure dapat berasal dari:

Wrong Diagnosis

Insufficient Scope

Weak Implementation

Dependency Failure

Measurement Failure

Authority Delay

Capability Constraint

External Constraint

Unexpected Side Effect

Failure tidak boleh direlabel sebagai success hanya karena action telah completed.

⸻

40\. Improvement Learning

Setiap material improvement harus menghasilkan learning yang dapat digunakan untuk:

• future improvement;

• policy evolution;

• control improvement;

• certification readiness;

• governance standards evolution;

• performance model refinement;

• maturity improvement.

Learning harus dibedakan dari anecdotal observation.

⸻

42\. Improvement Reporting

Improvement reporting dapat diintegrasikan ke E7.

Improvement Status

Materiality

Owner

Due State

Blocker

Actual Result

Effectiveness

43\. Improvement Escalation

Escalation diperlukan apabila:

• authority tidak tersedia;

• action blocked;

• dependency unresolved;

• material risk meningkat;

• repeated failure terjadi;

• improvement ineffective;

• cross-platform conflict terjadi.

Flow:

Improvement Issue

&nbsp; ↓

Owner Resolution

&nbsp; ↓

If unresolved

&nbsp; ↓

E6 Governance Escalation

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Authority

E8 tidak menciptakan escalation authority baru.

⸻

44\. Cross-Platform Improvement

PD-03 improvement dapat membutuhkan action dari:

PD-02 Architecture

PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence

PD-05 Runtime & Execution

PD-06 AI Engineering

PD-07 Infrastructure & Platform

PD-08 Security

PD-09 Quality & Evaluation

PD-10 Developer Experience

Namun:

PD-03 Improvement Requirement

&nbsp; ≠

PD-03 Ownership of Other Platform

Platform lain tetap memiliki domain ownership masing-masing.

⸻

45\. Architecture Change Boundary

Jika improvement menghasilkan kebutuhan untuk mengubah architecture:

Performance Gap

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Signal

&nbsp; ↓

Architecture Implication

&nbsp; ↓

Architecture Review

&nbsp; ↓

Architecture Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Change

PD-03 tidak dapat mengubah Architecture Baseline secara unilateral.

⸻

46\. Governance Change Boundary

Jika improvement membutuhkan governance change:

Improvement Signal

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Implication

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Approved Governance Change

E8 tidak menggantikan C1–C10 Governance Architecture.

⸻

47\. Policy Change Boundary

Jika improvement membutuhkan policy modification:

Policy Gap

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Signal

&nbsp; ↓

Policy Review

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Policy Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Policy Change

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measurement

No silent policy mutation.

⸻

48\. Certification Improvement Boundary

Certification improvement harus mempertahankan:

Certification Governance

&nbsp; ≠

Implementation Ownership

Jika certification gap berasal dari domain lain:

Certification Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Corrective Action

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Certification Reassessment

⸻

49\. Anti-Gaming Controls

E8 harus mencegah:

• closing action before verification;

• redefining success after failure;

• changing baseline after implementation;

• selective measurement;

• hiding residual gap;

• relabeling ineffective action;

• removing difficult cases from scope;

• changing KPI solely to declare improvement;

• suppressing regression;

• claiming improvement from activity count alone.

Canonical:

Activity

&nbsp; ≠

Outcome

Outcome

&nbsp; ≠

Effectiveness

Effectiveness requires evidence.

⸻

50\. Improvement Integrity

Material improvement record harus mempertahankan:

Original Finding

Original Baseline

Authorized Action

Implementation Record

Post-Implementation Measurement

Effectiveness Assessment

Residual Gap

Closure Decision

Historical state tidak boleh dihapus untuk membuat improvement history terlihat lebih baik.

⸻

51\. Improvement Cadence

Improvement cycle dapat bersifat:

• continuous;

• periodic;

• event-driven;

• review-triggered;

• certification-triggered;

• governance-triggered.

Exact PD-03 cadence belum ditemukan.

Therefore:

PD-03 Improvement Cadence

\= SOURCE GAP

Claude Code harus mencari source authoritative sebelum menetapkan cadence mandatory.

⸻

52\. Improvement Materiality

Materiality menentukan intensity of response.

Possible classification:

Low

Moderate

High

Critical

Exact thresholds tidak ditetapkan oleh source PD-03 yang tersedia.

Karena itu thresholds harus dicari dan tidak boleh diinvent.

⸻

53\. Improvement Success Criteria

E8 PD-03 dianggap berhasil apabila:

0\. material performance gaps dapat diidentifikasi;

0\. ordinary variance dapat dibedakan dari material gap;

0\. improvement signal memiliki evidence;

0\. cause tidak dinyatakan sebagai fact tanpa evidence;

0\. corrective action dibedakan dari improvement initiative;

0\. improvement memiliki objective;

0\. material improvement memiliki accountable owner;

0\. authority dapat diidentifikasi;

0\. dependencies dapat ditelusuri;

0\. action completion dibedakan dari effectiveness;

0\. re-measurement dilakukan ketika diperlukan;

0\. effectiveness dapat dinilai;

0\. residual gap dapat diidentifikasi;

0\. ineffective improvement memicu re-diagnosis;

0\. regression dapat dideteksi;

0\. optimization tidak merusak material performance dimension lain;

0\. improvement history dapat diaudit;

0\. closure tidak menyembunyikan unresolved material condition;

0\. improvement reporting dapat diintegrasikan ke E7;

0\. improvement governance tetap mengikuti E6;

0\. architecture changes tetap mengikuti Architecture authority;

0\. governance changes tetap mengikuti Governance authority;

0\. policy changes tetap mengikuti applicable authority;

0\. cross-platform improvement tidak memindahkan ownership;

0\. learning dapat dikembalikan ke performance cycle berikutnya.

63\. Canonical Freeze Statement

E8 — Performance Improvement establishes the improvement architecture through which validated Performance gaps and findings within PD-03 Governance & Compliance are converted into authorized improvement responses, corrective actions, and optimization initiatives, while preserving governance authority, domain ownership, evidence integrity, and cross-platform boundaries.

Improvement effectiveness is not inferred from action completion. Material improvement must be verified through re-measurement and effectiveness assessment.

Governance Policy, Governance Control, Certification, and Governance Standards improvement remain within the Governance & Compliance boundary; execution ownership remains with the applicable domain owner.

Architecture, strategy, governance, security, quality, and operational changes remain subject to their respective authority domains.

PD-03-specific improvement catalogues, thresholds, cadence, authority matrices, repositories, and local implementation rules remain SOURCE GAP until authoritative sources are reconciled.

E8 — Performance Improvement is therefore FROZEN as a source-bounded, domain-adapted Performance Improvement Architecture for Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

⸻
