⸻

**1\. Purpose**

E8 menetapkan bagaimana hasil performance PD-03 digunakan untuk:

- mengidentifikasi performance gap;
- menentukan improvement response;
- mengelola corrective action;
- mengelola improvement initiative;
- melakukan re-measurement;
- menilai effectiveness;
- mengidentifikasi residual gap;
- menghasilkan learning;
- melakukan optimization;
- mengembalikan hasil ke performance cycle berikutnya.

E8 menjawab:

"Bagaimana performance Governance & Compliance digunakan untuk melakukan improvement secara evidence-based dan terkontrol?"

E8 bukan sekadar action tracker.

⸻

**2\. Constitutional Position**

Canonical Part E:

E1 — Performance Constitution

&nbsp; ↓

E2 — Performance Model

&nbsp; ↓

E3 — Performance Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

E4 — KPI & Success Metrics

&nbsp; ↓

E5 — Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

E6 — Performance Governance

&nbsp; ↓

E7 — Performance Reporting

&nbsp; ↓

E8 — Performance Improvement

&nbsp; ↓

E9 — Performance Maturity

&nbsp; ↓

E10 — Performance Success Constitution

E8 menerima output dari E5 dan E7, menggunakan governance boundary E6, lalu menghasilkan feedback menuju measurement dan performance cycle berikutnya.

Reference architecture menetapkan E8 sebagai layer yang menjawab "How is performance used to improve?" dan mengembalikan hasil ke re-measurement/next performance cycle.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

**3\. Core Improvement Principle**

Performance improvement is an evidence-driven process that converts validated performance gaps and findings into authorized responses, verifies their effectiveness through re-measurement, and feeds the resulting learning into the next performance cycle.

Karena itu:

Performance Gap

&nbsp; ≠

Improvement Action

Action Completed

&nbsp; ≠

Improvement Effective

Improvement Effective

&nbsp; =

Evidence of improved condition

-

Re-measurement

-

Effectiveness Assessment

Pemisahan antara action completion dan improvement effectiveness merupakan invariant canonical E8.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

**4\. PD-03 Improvement Boundary**

E8 PD-03 berlaku terhadap performance dalam domain:

Governance & Compliance

dengan primary responsibility yang mencakup:

Governance Policy

Governance Control

Certification

Governance Standards

PD-03 tidak memperoleh ownership atas execution Platform lain hanya karena improvement membutuhkan tindakan pada Platform tersebut.

Canonical boundary:

PD-03

Governance & Compliance

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Requirement / Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Execution

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Improvement Verification

PD-03 source secara eksplisit mempertahankan prinsip Governance Control ≠ Execution dan Certification ≠ Implementation.

⸻

**5\. Improvement Architecture**

E3 — Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

E4 — KPI / Success Metrics

&nbsp; ↓

E5 — Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Gap / Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Decision

&nbsp; ↓

E8 — Performance Improvement

&nbsp; ↓

┌────────────────────────────┐

│ │

▼ ▼

Corrective Action Improvement Initiative

│ │

└─────────────┬──────────────┘

&nbsp; ↓

&nbsp; Authorization

&nbsp; ↓

&nbsp; Implementation

&nbsp; ↓

&nbsp; Re-measurement

&nbsp; ↓

&nbsp; Effectiveness Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

&nbsp; Learning / Optimization

&nbsp; ↓

&nbsp; Next Performance Cycle

⸻

**6\. Improvement vs Review**

E5 bertanggung jawab terhadap formal performance review.

E5 dapat menghasilkan:

- finding;
- gap;
- improvement signal;
- corrective action requirement;
- escalation requirement.

E8 mengelola lifecycle response terhadap signal tersebut.

E5

Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Signal

&nbsp; ↓

E8

Improvement Lifecycle

E8 tidak melakukan hidden re-review.

⸻

**7\. Improvement vs Reporting**

E7 mengkomunikasikan:

- improvement status;
- action status;
- material gap;
- effectiveness;
- blocked action;
- recurring issue;
- improvement outcome.

E8 menghasilkan information yang kemudian dapat digunakan oleh E7.

E8 Improvement

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement State

&nbsp; ↓

E7 Reporting

Reporting tidak mengubah improvement state.

⸻

**8\. Improvement vs Governance**

E6 menentukan:

- authority;
- accountability;
- ownership;
- escalation;
- decision rights.

E8 menggunakan governance architecture tersebut.

E8 tidak menciptakan:

Improvement Authority

yang berada di luar governance architecture.

Canonical:

Improvement Need

&nbsp; ↓

Authority Check

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Response

&nbsp; ↓

Accountable Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Execution

&nbsp; ↓

Verification

Reference E6 secara eksplisit mensyaratkan improvement action memiliki accountable owner dan effectiveness dapat diverifikasi.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

**9\. Improvement vs Execution**

Performance Management tidak mengambil alih execution.

Performance Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Execution

&nbsp; ↓

New Result

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Verification

Jika improvement membutuhkan perubahan pada:

- architecture;
- strategy;
- governance;
- security;
- quality;
- operational execution;

perubahan tersebut tetap mengikuti authority domain masing-masing.

Reference E8 menetapkan bahwa architecture, strategy, dan governance changes tetap berada pada authority domain masing-masing.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

**10\. Performance Gap Identification**

Performance gap adalah perbedaan material antara:

Expected Condition

&nbsp; vs

Actual Condition

Gap assessment harus mempertimbangkan:

- expected condition;
- actual result;
- evidence quality;
- context;
- trend;
- materiality;
- confidence.

Expected

-

Actual

-

Evidence

-

Context

-

Trend

&nbsp; ↓

Gap Assessment

Tidak setiap variance otomatis menjadi improvement requirement.

⸻

**11\. Gap Classification**

Gap dapat diklasifikasikan sebagai:

No Material Gap

Ordinary Variance

Material Gap

Recurring Gap

Critical Gap

Measurement Gap

Evidence Gap

Governance Gap

Capability Gap

Execution Gap

External Constraint

Unknown Cause

Classification harus berbasis evidence.

⸻

**12\. Cause Attribution**

E8 tidak boleh melakukan premature attribution.

Cause harus dibedakan menjadi:

Verified Cause

Potential Cause

Unknown

Jika evidence belum cukup:

Cause Under Investigation

harus digunakan.

Jangan mengubah correlation menjadi causation.

⸻

**13\. Improvement Signal**

Improvement Signal adalah output yang menunjukkan bahwa performance condition membutuhkan response.

Minimum:

Improvement Signal ID

Source Finding

Observed Condition

Expected Condition

Gap

Materiality

Evidence

Confidence

Potential Cause

Recommended Response

Required Authority

Owner Requirement

Improvement signal tidak sama dengan authorized action.

⸻

**14\. Improvement Decision**

Possible outcomes:

Maintain

Monitor

Investigate

Re-measure

Improve

Correct

Escalate

Decision harus mengikuti authority yang berlaku.

E8 tidak boleh menganggap:

Recommendation = Authorization

⸻

**15\. Improvement Initiative**

Improvement Initiative digunakan ketika response membutuhkan perubahan yang lebih luas daripada corrective action sederhana.

Minimum:

Initiative ID

Objective

Problem Statement

Baseline

Expected Result

Success Criterion

Owner

Authority

Dependencies

Actions

Timeline

Risk

Measurement Method

Review Point

Exact PD-03 improvement portfolio belum tersedia dalam source.

Karena itu catalogue implementation-specific tetap:

SOURCE GAP

⸻

**16\. Corrective Action**

Corrective Action digunakan untuk merespons kondisi yang telah ditemukan dan membutuhkan correction.

Model:

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Cause / Contributing Factor

&nbsp; ↓

Corrective Action

&nbsp; ↓

Implementation

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Effectiveness

Corrective Action tidak boleh dianggap efektif hanya karena statusnya Completed.

⸻

**17\. Improvement vs Corrective Action**

| **Attribute**   | **Corrective Action**   | **Improvement Initiative**        |
| --------------- | ----------------------- | --------------------------------- |
| Primary purpose | Mengoreksi kondisi      | Meningkatkan kondisi              |
| Trigger         | Finding / nonconformity | Gap / opportunity                 |
| Scope           | Relatif bounded         | Dapat lebih luas                  |
| Success         | Condition corrected     | Expected improvement demonstrated |
| Verification    | Required                | Required                          |
| Re-measurement  | Required when material  | Required                          |
| Optimization    | Not necessarily         | Possible                          |

Keduanya dapat coexist.

⸻

**18\. Optimization**

Optimization berbeda dari correction.

Correction

&nbsp; =

Mengembalikan condition ke required state

Improvement

&nbsp; =

Meningkatkan performance

Optimization

&nbsp; =

Mencari performance yang lebih baik

tanpa merusak required conditions

Optimization tidak boleh:

- mengorbankan compliance;
- mengurangi governance integrity;
- melemahkan control;
- menghapus evidence requirement;
- mengubah authority secara informal.

⸻

**19\. Governance Policy Improvement**

PD-03 dapat mengidentifikasi improvement terhadap Governance Policy.

Canonical:

Policy Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Policy Performance

&nbsp; ↓

Gap

&nbsp; ↓

Policy Improvement Signal

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Policy Review

&nbsp; ↓

Approved Change

&nbsp; ↓

Implementation

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measurement

E8 tidak dapat mengubah policy secara unilateral.

⸻

**20\. Governance Control Improvement**

Control improvement harus membedakan:

Control Design

&nbsp; vs

Control Execution

&nbsp; vs

Control Effectiveness

PD-03 dapat memiliki governance responsibility terhadap control.

Applicable domain owner tetap menjalankan implementation apabila control tersebut berada dalam domainnya.

Governance Control Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Domain

&nbsp; ↓

Control Implementation

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Effectiveness Assessment

⸻

**21\. Certification Improvement**

Certification improvement dapat mencakup:

- evidence completeness;
- finding remediation;
- readiness improvement;
- certification process improvement;
- recurring certification gap reduction.

Namun:

Certification Improvement

&nbsp; ≠

Subject Platform Ownership

Certification improvement tidak memindahkan execution ownership.

⸻

**22\. Governance Standards Improvement**

Governance Standards improvement dapat mencakup:

- standard clarity;
- applicability;
- adoption;
- adherence;
- effectiveness;
- exception reduction;
- standard lifecycle.

Technical standards tetap berada pada applicable technical/domain authority.

⸻

**23\. Improvement Prioritization**

Prioritization harus mempertimbangkan:

Materiality

Impact

Risk

Urgency

Recurrence

Strategic Relevance

Governance Significance

Dependency

Effort

Expected Benefit

Priority tidak boleh ditentukan hanya berdasarkan:

- visibility;
- executive preference;
- ease of completion;
- number of complaints.

Exact PD-03 prioritization threshold:

SOURCE GAP

⸻

**24\. Improvement Ownership**

Setiap material improvement harus memiliki:

Improvement Owner

Action Owner

Evidence Owner

Verification Owner

Decision Authority

Role tersebut dapat berbeda.

Satu role tidak boleh otomatis diasumsikan memegang seluruh responsibility.

⸻

**25\. Accountability Boundary**

Performance Management memiliki accountability terhadap performance-management capability.

Domain owner memiliki accountability terhadap domain execution.

Improvement action owner memiliki accountability terhadap action.

Effectiveness reviewer memiliki accountability terhadap assessment.

Canonical:

Performance Management

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Framework

Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Execution

Action Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Action Completion

Reviewer

&nbsp; ↓

Effectiveness Assessment

⸻

**26\. Improvement Authorization**

Sebelum material action dijalankan:

Improvement Need

&nbsp; ↓

Scope Check

&nbsp; ↓

Authority Check

&nbsp; ↓

Authorization

&nbsp; ↓

Action

Jika action berada di luar authority:

Escalate

bukan:

Proceed Anyway

⸻

**27\. Improvement Dependencies**

Material improvement harus mencatat dependencies.

Contoh:

Architecture Dependency

Security Dependency

Quality Dependency

Operational Dependency

Infrastructure Dependency

Governance Dependency

External Dependency

Dependency tidak berarti ownership.

PD-03 tetap mempertahankan boundary Governance & Compliance. Source PD-03 secara eksplisit menyatakan bahwa Architecture, Security, dan Quality adalah dependencies, bukan subordinate units.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

**28\. Improvement Implementation**

Implementation state harus dapat dibedakan dari effectiveness.

Recommended states:

Not Started

Planned

Authorized

In Progress

Implemented

Pending Re-measurement

Under Effectiveness Review

Effective

Partially Effective

Ineffective

Closed

Exception states:

Blocked

Deferred

Cancelled

Escalated

Implemented tidak sama dengan Effective.

⸻

**29\. Re-measurement**

Re-measurement merupakan control utama E8.

Canonical:

Baseline

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement

&nbsp; ↓

Post-Improvement Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Comparison

&nbsp; ↓

Effectiveness Assessment

Jika measurement method berubah, perubahan harus didokumentasikan dan effectiveness conclusion harus mempertimbangkannya.

⸻

**30\. Effectiveness Assessment**

Effectiveness menjawab:

"Apakah improvement menghasilkan perubahan yang diharapkan?"

Minimum assessment:

Expected Result

Actual Result

Baseline

Post-Improvement Result

Variance

Evidence

Confidence

Residual Gap

Side Effect

Conclusion

Possible conclusion:

Effective

Partially Effective

Ineffective

Inconclusive

⸻

**31\. Effective Improvement**

Improvement dapat dinyatakan Effective apabila:

1. implementation selesai;
2. evidence tersedia;
3. post-improvement measurement tersedia;
4. expected result dapat dibandingkan;
5. improvement effect dapat dibuktikan;
6. material residual gap telah dinilai;
7. material negative side effect tidak diabaikan;
8. effectiveness assessment telah dilakukan oleh role yang sesuai.

⸻

**32\. Partially Effective**

Partially Effective digunakan ketika:

- target hanya tercapai sebagian;
- improvement bekerja pada sebagian scope;
- residual gap masih material;
- benefit belum stabil;
- dependency masih unresolved.

Partial success tidak boleh dipresentasikan sebagai full success.

⸻

**33\. Ineffective Improvement**

Jika improvement ineffective:

Ineffective

&nbsp; ↓

Re-diagnosis

&nbsp; ↓

Cause Reassessment

&nbsp; ↓

New Improvement Signal

&nbsp; ↓

New / Modified Initiative

Ineffectiveness adalah evidence.

Jangan menutup action hanya untuk menghapus negative result.

⸻

**34\. Inconclusive Improvement**

Inconclusive digunakan jika:

- evidence insufficient;
- measurement unreliable;
- observation period terlalu pendek;
- confounding factor material;
- baseline tidak comparable.

Inconclusive bukan Effective.

⸻

**35\. Residual Gap**

Setelah improvement:

Original Gap

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement

&nbsp; ↓

Residual Gap

Residual gap harus diklasifikasikan.

Possible:

No Material Gap

Reduced Gap

Persistent Gap

New Gap

Worsened Gap

Unknown

⸻

**36\. Regression Detection**

Improvement harus memeriksa apakah improvement menghasilkan regression pada dimension lain.

Contoh:

Compliance ↑

&nbsp; BUT

Operational Reliability ↓

atau:

Control Coverage ↑

&nbsp; BUT

Execution Burden ↑ materially

Optimization tidak boleh mengoptimalkan satu metric dengan merusak material performance dimension lain.

⸻

**37\. Improvement Closure**

Improvement dapat ditutup hanya jika:

1. implementation selesai;
2. evidence tersedia;
3. required measurement dilakukan;
4. effectiveness assessed;
5. residual material issue ditangani;
6. applicable owner/authority menerima closure;
7. historical record preserved.

Implemented

&nbsp; ≠

Closed

Reference E8 secara eksplisit melarang penggunaan Closed untuk menyembunyikan unresolved material condition.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

**38\. Conditional Closure**

Conditional closure dapat digunakan jika:

- improvement effective;
- residual monitoring diperlukan;
- sustainability belum sepenuhnya terbukti;
- follow-up review dijadwalkan.

Status harus tetap menunjukkan condition tersebut.

⸻

**39\. Improvement Failure**

Failure dapat berasal dari:

Wrong Diagnosis

Insufficient Scope

Weak Implementation

Dependency Failure

Measurement Failure

Authority Delay

Capability Constraint

External Constraint

Unexpected Side Effect

Failure tidak boleh direlabel sebagai success hanya karena action telah completed.

⸻

**40\. Improvement Learning**

Setiap material improvement harus menghasilkan learning yang dapat digunakan untuk:

- future improvement;
- policy evolution;
- control improvement;
- certification readiness;
- governance standards evolution;
- performance model refinement;
- maturity improvement.

Learning harus dibedakan dari anecdotal observation.

⸻

**41\. Improvement Register**

PD-03 implementation dapat menggunakan Improvement Register.

Minimum fields:

Improvement ID

Source Finding

Domain

Improvement Type

Objective

Baseline

Expected Result

Owner

Authority

Priority

Dependencies

Status

Action

Measurement

Effectiveness

Residual Gap

Decision

Evidence

Closure

Version

Exact PD-03 repository/schema belum ditemukan.

Maka struktur di atas adalah:

BOUNDED REFERENCE

bukan mandatory PD-03 repository schema.

⸻

**42\. Improvement Reporting**

Improvement reporting dapat diintegrasikan ke E7.

Minimum information:

Improvement Status

Materiality

Owner

Due State

Blocker

Expected Result

Actual Result

Effectiveness

Residual Gap

Escalation

E7 mengkomunikasikan state.

E8 menentukan/menjaga improvement lifecycle.

⸻

**43\. Improvement Escalation**

Escalation diperlukan apabila:

- authority tidak tersedia;
- action blocked;
- dependency unresolved;
- material risk meningkat;
- repeated failure terjadi;
- improvement ineffective;
- cross-platform conflict terjadi.

Flow:

Improvement Issue

&nbsp; ↓

Owner Resolution

&nbsp; ↓

If unresolved

&nbsp; ↓

E6 Governance Escalation

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Authority

E8 tidak menciptakan escalation authority baru.

⸻

**44\. Cross-Platform Improvement**

PD-03 improvement dapat membutuhkan action dari:

PD-02 Architecture

PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence

PD-05 Runtime & Execution

PD-06 AI Engineering

PD-07 Infrastructure & Platform

PD-08 Security

PD-09 Quality & Evaluation

PD-10 Developer Experience

Namun:

PD-03 Improvement Requirement

&nbsp; ≠

PD-03 Ownership of Other Platform

Platform lain tetap memiliki domain ownership masing-masing.

⸻

**45\. Architecture Change Boundary**

Jika improvement menghasilkan kebutuhan untuk mengubah architecture:

Performance Gap

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Signal

&nbsp; ↓

Architecture Implication

&nbsp; ↓

Architecture Review

&nbsp; ↓

Architecture Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Change

PD-03 tidak dapat mengubah Architecture Baseline secara unilateral.

⸻

**46\. Governance Change Boundary**

Jika improvement membutuhkan governance change:

Improvement Signal

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Implication

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Approved Governance Change

E8 tidak menggantikan C1–C10 Governance Architecture.

⸻

**47\. Policy Change Boundary**

Jika improvement membutuhkan policy modification:

Policy Gap

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Signal

&nbsp; ↓

Policy Review

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Policy Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Policy Change

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measurement

No silent policy mutation.

⸻

**48\. Certification Improvement Boundary**

Certification improvement harus mempertahankan:

Certification Governance

&nbsp; ≠

Implementation Ownership

Jika certification gap berasal dari domain lain:

Certification Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Corrective Action

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Certification Reassessment

⸻

**49\. Anti-Gaming Controls**

E8 harus mencegah:

- closing action before verification;
- redefining success after failure;
- changing baseline after implementation;
- selective measurement;
- hiding residual gap;
- relabeling ineffective action;
- removing difficult cases from scope;
- changing KPI solely to declare improvement;
- suppressing regression;
- claiming improvement from activity count alone.

Canonical:

Activity

&nbsp; ≠

Outcome

Outcome

&nbsp; ≠

Effectiveness

Effectiveness requires evidence.

⸻

**50\. Improvement Integrity**

Material improvement record harus mempertahankan:

Original Finding

Original Baseline

Authorized Action

Implementation Record

Post-Implementation Measurement

Effectiveness Assessment

Residual Gap

Closure Decision

Historical state tidak boleh dihapus untuk membuat improvement history terlihat lebih baik.

⸻

**51\. Improvement Cadence**

Improvement cycle dapat bersifat:

- continuous;
- periodic;
- event-driven;
- review-triggered;
- certification-triggered;
- governance-triggered.

Exact PD-03 cadence belum ditemukan.

Therefore:

PD-03 Improvement Cadence

\= SOURCE GAP

Claude Code harus mencari source authoritative sebelum menetapkan cadence mandatory.

⸻

**52\. Improvement Materiality**

Materiality menentukan intensity of response.

Possible classification:

Low

Moderate

High

Critical

Exact thresholds tidak ditetapkan oleh source PD-03 yang tersedia.

Karena itu thresholds harus dicari dan tidak boleh diinvent.

⸻

**53\. Improvement Success Criteria**

E8 PD-03 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1. material performance gaps dapat diidentifikasi;
2. ordinary variance dapat dibedakan dari material gap;
3. improvement signal memiliki evidence;
4. cause tidak dinyatakan sebagai fact tanpa evidence;
5. corrective action dibedakan dari improvement initiative;
6. improvement memiliki objective;
7. material improvement memiliki accountable owner;
8. authority dapat diidentifikasi;
9. dependencies dapat ditelusuri;
10. action completion dibedakan dari effectiveness;
11. re-measurement dilakukan ketika diperlukan;
12. effectiveness dapat dinilai;
13. residual gap dapat diidentifikasi;
14. ineffective improvement memicu re-diagnosis;
15. regression dapat dideteksi;
16. optimization tidak merusak material performance dimension lain;
17. improvement history dapat diaudit;
18. closure tidak menyembunyikan unresolved material condition;
19. improvement reporting dapat diintegrasikan ke E7;
20. improvement governance tetap mengikuti E6;
21. architecture changes tetap mengikuti Architecture authority;
22. governance changes tetap mengikuti Governance authority;
23. policy changes tetap mengikuti applicable authority;
24. cross-platform improvement tidak memindahkan ownership;
25. learning dapat dikembalikan ke performance cycle berikutnya.

⸻

**54\. Relationship With E9**

E9 menilai:

"How mature is the performance system?"

E8 mengelola improvement yang dapat meningkatkan:

- performance;
- capability;
- governance effectiveness;
- improvement capability;
- maturity.

Canonical:

E9

Maturity Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Maturity Gap

&nbsp; ↓

E8

Improvement

&nbsp; ↓

Implementation

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measurement

&nbsp; ↓

E9

Maturity Re-assessment

Maturity improvement tetap membutuhkan evidence.

⸻

E10 menentukan apakah Performance Architecture berhasil.

E8 dapat menerima:

sebagai improvement signal.

E8

Improvement

Re-measurement

&nbsp; ↓

E10

**63\. Canonical Freeze Statement**

E8 — Performance Improvement establishes the improvement architecture through which validated Performance gaps and findings within PD-03 Governance & Compliance are converted into authorized improvement responses, corrective actions, and optimization initiatives, while preserving governance authority, domain ownership, evidence integrity, and cross-platform boundaries.

Improvement effectiveness is not inferred from action completion. Material improvement must be verified through re-measurement and effectiveness assessment.

Governance Policy, Governance Control, Certification, and Governance Standards improvement remain within the Governance & Compliance boundary; execution ownership remains with the applicable domain owner.

Architecture, strategy, governance, security, quality, and operational changes remain subject to their respective authority domains.

PD-03-specific improvement catalogues, thresholds, cadence, authority matrices, repositories, and local implementation rules remain SOURCE GAP until authoritative sources are reconciled.

E8 — Performance Improvement is therefore FROZEN as a source-bounded, domain-adapted Performance Improvement Architecture for Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

Parent Sections: E1 — Performance Constitution; E2 — Performance Model; E3 — Performance Measurement; E4 — KPI & Success Metrics; E5 — Performance Review; E6 — Performance Governance; E7 — Performance Reporting; E8 — Performance Improvement  
Successor: E10 — Performance Success Constitution

⸻

1\. Purpose

E9 mendefinisikan bagaimana kematangan Performance Architecture PD-03 Governance & Compliance diukur, dinilai, dibuktikan, dan dikembangkan.

E9 menjawab:

"Seberapa matang sistem PD-03 dalam mengelola, mengukur, mereview, mengatur, melaporkan, dan meningkatkan performance Governance & Compliance?"

Maturity tidak hanya mengukur apakah target performance tercapai.

Maturity mengukur apakah Performance Architecture memiliki kemampuan yang:

• defined;

• repeatable;

• measurable;

• governed;

• managed;

• continuously improved;

• sustainable;

• adaptive.

Maturity assessment harus berbasis evidence dan tidak boleh diturunkan hanya dari performance score.

⸻

2\. Constitutional Position

Canonical Part E relationship:

E1 — Performance Constitution

&nbsp; ↓

E2 — Performance Model

&nbsp; ↓

E3 — Performance Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

E4 — KPI & Success Metrics

&nbsp; ↓

E5 — Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

E6 — Performance Governance

&nbsp; ↓

E7 — Performance Reporting

&nbsp; ↓

E8 — Performance Improvement

&nbsp; ↓

E9 — Performance Maturity

&nbsp; ↓

E10 — Performance Success Constitution

E9 menilai kualitas dan kemampuan keseluruhan Performance Architecture yang dibentuk oleh E1–E8.

E9 tidak menggantikan section sebelumnya.

E9 juga bukan terminal success assessment; terminal acceptance berada pada E10.

⸻

3\. Core Maturity Principle

Performance maturity is the demonstrated capability of PD-03's performance system to consistently measure, evaluate, govern, communicate, improve, and adapt Governance & Compliance performance using reliable evidence.

Dengan demikian:

High Performance

&nbsp; ≠

High Performance Maturity

dan:

High KPI Result

&nbsp; ≠

High Maturity

Sebuah governance condition dapat memiliki hasil KPI yang baik tetapi maturity system yang masih rendah apabila proses measurement, review, governance, improvement, atau learning belum reliable.

Sebaliknya, performance result dapat berada di bawah target sementara maturity system sudah Managed atau Optimized apabila capability untuk mengelola performance telah terbukti.

⸻

4\. Performance vs Performance Maturity

E2 menjawab:

"What dimensions describe performance?"

E9 menjawab:

"How capable and mature is the system that manages those performance dimensions?"

Canonical distinction:

Performance

&nbsp; ↓

"What results are being achieved?"

Performance Maturity

&nbsp; ↓

"How capable is the system of managing those results?"

Maturity tidak boleh disimpulkan hanya dari KPI result.

⸻

5\. PD-03 Maturity Boundary

E9 berlaku terhadap Performance Architecture dalam domain:

Governance & Compliance

Primary PD-03 responsibility includes:

Policy

Control

Certification

Compliance

Governance Standards

Maturity assessment terhadap area tersebut harus tetap menghormati ownership boundary.

PD-03

Governance & Compliance

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├── Governance Policy

&nbsp; ├── Governance Control

&nbsp; ├── Certification

&nbsp; └── Governance Standards

PD-03 tidak memperoleh operational execution ownership hanya karena maturity assessment mengidentifikasi gap pada Platform lain.

⸻

6\. Governance / Execution Boundary

Maturity assessment harus mempertahankan:

Governance

&nbsp; ≠

Execution

dan:

Certification

&nbsp; ≠

Implementation

Jika maturity assessment menemukan gap pada implementation domain:

PD-03 Maturity Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Response

&nbsp; ↓

Execution

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Maturity Re-assessment

Maturity assessment tidak memindahkan ownership kepada PD-03.

⸻

7\. Maturity Architecture

Canonical E9 architecture:

E1 — Performance Constitution

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Philosophy / Boundary

&nbsp; ↓

E2 — Performance Model

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Dimensions

&nbsp; ↓

E3 — Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment / Finding

E9 menilai system capability yang dibentuk oleh E1–E8.

⸻

8\. Maturity Dimensions

Minimum maturity dimensions:

1\. Performance Intent

2\. Measurement

3\. Evaluation & Review

4\. Governance

5\. Reporting & Transparency

6\. Improvement

7\. Learning & Adaptation

Dimensions tersebut adalah maturity dimensions, bukan replacement terhadap Performance Dimensions E2.

Canonical distinction:

E2 Performance Dimensions

&nbsp; ↓

What is being managed?

E9 Maturity Dimensions

&nbsp; ↓

How mature is the system managing it?

⸻

9\. Dimension 1 — Performance Intent

Menilai apakah PD-03 memiliki:

• explicit performance objectives;

• expected conditions;

• success criteria;

• scope;

• traceability;

• relationship antara objective dan measurement.

Questions:

Apakah PD-03 mengetahui apa yang harus dicapai?

Apakah expected governance condition jelas?

Apakah objective dapat ditelusuri ke metric?

Apakah success condition dapat dinilai?

Jika performance intent tidak jelas, maturity ceiling dapat terpengaruh.

⸻

10\. Dimension 2 — Measurement

Menilai apakah PD-03 memiliki measurement capability yang:

• defined;

• valid;

• repeatable;

• traceable;

• owned;

• evidence-backed;

• version-controlled.

Measurement integrity berasal dari E3.

Canonical:

Measurement Specification

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Execution

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Integrity

&nbsp; ↓

Maturity Confidence

Maturity conclusion tidak boleh lebih confident daripada evidence foundation.

⸻

11\. Dimension 3 — Evaluation & Review

Menilai kemampuan PD-03 untuk:

• membandingkan actual vs expected;

• mengidentifikasi gap;

• membaca trend;

• mempertimbangkan context;

• menilai evidence quality;

• melakukan review;

• menghasilkan finding;

• menghasilkan improvement signal.

E5 menyediakan formal review mechanism.

E9 menggunakan output review sebagai evidence maturity.

⸻

12\. Dimension 4 — Governance

Menilai apakah performance system memiliki:

• ownership;

• authority;

• accountability;

• review responsibility;

• escalation;

• decision boundary;

• governance traceability.

E6 tetap menjadi governance authority layer.

E9 hanya menilai maturity governance capability.

E9 tidak menciptakan:

"Maturity Authority"

di atas Governance Authority.

⸻

13\. Dimension 5 — Reporting & Transparency

Menilai kemampuan PD-03 untuk:

• menghasilkan performance visibility;

• menyampaikan evidence;

• menunjukkan confidence;

• menunjukkan trend;

• menunjukkan gap;

• menunjukkan maturity state;

• mengkomunikasikan limitation.

E7 memiliki ownership terhadap reporting mechanism.

E9 menggunakan reporting quality sebagai maturity evidence.

Reporting tidak menentukan maturity level secara unilateral.

⸻

14\. Dimension 6 — Improvement

Menilai apakah PD-03 dapat:

• identify improvement needs;

• prioritize improvement;

• assign ownership;

• execute authorized improvement;

• re-measure;

• assess effectiveness;

• detect residual gap;

• learn from outcome.

E8 merupakan improvement layer.

Canonical:

Maturity Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Maturity Gap

&nbsp; ↓

E8 Improvement

&nbsp; ↓

Implementation

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Maturity Re-assessment

⸻

15\. Dimension 7 — Learning & Adaptation

Menilai apakah PD-03 dapat:

• learn from findings;

• learn from failed improvements;

• identify recurring patterns;

• adapt governance capability;

• improve measurement;

• improve review;

• improve controls;

• improve certification capability;

• adapt appropriately to material context changes.

Adaptation tidak berarti uncontrolled change.

Adaptive

&nbsp; ≠

Uncontrolled Change

Material changes tetap mengikuti applicable authority.

⸻

16\. Five Maturity Levels

E9 menggunakan lima maturity levels:

Initial

&nbsp; ↓

Defined

&nbsp; ↓

Managed

&nbsp; ↓

Optimized

&nbsp; ↓

Adaptive

Level tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai label kosmetik.

Setiap progression membutuhkan demonstrated evidence.

⸻

17\. Level 1 — Initial

Karakteristik:

• capability masih fragmented;

• processes belum consistent;

• measurement masih limited;

• review masih reactive;

• ownership dapat ambiguous;

• improvement belum systematic;

• learning belum structured.

Evidence cenderung:

Informal

Fragmented

Reactive

Initial bukan berarti tidak ada aktivitas.

Initial berarti capability belum sufficiently institutionalized.

⸻

18\. Level 2 — Defined

Karakteristik:

• objectives defined;

• processes documented;

• responsibilities lebih jelas;

• measurement definitions tersedia;

• review mechanism mulai standardized;

• governance boundaries lebih explicit;

• reporting structure mulai established.

Evidence:

Documented

Defined

Repeatable in principle

Defined tidak otomatis berarti Managed.

⸻

19\. Level 3 — Managed

Karakteristik:

• processes dijalankan secara repeatable;

• ownership established;

• measurement operational;

• review berlangsung secara consistent;

• governance mechanism aktif;

• reporting reliable;

• improvement dapat ditelusuri;

• evidence menunjukkan operational consistency.

Evidence:

Operational

Repeatable

Governed

Measured

Managed memerlukan demonstrated operation, bukan sekadar documentation.

⸻

20\. Level 4 — Optimized

Karakteristik:

• performance system actively improved;

• improvement effectiveness measured;

• recurring gaps analyzed;

• process efficiency ditingkatkan;

• evidence digunakan untuk optimization;

• lessons learned digunakan;

• bottleneck ditangani;

• maturity improvement menjadi systematic.

Evidence:

Effectiveness

Optimization

Learning

Improvement

Optimized tidak berarti Adaptive.

⸻

21\. Level 5 — Adaptive

Karakteristik:

• system mampu detect material context changes;

• learning menjadi institutional capability;

• performance model dapat berevolusi secara controlled;

• governance mechanism mampu merespons perubahan;

• measurement system dapat beradaptasi;

• improvement cycle responsive;

• adaptation tetap traceable dan governed.

Evidence:

Learning

Adaptation

Resilience

Controlled Evolution

Adaptive tidak berarti bebas mengubah baseline.

⸻

22\. Maturity Evidence Progression

Initial

&nbsp; ↓

Informal / Fragmented Evidence

Defined

&nbsp; ↓

Documented Evidence

Managed

&nbsp; ↓

Operational + Repeatable Evidence

Optimized

&nbsp; ↓

Effectiveness + Improvement Evidence

Adaptive

&nbsp; ↓

Learning + Adaptation Evidence

Documentation saja tidak cukup untuk level Managed.

Action completion saja tidak cukup untuk Optimized.

Novelty atau frequent change saja tidak cukup untuk Adaptive.

⸻

23\. Maturity Assessment Principle

Maturity assessment harus menjawab:

Claim

&nbsp; ↓

Criterion

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Conclusion

Setiap maturity conclusion material harus dapat diaudit kembali ke evidence.

⸻

24\. Maturity Assessment vs Self-Rating

Self-rating dapat digunakan sebagai input.

Self-rating tidak otomatis menjadi final maturity conclusion.

Canonical:

Self Assessment

&nbsp; +

Evidence

&nbsp; +

Review

&nbsp; ↓

Maturity Conclusion

Self-rating tanpa evidence tidak cukup.

⸻

25\. Maturity Confidence

Setiap maturity conclusion material harus memiliki confidence assessment apabila evidence memungkinkan.

Possible:

High

Medium

Low

Insufficient

Confidence dipengaruhi oleh:

• evidence completeness;

• evidence quality;

• measurement integrity;

• consistency;

• independence;

• recency;

• traceability.

⸻

26\. Evidence Sufficiency

Jika evidence tidak cukup:

INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE

harus dipertahankan.

Jangan memaksa maturity level hanya karena stakeholder membutuhkan angka.

Canonical:

Insufficient Evidence

&nbsp; ≠

Low Maturity

Keduanya adalah kondisi berbeda.

⸻

27\. Maturity Profile

PD-03 dapat memiliki maturity profile berbeda antar dimension.

Contoh:

Overall maturity tidak boleh menyembunyikan dimension yang masih materially weak.

⸻

28\. Bottleneck Principle

Jika satu maturity-critical dimension materially lebih rendah:

High Dimensions

&nbsp; +

Critical Weak Dimension

&nbsp; ↓

Overall Maturity Ceiling

Strength pada dimension lain tidak otomatis menghapus bottleneck.

Bottleneck harus terlihat dalam maturity conclusion.

⸻

29\. Maturity Ceiling

Maturity ceiling dapat muncul apabila:

• evidence tidak cukup;

• critical capability belum established;

• governance boundary belum stable;

• measurement integrity rendah;

• improvement belum effective;

• learning mechanism belum demonstrated;

• critical dependency unresolved.

Ceiling harus dijelaskan berdasarkan evidence.

⸻

30\. Maturity ≠ Simple Average

Overall maturity tidak boleh ditentukan dengan:

Average(All Dimension Scores)

karena maturity adalah capability architecture, bukan composite performance score sederhana.

Jika aggregation digunakan sebagai analytical aid, hasilnya tidak boleh menghapus:

• bottleneck;

• critical weakness;

• evidence limitation;

• confidence.

⸻

31\. Maturity Regression

Maturity dapat menurun.

Possible causes:

• governance degradation;

• measurement degradation;

• loss of capability;

• process fragmentation;

• repeated control failure;

• loss of evidence;

• organizational disruption;

• uncontrolled change;

• sustainability failure.

Canonical:

Managed

&nbsp; ↓

Regression

&nbsp; ↓

Defined

Regression harus dapat dicatat secara transparan.

⸻

32\. Maturity Sustainability

Maturity level tidak dianggap sustained hanya karena pernah dicapai.

Sustainability membutuhkan:

• continued evidence;

• repeated performance;

• stable governance;

• continued measurement;

• continued improvement;

• maintained capability.

Canonical:

Achieved

&nbsp; ≠

Sustained

⸻

33\. Maturity Improvement

Maturity gap menjadi input E8.

Maturity Gap

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Signal

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Improvement

&nbsp; ↓

Implementation

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Maturity Re-assessment

Maturity improvement harus mengikuti authority model E6.

⸻

34\. Maturity Improvement Ownership

Maturity assessment owner, improvement owner, action owner, dan effectiveness reviewer dapat berbeda.

Minimum traceability:

Maturity Assessment Owner

Improvement Owner

Action Owner

Evidence Owner

Effectiveness Reviewer

Decision Authority

Tidak boleh diasumsikan satu role otomatis memegang semua responsibility.

⸻

35\. Relationship With E3

E3 menyediakan measurement integrity.

Relationship:

E3 Measurement Integrity

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence Reliability

&nbsp; ↓

Maturity Confidence

Jika measurement foundation tidak reliable:

Maturity Conclusion Confidence

&nbsp; ↓

Reduced

atau:

Insufficient Evidence

jika evidence tidak memungkinkan conclusion.

⸻

36\. Relationship With E4

E4 menyediakan:

• objectives;

• metrics;

• KPI;

• targets;

• success criteria.

E9 menggunakan metric environment sebagai evidence input.

Namun:

KPI Achievement

&nbsp; ≠

Maturity Level

Maturity membutuhkan evidence tentang system capability.

⸻

37\. Relationship With E5

E5 menyediakan formal review mechanism.

E9 menyediakan:

• maturity criteria;

• maturity assessment model;

• maturity evidence model;

• maturity level determination;

• maturity gap.

Canonical:

E5

Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Review Findings

&nbsp; ↓

E9

Maturity Assessment

Annual maturity review dapat menggunakan E9 sebagai authoritative assessment model.

⸻

38\. Relationship With E6

E6 memastikan:

• maturity assessment memiliki owner;

• reviewer dapat ditentukan;

• accountability jelas;

• escalation tersedia;

• authority tidak dilampaui.

E9 tidak membuat governance authority baru.

Maturity conclusion tetap merupakan assessment result.

⸻

39\. Relationship With E7

E7 mengkomunikasikan:

• maturity level;

• confidence;

• trend;

• bottleneck;

• maturity gap;

• improvement priority.

E7 tidak menetapkan maturity level.

Reporting harus merepresentasikan maturity conclusion secara faithful.

⸻

40\. Relationship With E8

E8 mengelola improvement.

E9 mengukur maturity state.

Canonical:

E9

Maturity Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Maturity Gap

&nbsp; ↓

E8

Improvement

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measurement

&nbsp; ↓

E9

Re-assessment

E8 tidak menentukan maturity level.

E9 tidak menjalankan operational improvement secara unilateral.

⸻

41\. Relationship With E10

E10 menjawab:

"Apakah Performance Architecture PD-03 benar-benar berhasil?"

E9 menjawab:

"Seberapa matang Performance Architecture PD-03?"

Karena itu:

High Maturity

&nbsp; ≠

Success Automatically

E10 tetap melakukan integrated success assessment.

E9 menjadi salah satu input bagi E10 melalui:

Organizational Maturity

atau maturity condition yang berlaku dalam success constitution.

⸻

42\. Governance Maturity

PD-03 governance maturity harus mempertimbangkan apakah:

• governance responsibilities jelas;

• authority traceable;

• accountability clear;

• decision rights bounded;

• escalation operational;

• governance review repeatable;

• compliance evidence reliable;

• governance improvement effective.

Maturity tidak berarti semakin banyak governance.

Maturity berarti governance capability semakin reliable dan appropriate.

⸻

43\. Policy Maturity

Policy maturity dapat mempertimbangkan:

• policy clarity;

• policy ownership;

• lifecycle;

• applicability;

• traceability;

• review;

• change control;

• adoption;

• effectiveness;

• learning.

Policy maturity tidak boleh diukur hanya dari jumlah policy documents.

More Policies

&nbsp; ≠

Higher Maturity

⸻

44\. Control Maturity

Control maturity harus membedakan:

Control Design

&nbsp; ↓

Control Implementation

&nbsp; ↓

Control Operation

&nbsp; ↓

Control Effectiveness

&nbsp; ↓

Control Improvement

PD-03 dapat menilai governance/control maturity.

Implementation ownership tetap berada pada applicable domain apabila control implementation berada di domain tersebut.

⸻

45\. Certification Maturity

Certification maturity dapat mempertimbangkan:

• certification governance;

• readiness;

• evidence completeness;

• finding management;

• corrective action;

• closure verification;

• recurrence;

• sustainability.

Certification maturity tidak berarti PD-03 mengambil alih implementation domain yang menjadi subject certification.

⸻

46\. Governance Standards Maturity

Governance Standards maturity dapat mempertimbangkan:

• standard clarity;

• applicability;

• adoption;

• traceability;

• review;

• compliance;

• exception handling;

• effectiveness;

• lifecycle.

Governance Standards maturity tidak otomatis mencakup seluruh technical standards AIOS.

⸻

47\. Cross-Platform Maturity Boundary

PD-03 dapat melakukan maturity assessment terhadap governance relationship yang melibatkan Platform lain.

Namun:

Cross-Platform Assessment

&nbsp; ≠

Cross-Platform Ownership

Relationship:

PD-03 Maturity Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Platform

&nbsp; ↓

Execution / Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Assessment

Applicable Platform tetap memiliki ownership atas execution domain-nya.

⸻

48\. Maturity Escalation

Escalation diperlukan apabila:

• evidence conflict;

• maturity claim contested;

• critical gap unresolved;

• maturity regression material;

• governance authority conflict;

• cross-platform boundary conflict;

• assessment independence compromised.

Flow:

Maturity Issue

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Escalation

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Authority

E9 tidak menciptakan escalation authority baru.

⸻

49\. Anti-Gaming Controls

E9 harus mencegah:

• maturity inflation;

• self-rating tanpa evidence;

• KPI inflation;

• documentation inflation;

• selective evidence;

• hiding weak dimensions;

• changing criteria after assessment;

• deleting historical regression;

• declaring Adaptive karena frequent change;

• declaring Optimized karena banyak improvement;

• declaring Managed karena process telah didokumentasikan.

Canonical distinctions:

Documentation

&nbsp; ≠

Implementation

Implementation

&nbsp; ≠

Effectiveness

Effectiveness

&nbsp; ≠

Optimization

Optimization

&nbsp; ≠

Adaptability

⸻

50\. Maturity Evidence Integrity

Historical maturity evidence harus dipertahankan.

Minimum traceability:

Assessment Period

Maturity Claim

Criterion

Evidence

Evidence Source

Reviewer

Confidence

Conclusion

Gap

Decision

Evidence tidak boleh diubah untuk menghilangkan historical regression.

⸻

51\. Maturity Reporting

Maturity report minimum harus dapat menunjukkan:

Current Level

Previous Level

Target / Required Level

Confidence

Dimension Profile

Bottleneck

Maturity Gap

Improvement Priority

Regression

Sustainability

Evidence Limitation

Exact PD-03 report schema belum ditemukan dalam source.

Maka:

PD-03 Local Maturity Report Schema

\= SOURCE GAP

Claude Code harus mencari source authoritative sebelum menetapkan schema mandatory.

⸻

52\. Maturity Cadence

Maturity assessment dapat bersifat:

• periodic;

• annual;

• event-driven;

• post-material-change;

• governance-triggered;

• certification-triggered.

Reference architecture mendukung annual maturity review melalui E5.

Namun exact mandatory PD-03 cadence belum ditemukan.

Karena itu:

PD-03 Mandatory Maturity Cadence

\= SOURCE GAP

Tidak boleh diinvent sebelum source reconciliation.

⸻

53\. Maturity Thresholds

Required maturity dapat berbeda berdasarkan:

• domain criticality;

• governance significance;

• risk;

• certification requirement;

• organizational context;

• dependency.

Namun exact PD-03 maturity thresholds belum ditemukan.

Karena itu:

PD-03 Local Maturity Thresholds

\= SOURCE GAP

Claude Code harus mencari authority sebelum menetapkan mandatory threshold.

⸻

54\. Required Maturity

Tidak semua Governance & Compliance capability harus otomatis mencapai:

Adaptive

Required maturity harus ditentukan berdasarkan:

Context

Risk

Criticality

Authority

Dependency

Strategic Requirement

Canonical:

Adaptive

&nbsp; ≠

Mandatory for Everything

⸻

55\. Maturity Ceiling Rule

Jika required evidence untuk level tertentu tidak tersedia:

No Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

No Valid Progression

Contoh:

Defined Evidence

&nbsp; ≠

Managed Conclusion

tanpa evidence operational repeatability.

Demikian pula:

Optimized Label

&nbsp; ≠

Adaptive

tanpa evidence learning dan controlled adaptation.

⸻

56\. Maturity Assessment Workflow

Canonical workflow:

1\. Define Assessment Scope

&nbsp; ↓

2\. Confirm Criteria

&nbsp; ↓

3\. Collect Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

4\. Validate Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

5\. Assess Each Dimension

&nbsp; ↓

6\. Determine Bottleneck

&nbsp; ↓

7\. Determine Maturity Level

&nbsp; ↓

8\. Determine Confidence

&nbsp; ↓

9\. Identify Maturity Gap

&nbsp; ↓

10\. Report

&nbsp; ↓

11\. Trigger Improvement if Required

⸻

57\. Assessment Independence

Jika maturity assessment membutuhkan independence:

• reviewer harus memiliki sufficient separation;

• conflict of interest harus dinilai;

• evidence owner tidak otomatis menjadi final assessor;

• material dispute harus memiliki escalation path.

Exact PD-03 assessor assignment belum ditemukan.

PD-03 Maturity Assessor Matrix

\= SOURCE GAP

⸻

58\. Maturity Dispute

Jika maturity conclusion diperselisihkan:

Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Challenge

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence Reconciliation

&nbsp; ↓

Review

&nbsp; ↓

Decision

Tidak boleh menyelesaikan dispute hanya melalui authority assertion tanpa evidence review.

⸻

59\. Maturity Reassessment

Reassessment diperlukan setelah:

• material governance change;

• material performance architecture change;

• major improvement;

• material regression;

• material organizational change;

• material certification event;

• significant evidence correction.

Reassessment tidak boleh menghapus previous assessment.

⸻

60\. Maturity Regression Record

Jika level turun:

Previous Level

&nbsp; ↓

Observed Degradation

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Regression Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Current Level

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Signal

Regression harus dapat dilacak.

⸻

61\. Maturity Sustainability

Sustainability assessment harus memeriksa apakah capability:

• tetap berjalan;

• tetap owned;

• tetap measured;

• tetap governed;

• tetap effective;

• tetap improved.

Achieved

&nbsp; ↓

Sustained?

Jika tidak:

Maturity Regression Risk

⸻

62\. Maturity Learning Loop

Canonical loop:

Maturity Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Maturity Gap

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement

&nbsp; ↓

Result

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Re-assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Learning

&nbsp; ↓

Updated Capability

Learning harus dapat mempengaruhi:

• future assessment;

• improvement priorities;

• measurement;

• governance;

• reporting;

• policy;

• control;

• certification;

• standards.

⸻

63\. Maturity and Governance Baseline

Maturity improvement tidak boleh dilakukan dengan bypass terhadap Governance Baseline.

Jika maturity gap menunjukkan kebutuhan perubahan:

Maturity Gap

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Implication

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Governance Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Change

&nbsp; ↓

Implementation

&nbsp; ↓

Re-assessment

No silent baseline mutation.

⸻

64\. Maturity and Architecture

Jika maturity gap memiliki architectural implication:

Maturity Gap

&nbsp; ↓

Architecture Implication

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Architecture Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Architecture Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Implementation

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measurement

PD-03 tidak menggunakan maturity authority untuk mengambil alih Architecture Authority.

⸻

65\. Maturity and Security

Jika maturity gap memiliki security implication:

Maturity Gap

&nbsp; ↓

Security Implication

&nbsp; ↓

Security Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Response

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Re-assessment

PD-03 tidak mengambil operational security ownership.

⸻

66\. Maturity and Quality

Jika maturity gap memiliki quality implication:

Maturity Gap

&nbsp; ↓

Quality Implication

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Quality Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Response

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Re-assessment

PD-03 tidak mengambil quality execution ownership.

⸻

67\. Maturity and Certification

Certification findings dapat menjadi maturity evidence.

Namun:

Certification Finding

&nbsp; ≠

Automatic Maturity Level

Finding harus dianalisis dalam konteks capability, recurrence, evidence, governance, dan sustainability.

⸻

68\. Maturity and Compliance

Compliance condition dapat menjadi maturity evidence.

Namun:

Compliance

&nbsp; ≠

Maturity Automatically

100% compliance terhadap selected requirements tidak otomatis membuktikan maturity system jika measurement, governance, review, improvement, atau learning capability belum demonstrated.

⸻

69\. Executive Maturity View

Executive maturity view harus dapat menjawab:

Where are we?

&nbsp; ↓

Where should we be?

&nbsp; ↓

Why?

&nbsp; ↓

What prevents progression?

&nbsp; ↓

What decision / investment is required?

&nbsp; ↓

Who owns the response?

Maturity reporting harus mendukung decision-making.

Maturity reporting tidak menciptakan decision authority.

⸻

70\. Maturity Profile View

PD-03 maturity view dapat menggunakan:

PD-03

&nbsp; ↓

Maturity Profile

&nbsp; ├── Intent

&nbsp; ├── Measurement

&nbsp; ├── Review

&nbsp; ├── Governance

&nbsp; ├── Reporting

&nbsp; ├── Improvement

&nbsp; └── Learning

&nbsp; ↓

&nbsp; Maturity Level

&nbsp; ↓

&nbsp; Maturity Gap

Visualisasi seperti heatmap hanya merupakan presentation mechanism.

Heatmap bukan maturity authority.

⸻

71\. Maturity Trend

Maturity harus dapat dilihat secara temporal.

Contoh:

Assessment 1

Defined

Assessment 2

Defined

Assessment 3

Managed

Assessment 4

Managed

Assessment 5

Optimized

Trend dapat berupa:

• improving;

• stable;

• declining;

• volatile;

• insufficient evidence.

Trend tidak boleh dipaksa selalu naik.

⸻

72\. Maturity Plateau

Plateau dapat terjadi:

Defined

&nbsp; ↓

Managed

&nbsp; ↓

Managed

&nbsp; ↓

Managed

Plateau bukan otomatis failure.

Possible explanation:

• required maturity telah tercapai;

• higher maturity tidak justified;

• dependency belum tersedia;

• authority limitation;

• investment belum tersedia;

• context tidak membutuhkan higher maturity.

Plateau harus dijelaskan, bukan disembunyikan.

⸻

73\. Maturity Decision Boundary

E9 dapat menghasilkan:

• maturity assessment;

• maturity level;

• maturity gap;

• improvement priority;

• recommendation.

E9 tidak secara unilateral:

• mengubah Governance Baseline;

• mengubah policy;

• mengubah architecture;

• mengubah strategy;

• mengubah operational ownership;

• menetapkan organizational sanction;

• memindahkan Platform ownership.

Decision tetap mengikuti E6 dan applicable authority.

⸻

74\. Maturity Success Conditions

E9 dianggap berhasil apabila:

0\. maturity dapat dibedakan dari performance result;

0\. maturity dapat dibedakan dari KPI score;

0\. lima maturity levels memiliki definisi yang distinguishable;

0\. setiap level memiliki evidence expectations;

0\. maturity assessment memiliki confidence;

0\. insufficient evidence dapat menghentikan maturity overclaim;

0\. critical dimension weakness tetap terlihat;

0\. overall maturity tidak menyembunyikan bottleneck;

0\. progression membutuhkan demonstrated evidence;

0\. regression dapat dicatat;

0\. sustainability dapat dinilai;

0\. E8 dapat menerima maturity improvement signal;

0\. E5 dapat menggunakan E9 sebagai maturity assessment model;

0\. E7 dapat melaporkan maturity secara faithful;

0\. E6 tetap menjadi governance authority layer;

0\. maturity assessment tidak menciptakan authority baru;

0\. cross-platform assessment tidak memindahkan ownership;

0\. governance, architecture, security, quality, dan operational domains tetap memiliki authority masing-masing;

0\. assessment dapat diaudit dari claim → criterion → evidence → conclusion;

0\. maturity model tidak memaksakan Adaptive sebagai universal target;

0\. maturity regression dapat menghasilkan improvement signal;

0\. maturity conclusion dapat direassess setelah material change;

0\. maturity learning dapat dikembalikan ke improvement cycle;

0\. PD-03-specific thresholds dan cadence tidak diinvent tanpa source authority.

⸻

75\. Reference Implementation Pattern

E9 dapat diwariskan sebagai reference maturity architecture.

Yang diwariskan:

• five-level maturity architecture;

• maturity definitions;

• maturity evidence principle;

• maturity assessment method;

• maturity dimension model;

• bottleneck principle;

• maturity ceiling;

• confidence model;

• regression model;

• sustainability model;

• maturity improvement loop;

• anti-gaming rules;

• authority boundary.

Yang harus didomainkan:

• required maturity level;

• local evidence;

• local thresholds;

• assessment cadence;

• maturity-critical dimensions;

• domain-specific maturity criteria;

• applicable reviewer;

• local maturity improvement portfolio.

⸻

76\. PD-03 Domain Adaptation

PD-03 adaptation menggunakan:

Governance & Compliance

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├── Policy

&nbsp; ├── Control

&nbsp; ├── Certification

&nbsp; ├── Compliance

&nbsp; └── Governance Standards

Maturity criteria harus mengukur kemampuan PD-03 dalam mengelola domain tersebut.

Namun:

PD-03 Maturity

&nbsp; ≠

Maturity of Every Other Platform

PD-03 dapat memiliki maturity interface terhadap Platform lain tanpa menjadi owner maturity execution mereka.

⸻

82\. Maturity Integrity Principle

A PD-03 performance system is mature when its ability to manage Governance & Compliance performance is demonstrably reliable, governed, repeatable, effective, continuously improving, and appropriately adaptive — not merely when Governance & Compliance performance scores are high.

84\. Canonical Freeze Statement

E9 — Performance Maturity establishes the authoritative maturity architecture for PD-03 Governance & Compliance, defining how the capability and consistency of its Performance Architecture are assessed through evidence across Performance Intent, Measurement, Evaluation & Review, Governance, Reporting & Transparency, Improvement, and Learning & Adaptation.

PD-03 maturity is not determined solely by KPI results, documentation volume, compliance percentage, certification status, action completion, or any single aggregate score. Maturity progression requires demonstrated capability and evidence.

The five maturity levels are Initial, Defined, Managed, Optimized, and Adaptive. Each level represents a progressively stronger demonstrated capability of the performance-management system.

Maturity assessment does not create governance authority, operational authority, architecture authority, or cross-platform ownership. Governance & Compliance remains the PD-03 domain, while execution ownership remains with the applicable domain owner.

Maturity regression, insufficient evidence, bottlenecks, and sustainability limitations must remain visible and must not be suppressed by stronger dimensions elsewhere.

Maturity gaps may generate improvement signals to E8. Material improvements require re-measurement and maturity re-assessment before progression is claimed.

PD-03-specific thresholds, mandatory cadence, assessor matrix, report schema, maturity portfolio, and local scoring rules remain SOURCE GAP until authoritative source reconciliation is completed.

E9 — Performance Maturity is therefore FROZEN as a source-bounded, domain-adapted Performance Maturity Architecture for Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

⸻

Successor: E10 — Performance Success Constitution

⸻

**1\. Purpose**

E9 mendefinisikan bagaimana kematangan Performance Architecture PD-03 Governance & Compliance diukur, dinilai, dibuktikan, dan dikembangkan.

E9 menjawab:

"Seberapa matang sistem PD-03 dalam mengelola, mengukur, mereview, mengatur, melaporkan, dan meningkatkan performance Governance & Compliance?"

Maturity tidak hanya mengukur apakah target performance tercapai.

Maturity mengukur apakah Performance Architecture memiliki kemampuan yang:

- defined;
- repeatable;
- measurable;
- governed;
- managed;
- continuously improved;
- sustainable;
- adaptive.

Maturity assessment harus berbasis evidence dan tidak boleh diturunkan hanya dari performance score.

⸻

**2\. Constitutional Position**

Canonical Part E relationship:

E1 — Performance Constitution

&nbsp; ↓

E2 — Performance Model

&nbsp; ↓

E3 — Performance Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

E4 — KPI & Success Metrics

&nbsp; ↓

E5 — Performance Review

&nbsp; ↓

E6 — Performance Governance

&nbsp; ↓

E7 — Performance Reporting

&nbsp; ↓

E8 — Performance Improvement

&nbsp; ↓

E9 — Performance Maturity

&nbsp; ↓

E10 — Performance Success Constitution

E9 menilai kualitas dan kemampuan keseluruhan Performance Architecture yang dibentuk oleh E1–E8.

E9 tidak menggantikan section sebelumnya.

E9 juga bukan terminal success assessment; terminal acceptance berada pada E10.

⸻

**3\. Core Maturity Principle**

Performance maturity is the demonstrated capability of PD-03's performance system to consistently measure, evaluate, govern, communicate, improve, and adapt Governance & Compliance performance using reliable evidence.

Dengan demikian:

High Performance

&nbsp; ≠

High Performance Maturity

dan:

High KPI Result

&nbsp; ≠

High Maturity

Sebuah governance condition dapat memiliki hasil KPI yang baik tetapi maturity system yang masih rendah apabila proses measurement, review, governance, improvement, atau learning belum reliable.

Sebaliknya, performance result dapat berada di bawah target sementara maturity system sudah Managed atau Optimized apabila capability untuk mengelola performance telah terbukti.

⸻

**4\. Performance vs Performance Maturity**

E2 menjawab:

"What dimensions describe performance?"

E9 menjawab:

"How capable and mature is the system that manages those performance dimensions?"

Canonical distinction:

Performance

&nbsp; ↓

"What results are being achieved?"

Performance Maturity

&nbsp; ↓

"How capable is the system of managing those results?"

Maturity tidak boleh disimpulkan hanya dari KPI result.

⸻

**5\. PD-03 Maturity Boundary**

E9 berlaku terhadap Performance Architecture dalam domain:

Governance & Compliance

Primary PD-03 responsibility includes:

Policy

Control

Certification

Compliance

Governance Standards

Maturity assessment terhadap area tersebut harus tetap menghormati ownership boundary.

PD-03

Governance & Compliance

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├── Governance Policy

&nbsp; ├── Governance Control

&nbsp; ├── Certification

&nbsp; └── Governance Standards

PD-03 tidak memperoleh operational execution ownership hanya karena maturity assessment mengidentifikasi gap pada Platform lain.

⸻

**6\. Governance / Execution Boundary**

Maturity assessment harus mempertahankan:

Governance

&nbsp; ≠

Execution

dan:

Certification

&nbsp; ≠

Implementation

Jika maturity assessment menemukan gap pada implementation domain:

PD-03 Maturity Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Response

&nbsp; ↓

Execution

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Maturity Re-assessment

Maturity assessment tidak memindahkan ownership kepada PD-03.

⸻

**7\. Maturity Architecture**

Canonical E9 architecture:

E1 — Performance Constitution

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Philosophy / Boundary

&nbsp; ↓

E2 — Performance Model

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Dimensions

&nbsp; ↓

E3 — Measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

E5 — Review

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment / Finding

&nbsp; ↓

E6 — Governance

&nbsp; ↓

Ownership / Authority / Accountability

&nbsp; ↓

E7 — Reporting

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Visibility

&nbsp; ↓

E8 — Improvement

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement / Learning / Optimization

&nbsp; ↓

E9 — Maturity

&nbsp; ↓

Maturity Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Maturity Gap

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Maturity Improvement

E9 menilai system capability yang dibentuk oleh E1–E8.

⸻

**8\. Maturity Dimensions**

Minimum maturity dimensions:

1\. Performance Intent

2\. Measurement

3\. Evaluation & Review

4\. Governance

5\. Reporting & Transparency

6\. Improvement

7\. Learning & Adaptation

Dimensions tersebut adalah maturity dimensions, bukan replacement terhadap Performance Dimensions E2.

Canonical distinction:

E2 Performance Dimensions

&nbsp; ↓

What is being managed?

E9 Maturity Dimensions

&nbsp; ↓

How mature is the system managing it?

⸻

**9\. Dimension 1 — Performance Intent**

Menilai apakah PD-03 memiliki:

- explicit performance objectives;
- expected conditions;
- success criteria;
- scope;
- traceability;
- relationship antara objective dan measurement.

Questions:

Apakah PD-03 mengetahui apa yang harus dicapai?

Apakah expected governance condition jelas?

Apakah objective dapat ditelusuri ke metric?

Apakah success condition dapat dinilai?

Jika performance intent tidak jelas, maturity ceiling dapat terpengaruh.

⸻

**10\. Dimension 2 — Measurement**

Menilai apakah PD-03 memiliki measurement capability yang:

- defined;
- valid;
- repeatable;
- traceable;
- owned;
- evidence-backed;
- version-controlled.

Measurement integrity berasal dari E3.

Canonical:

Measurement Specification

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Execution

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Measurement Integrity

&nbsp; ↓

Maturity Confidence

Maturity conclusion tidak boleh lebih confident daripada evidence foundation.

⸻

**11\. Dimension 3 — Evaluation & Review**

Menilai kemampuan PD-03 untuk:

- membandingkan actual vs expected;
- mengidentifikasi gap;
- membaca trend;
- mempertimbangkan context;
- menilai evidence quality;
- melakukan review;
- menghasilkan finding;
- menghasilkan improvement signal.

E5 menyediakan formal review mechanism.

E9 menggunakan output review sebagai evidence maturity.

⸻

**12\. Dimension 4 — Governance**

Menilai apakah performance system memiliki:

- ownership;
- authority;
- accountability;
- review responsibility;
- escalation;
- decision boundary;
- governance traceability.

E6 tetap menjadi governance authority layer.

E9 hanya menilai maturity governance capability.

E9 tidak menciptakan:

"Maturity Authority"

di atas Governance Authority.

⸻

**13\. Dimension 5 — Reporting & Transparency**

Menilai kemampuan PD-03 untuk:

- menghasilkan performance visibility;
- menyampaikan evidence;
- menunjukkan confidence;
- menunjukkan trend;
- menunjukkan gap;
- menunjukkan maturity state;
- mengkomunikasikan limitation.

E7 memiliki ownership terhadap reporting mechanism.

E9 menggunakan reporting quality sebagai maturity evidence.

Reporting tidak menentukan maturity level secara unilateral.

⸻

**14\. Dimension 6 — Improvement**

Menilai apakah PD-03 dapat:

- identify improvement needs;
- prioritize improvement;
- assign ownership;
- execute authorized improvement;
- re-measure;
- assess effectiveness;
- detect residual gap;
- learn from outcome.

E8 merupakan improvement layer.

Canonical:

Maturity Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Maturity Gap

&nbsp; ↓

E8 Improvement

&nbsp; ↓

Implementation

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Maturity Re-assessment

⸻

**15\. Dimension 7 — Learning & Adaptation**

Menilai apakah PD-03 dapat:

- learn from findings;
- learn from failed improvements;
- identify recurring patterns;
- adapt governance capability;
- improve measurement;
- improve review;
- improve controls;
- improve certification capability;
- adapt appropriately to material context changes.

Adaptation tidak berarti uncontrolled change.

Adaptive

&nbsp; ≠

Uncontrolled Change

Material changes tetap mengikuti applicable authority.

⸻

**16\. Five Maturity Levels**

E9 menggunakan lima maturity levels:

Initial

&nbsp; ↓

Defined

&nbsp; ↓

Managed

&nbsp; ↓

Optimized

&nbsp; ↓

Adaptive

Level tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai label kosmetik.

Setiap progression membutuhkan demonstrated evidence.

⸻

**17\. Level 1 — Initial**

Karakteristik:

- capability masih fragmented;
- processes belum consistent;
- measurement masih limited;
- review masih reactive;
- ownership dapat ambiguous;
- improvement belum systematic;
- learning belum structured.

Evidence cenderung:

Informal

Fragmented

Reactive

Initial bukan berarti tidak ada aktivitas.

Initial berarti capability belum sufficiently institutionalized.

⸻

**18\. Level 2 — Defined**

Karakteristik:

- objectives defined;
- processes documented;
- responsibilities lebih jelas;
- measurement definitions tersedia;
- review mechanism mulai standardized;
- governance boundaries lebih explicit;
- reporting structure mulai established.

Evidence:

Documented

Defined

Repeatable in principle

Defined tidak otomatis berarti Managed.

⸻

**19\. Level 3 — Managed**

Karakteristik:

- processes dijalankan secara repeatable;
- ownership established;
- measurement operational;
- review berlangsung secara consistent;
- governance mechanism aktif;
- reporting reliable;
- improvement dapat ditelusuri;
- evidence menunjukkan operational consistency.

Evidence:

Operational

Repeatable

Governed

Measured

Managed memerlukan demonstrated operation, bukan sekadar documentation.

⸻

**20\. Level 4 — Optimized**

Karakteristik:

- performance system actively improved;
- improvement effectiveness measured;
- recurring gaps analyzed;
- process efficiency ditingkatkan;
- evidence digunakan untuk optimization;
- lessons learned digunakan;
- bottleneck ditangani;
- maturity improvement menjadi systematic.

Evidence:

Effectiveness

Optimization

Learning

Improvement

Optimized tidak berarti Adaptive.

⸻

**21\. Level 5 — Adaptive**

Karakteristik:

- system mampu detect material context changes;
- learning menjadi institutional capability;
- performance model dapat berevolusi secara controlled;
- governance mechanism mampu merespons perubahan;
- measurement system dapat beradaptasi;
- improvement cycle responsive;
- adaptation tetap traceable dan governed.

Evidence:

Learning

Adaptation

Resilience

Controlled Evolution

Adaptive tidak berarti bebas mengubah baseline.

⸻

**22\. Maturity Evidence Progression**

Initial

&nbsp; ↓

Informal / Fragmented Evidence

Defined

&nbsp; ↓

Documented Evidence

Managed

&nbsp; ↓

Operational + Repeatable Evidence

Optimized

&nbsp; ↓

Effectiveness + Improvement Evidence

Adaptive

&nbsp; ↓

Learning + Adaptation Evidence

Documentation saja tidak cukup untuk level Managed.

Action completion saja tidak cukup untuk Optimized.

Novelty atau frequent change saja tidak cukup untuk Adaptive.

⸻

**23\. Maturity Assessment Principle**

Maturity assessment harus menjawab:

Claim

&nbsp; ↓

Criterion

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Conclusion

Setiap maturity conclusion material harus dapat diaudit kembali ke evidence.

⸻

**24\. Maturity Assessment vs Self-Rating**

Self-rating dapat digunakan sebagai input.

Self-rating tidak otomatis menjadi final maturity conclusion.

Canonical:

Self Assessment

&nbsp; +

Evidence

&nbsp; +

Review

&nbsp; ↓

Maturity Conclusion

Self-rating tanpa evidence tidak cukup.

⸻

**25\. Maturity Confidence**

Setiap maturity conclusion material harus memiliki confidence assessment apabila evidence memungkinkan.

Possible:

High

Medium

Low

Insufficient

Confidence dipengaruhi oleh:

- evidence completeness;
- evidence quality;
- measurement integrity;
- consistency;
- independence;
- recency;
- traceability.

⸻

**26\. Evidence Sufficiency**

Jika evidence tidak cukup:

INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE

harus dipertahankan.

Jangan memaksa maturity level hanya karena stakeholder membutuhkan angka.

Canonical:

Insufficient Evidence

&nbsp; ≠

Low Maturity

Keduanya adalah kondisi berbeda.

⸻

**27\. Maturity Profile**

PD-03 dapat memiliki maturity profile berbeda antar dimension.

Contoh:

| **Dimension** | **Level** | **Confidence** |
| ------------- | --------- | -------------- |
| Intent        | Managed   | High           |
| Measurement   | Defined   | High           |
| Review        | Managed   | Medium         |
| Governance    | Managed   | High           |
| Reporting     | Optimized | High           |
| Improvement   | Defined   | Medium         |
| Learning      | Initial   | Medium         |

Overall maturity tidak boleh menyembunyikan dimension yang masih materially weak.

⸻

**28\. Bottleneck Principle**

Jika satu maturity-critical dimension materially lebih rendah:

High Dimensions

&nbsp; +

Critical Weak Dimension

&nbsp; ↓

Overall Maturity Ceiling

Strength pada dimension lain tidak otomatis menghapus bottleneck.

Bottleneck harus terlihat dalam maturity conclusion.

⸻

**29\. Maturity Ceiling**

Maturity ceiling dapat muncul apabila:

- evidence tidak cukup;
- critical capability belum established;
- governance boundary belum stable;
- measurement integrity rendah;
- improvement belum effective;
- learning mechanism belum demonstrated;
- critical dependency unresolved.

Ceiling harus dijelaskan berdasarkan evidence.

⸻

**30\. Maturity ≠ Simple Average**

Overall maturity tidak boleh ditentukan dengan:

Average(All Dimension Scores)

karena maturity adalah capability architecture, bukan composite performance score sederhana.

Jika aggregation digunakan sebagai analytical aid, hasilnya tidak boleh menghapus:

- bottleneck;
- critical weakness;
- evidence limitation;
- confidence.

⸻

**31\. Maturity Regression**

Maturity dapat menurun.

Possible causes:

- governance degradation;
- measurement degradation;
- loss of capability;
- process fragmentation;
- repeated control failure;
- loss of evidence;
- organizational disruption;
- uncontrolled change;
- sustainability failure.

Canonical:

Managed

&nbsp; ↓

Regression

&nbsp; ↓

Defined

Regression harus dapat dicatat secara transparan.

⸻

**32\. Maturity Sustainability**

Maturity level tidak dianggap sustained hanya karena pernah dicapai.

Sustainability membutuhkan:

- continued evidence;
- repeated performance;
- stable governance;
- continued measurement;
- continued improvement;
- maintained capability.

Canonical:

Achieved

&nbsp; ≠

Sustained

⸻

**33\. Maturity Improvement**

Maturity gap menjadi input E8.

Maturity Gap

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Signal

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Improvement

&nbsp; ↓

Implementation

&nbsp; ↓

Re-measurement

&nbsp; ↓

Maturity Re-assessment

Maturity improvement harus mengikuti authority model E6.

⸻

**34\. Maturity Improvement Ownership**

Maturity assessment owner, improvement owner, action owner, dan effectiveness reviewer dapat berbeda.

Minimum traceability:

Maturity Assessment Owner

Improvement Owner

Action Owner

Evidence Owner

Effectiveness Reviewer

Decision Authority

Tidak boleh diasumsikan satu role otomatis memegang semua responsibility.

⸻

**35\. Relationship With E3**

E3 menyediakan measurement integrity.

Relationship:

E3 Measurement Integrity

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence Reliability

&nbsp; ↓

Maturity Confidence

Jika measurement foundation tidak reliable:

Maturity Conclusion Confidence

&nbsp; ↓

Reduced

atau:

Insufficient Evidence

jika evidence tidak memungkinkan conclusion.

⸻

**36\. Relationship With E4**

E4 menyediakan:

- objectives;
- metrics;
- KPI;
- targets;
- success criteria.

E9 menggunakan metric environment sebagai evidence input.

Namun:

KPI Achievement

&nbsp; ≠

Maturity Level

Maturity membutuhkan evidence tentang system capability.

⸻

**37\. Relationship With E5**

**42\. Governance Maturity**

PD-03 governance maturity harus mempertimbangkan apakah:

- governance responsibilities jelas;
- authority traceable;
- accountability clear;
- decision rights bounded;
- escalation operational;
- governance review repeatable;
- compliance evidence reliable;
- governance improvement effective.

Maturity tidak berarti semakin banyak governance.

Maturity berarti governance capability semakin reliable dan appropriate.

⸻

Policy maturity dapat mempertimbangkan:

- policy clarity;
- policy ownership;
- lifecycle;
- applicability;
- traceability;
- review;
- change control;
- adoption;
- effectiveness;
- learning.

Policy maturity tidak boleh diukur hanya dari jumlah policy documents.

More Policies

&nbsp; ≠

Higher Maturity

Implementation ownership tetap berada pada applicable domain apabila control implementation berada di domain tersebut.

**48\. Maturity Escalation**

- maturity regression material;

- assessment independence compromised.

Flow:

Maturity Issue

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Escalation

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Authority

**49\. Anti-Gaming Controls**

E9 harus mencegah:

- maturity inflation;
- self-rating tanpa evidence;
- KPI inflation;
- documentation inflation;
- selective evidence;
- hiding weak dimensions;
- changing criteria after assessment;
- deleting historical regression;
- declaring Adaptive karena frequent change;
- declaring Optimized karena banyak improvement;
- declaring Managed karena process telah didokumentasikan.

Canonical distinctions:

Documentation

&nbsp; ≠

Implementation

Implementation

&nbsp; ≠

Effectiveness

Effectiveness

&nbsp; ≠

Optimization

Optimization

&nbsp; ≠

Adaptability

&nbsp; ↓

&nbsp; ↓

Possible explanation:

- required maturity telah tercapai;
- higher maturity tidak justified;
- dependency belum tersedia;
- authority limitation;
- investment belum tersedia;
- context tidak membutuhkan higher maturity.

Plateau harus dijelaskan, bukan disembunyikan.

**73\. Maturity Decision Boundary**

E9 dapat menghasilkan:

- maturity assessment;
- maturity level;
- maturity gap;
- improvement priority;
- recommendation.

E9 tidak secara unilateral:

- mengubah Governance Baseline;
- mengubah policy;
- mengubah architecture;
- mengubah strategy;
- mengubah operational ownership;
- menetapkan organizational sanction;
- memindahkan Platform ownership.

Decision tetap mengikuti E6 dan applicable authority.

⸻

**74\. Maturity Success Conditions**

E9 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1. maturity dapat dibedakan dari performance result;
2. maturity dapat dibedakan dari KPI score;
3. lima maturity levels memiliki definisi yang distinguishable;
4. setiap level memiliki evidence expectations;
5. maturity assessment memiliki confidence;
6. insufficient evidence dapat menghentikan maturity overclaim;
7. critical dimension weakness tetap terlihat;
8. overall maturity tidak menyembunyikan bottleneck;
9. progression membutuhkan demonstrated evidence;
10. regression dapat dicatat;
11. sustainability dapat dinilai;
12. E8 dapat menerima maturity improvement signal;
13. E5 dapat menggunakan E9 sebagai maturity assessment model;
14. E7 dapat melaporkan maturity secara faithful;
15. E6 tetap menjadi governance authority layer;
16. maturity assessment tidak menciptakan authority baru;
17. cross-platform assessment tidak memindahkan ownership;
18. governance, architecture, security, quality, dan operational domains tetap memiliki authority masing-masing;
19. assessment dapat diaudit dari claim → criterion → evidence → conclusion;
20. maturity model tidak memaksakan Adaptive sebagai universal target;
21. maturity regression dapat menghasilkan improvement signal;
22. maturity conclusion dapat direassess setelah material change;
23. maturity learning dapat dikembalikan ke improvement cycle;
24. PD-03-specific thresholds dan cadence tidak diinvent tanpa source authority.

⸻

**82\. Maturity Integrity Principle**

A PD-03 performance system is mature when its ability to manage Governance & Compliance performance is demonstrably reliable, governed, repeatable, effective, continuously improving, and appropriately adaptive — not merely when Governance & Compliance performance scores are high.
